### [1] [UNREAL: Unifying Retrieval and Long-Context with a Single Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08463)
*Edan Kinderman,Elad Hoffer,Yochai Blau,Brian Chmiel,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UNREAL提出用冻结LLM内部表示统一实现语料级检索与长上下文证据选择，仅增加<500K参数，在HotpotQA等任务上大幅提升召回，并降低长上下文推理的FLOPs和首token延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何用单一的模型内部机制统一实现从大规模语料检索到长上下文推理中的证据选择，替代传统的外部检索器-重排序器系统。

Method: UNREAL在冻结的LLM内部表示之上训练轻量模块（<500K参数），用于编码文档块并生成检索查询。查询和块向量在共享表示空间中匹配，完成语料级检索；在长上下文推理时，同一机制在生成前对候选证据进行选择与去噪，丢弃无关内容。整个过程不更新LLM主干，推理时先通过内部表示检索或过滤证据，再交由原LLM生成，从而在语料级和单提示级均实现证据稀疏化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "recall", "value": "49.1% 提升至 73.2%", "setting": "HotpotQA，3B token、21M chunk Wikipedia索引"}, {"metric": "recall", "value": "31.7% 提升至 60.1%", "setting": "2WikiMultiHopQA，同上索引"}, {"metric": "recall", "value": "8.8% 提升至 14.4%", "setting": "MuSiQue，同上索引"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "1.0% 提升至 24.83%", "setting": "NoLiMa，128K最大上下文长度"}, {"metric": "F1", "value": "49.97% 提升至 54.66%", "setting": "LV-Eval，256K上下文"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从冻结LLM的内部表示中直接生成检索查询并编码文档块，无需修改主干模型。；仅增加不到500K可训练参数，实现跨语料检索与长上下文推理的统一证据选择。；在长上下文任务中利用同一选择机制去除干扰项，降低FLOPs和首token延迟。

Abstract: Long-context inference and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) handle evidence selection at vastly different scales, from a single long prompt to an entire corpus. We ask whether a single model-internal mechanism can select evidence across this range. We introduce UNifying REtrieval And Long-Context with a Single Model (UNREAL), a model-native evidence selection framework to span corpus retrieval and long-context inference. UNREAL encodes chunks and derives retrieval queries directly from the frozen LLM's internal representations. It adds fewer than 500K trainable parameters and leaves the backbone unchanged. On a 3B-token, 21M-chunk Wikipedia index, all four dense and hybrid UNREAL backbones outperform state-of-the-art retriever-reranker systems. The best model raises recall from 49.1% to 73.2% on HotpotQA, from 31.7% to 60.1% on 2WikiMultiHopQA, and from 8.8% to 14.4% on MuSiQue. Applied to long-context tasks, the same selection mechanism removes distractors before generation, raising NoLiMa accuracy from 1.0% to 24.83% at its maximum context length of 128K tokens, and LV-Eval's F1 score from 49.97% to 54.66% at 256K. UNREAL also reduces FLOPs and time-to-first-token relative to full-context inference from roughly 32K tokens onward, with larger gains as context grows. Together, these results establish model-internal evidence selection as a common foundation for corpus retrieval and evidence-sparse long-context inference.

</details>


### [2] [Agentic AutoRAG: RAG Pipeline Optimization through Reasoning-Driven Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08452)
*Lasse B. Strand,Robert Jakob,Kevin O'Sullivan,Markus Kreft*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Agentic AutoRAG 用 LLM 智能体优化 RAG 流水线超参数：通过 Diagnoser 将失败归因到检索或生成，由 Proposer 结合模型排名与价格知识库选择下一配置，实现准确率-成本帕累托搜索。在三个多跳 QA 基准上优于所有基线，前 10 次试验即匹配基线 30 次试验的准确率；在真实医疗语料上中位准确率 77% 对最强基线 71.5%，成本约为其 58%，达到 71.5% 时成本约为其 22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 流水线配置涉及分块、嵌入模型、重排与生成等多个相互作用的超参数，现有优化器将每次试验压缩为单一聚合分数，无法解释失败究竟源于检索还是生成，导致搜索效率低、成本高且难以权衡准确率与成本。

Method: Agentic AutoRAG 是一个由 LLM 智能体驱动的多目标 RAG 超参数优化器。输入为语料库以及待优化的 RAG 配置空间，涵盖分块方式、嵌入模型、重排器和生成模型等相互作用的超参数。优化过程采用冻结考卷（frozen exam）式评测：从语料中构造一组问题作为固定的评测集，每次试验提出一个配置并在该考卷上运行完整 RAG 流水线，得到每个问题的正确与否。随后 Diagnoser 对每个失败问题进行失败归因，判断失败发生在检索阶段还是生成阶段；这一归因利用了已检索到的文本块所提供的证据。Proposer 接收诊断结果，并以一个包含模型排名与价格的知识库为基础，选择下一个待评估的配置；其目标不是单一聚合分数，而是在准确率与成本之间进行多目标权衡，从而逐步描绘帕累托前沿。整个循环反复进行“提出配置—运行考卷—诊断失败—再提议”的过程，使得搜索能够针对失败类型调整配置，而不是盲目搜索。在成本感知模式下，Proposer 会显式考虑查询成本，从而在较低成本下达到可接受准确率。论文未披露底层使用的具体模型、训练数据、硬件或延迟吞吐信息。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "LLM-judge 准确率", "value": "高于所有对比基线", "setting": "三个多跳 QA 基准"}, {"metric": "LLM-judge 准确率", "value": "在前 10 次试验内匹配或超过统计基线完整 30 次试验的准确率", "setting": "三个多跳 QA 基准，前 10 次试验对比基线 30 次试验"}, {"metric": "中位考卷准确率", "value": "77%", "setting": "真实医疗语料，成本感知模式"}, {"metric": "中位考卷准确率", "value": "71.5%", "setting": "最强基线，真实医疗语料"}, {"metric": "每查询成本", "value": "约为最强基线的 58%", "setting": "真实医疗语料，成本感知模式，达到 77% 准确率时"}, {"metric": "每查询成本", "value": "约为最强基线的 22%", "setting": "真实医疗语料，成本感知模式，达到基线 71.5% 准确率时"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Agentic AutoRAG：基于 LLM 智能体的多目标 RAG 超参数优化器，带检索-生成失败归因；Diagnoser 模块在每次试验后把每个失败问题归因到检索阶段或生成阶段；Proposer 模块基于模型排名与价格的知识库，选择下一个配置，并在准确率与成本间权衡以刻画帕累托前沿；在冻结考卷式评测上进行评分，并在成本感知模式下针对真实医疗语料进行优化

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a widely used approach for grounding large language models (LLMs) in external knowledge. However, configuring a pipeline is an expensive hyperparameter optimization problem over many interacting choices, from chunking and embedding model to reranking and generation. Existing optimizers, from greedy search to Bayesian optimization, reduce each trial to an aggregate score and search without modeling why a configuration performed as it did, even though the retrieved chunks already provide evidence about whether each failure occurred during retrieval or after it. We introduce Agentic AutoRAG, an LLM-agent optimizer for multi-objective RAG hyperparameter optimization with retrieval-versus-generation failure attribution. It proposes configurations scored on a frozen exam from the corpus: after each trial a Diagnoser attributes each failed question to retrieval or generation, and a Proposer, grounded in a knowledge base of model rankings and pricing, selects the next configuration, weighing accuracy against cost to trace a Pareto frontier. On three multi-hop QA benchmarks it reaches higher LLM-judge accuracy than every baseline we compare, and within its first 10 trials it matches or beats the statistical baselines' full 30-trial judge accuracy. In its cost-aware mode on a real-world healthcare corpus it reaches a median exam accuracy of 77%, above the strongest baseline's 71.5%, at about 58% of that baseline's cost per query, and it matches that 71.5% at about 22% of the cost.

</details>


### [3] [Two Vectors Replace In-Context Demos: Structured Task Adaptation via Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07572)
*Xi Ding,Naichen Shi,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STAVE通过两个任务特定向量替换上下文示例，实现结构化任务适应，在保持零样本推理成本的同时，以更少参数匹配或超越最先进方法，适用于多模态和文本任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文学习（ICL）需要为每个查询重新编码演示示例，导致大量视觉令牌开销；而现有的无演示方法在每个任务或每个解码器层添加紧凑任务状态，导致任务参数随深度增长，且插入的令牌或键无法改变原始提示在层内的注意力分配。

Method: STAVE方法将任务适应表示为两个任务特定向量：readout向量和context向量。输入嵌入包括原始提示的令牌嵌入，STAVE将readout向量添加到负责生成答案的令牌嵌入上，context向量添加到其他结构令牌组（如指令、问题等）的嵌入上。训练时，使用有无演示的提示及答案标签进行优化。理论上通过损失函数的一阶分析和边界约束来指导设计。推理时，模型直接使用添加向量的嵌入，无需演示示例，实现零样本推理成本。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能", "value": "在18个文本任务上超越15-shot ICL和先前的任务向量方法", "setting": "文本任务评估"}, {"metric": "任务参数数量", "value": "远少于最先进方法", "setting": "多模态任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用两个任务特定向量（readout向量和context向量）替换演示示例，分别更新答案生成令牌和其他结构令牌组；通过一阶损失分析和边界约束从理论上证明设计选择；在保持零样本推理成本的同时，以更少任务参数匹配或超越现有方法

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) adapts frozen large multimodal models (LMMs) to new tasks from a few demonstrations (demos), but re-encodes them at every query, where each demo image adds up to hundreds of visual tokens. Demo-free methods remove this cost with a compact task state. However, they add it at locations searched per task or at every decoder layer, where task parameters grow with depth. Moreover, inserted tokens or keys cannot change how the original prompt divides its attention within a layer. To address these issues, we propose Structured Task Adaptation via Embeddings (STAVE), which replaces demos with two task-specific vectors added to existing input embeddings. Specifically, a readout vector updates the answer-producing tokens and a context vector updates the other structural token groups. Both are trained with answer labels on prompts with and without demos. We justify these design choices theoretically using a first-order analysis of the loss and a margin bound. Extensive experiments on six LMMs and five large language models show that STAVE matches or outperforms state-of-the-art methods on multimodal tasks with far fewer task parameters and surpasses 15-shot ICL and prior task vectors on 18 text tasks, all at zero-shot inference cost.

</details>


### [4] [Hybrid Latent Attention for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07940)
*Yuhan Chen,Siyuan Zhang,Nan Wang,Feiyang Kang,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HLA 针对循环语言模型 KV cache 随循环次数线性膨胀的问题，提出在滑动窗口内保留精确 KV、窗口外将每个 token 压缩为 latent 并由各 loop 的 query 直接读取的混合注意力。在 Ouro T=4 模型上微调新增参数后，cache 缩减 10.7x、并发序列提升 4.0-8.8x、解码吞吐提升 2.5x（1K）至 7.4x（16K），并保留 97% 以上数学/知识/推理准确率与 96-100% 长上下文检索准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型对每个 token 重复应用同一层栈 T 次会线性放大 KV cache，限制 GPU 可并发解码的序列数并拖慢每步解码。

Method: HLA 面向循环语言模型：这类模型将同一层栈重复应用 T 次。标准注意力下，每个 loop 都会读取并缓存 key/value，使 KV cache 随 T 线性增长。HLA 对每个 token 采用混合表示：最近的 W 个 token 保留精确的 key 与 value，参与常规注意力；更早的 token 则存储为紧凑 latent，每个 loop 的 query 直接与该 latent 交互，而不显式重建 key/value。这样 cache 中的历史部分从 T 份 K/V 压缩为各 token 一份 latent，每 token cache 缩减 10.7x，同时近期上下文仍保持精确注意力。训练时，在 Ouro 循环模型（T=4，1.4B 与 2.6B）上冻结原始预训练权重，仅训练新增参数，目标为复现原始注意力输出，即通过 uptrain 使混合注意力行为对齐原模型。推理时，每个解码步读取滑动窗口内的精确 K/V 及全部历史 latent，无需重建 K/V，从而降低每步读取的 cache 量，支持更多并发序列并提升解码吞吐。在长上下文检索等任务中，latent 直接供 query 读取的机制保留了历史信息，实验显示 16K token 内检索准确率保持 96-100%，数学、知识、推理基准保留 97% 以上原始准确率，监督微调后竞赛级数学表现与原模型持平。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "每 token cache 缩减", "value": "10.7x", "setting": "Ouro 循环模型 T=4，1.4B 和 2.6B 参数，uptrain 后"}, {"metric": "每 GPU 并发序列数", "value": "4.0-8.8x", "setting": "相同显存预算下"}, {"metric": "解码吞吐", "value": "2.5x", "setting": "1K-token 上下文"}, {"metric": "解码吞吐", "value": "最高 7.4x", "setting": "16K-token 上下文"}, {"metric": "原始准确率保留比例", "value": ">97%", "setting": "数学、知识、推理基准"}, {"metric": "长上下文检索准确率保留比例", "value": "96-100%", "setting": "最长 16K token 检索任务"}, {"metric": "竞赛级数学表现", "value": "与微调后的原始模型持平", "setting": "监督微调之后"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 混合注意力：最近的 W 个 token 保留精确 key/value，更老的 token 压缩为 latent，由每个 loop 的 query 直接读取，无需重建 K/V；在 Ouro 循环模型（T=4）上冻结预训练权重、仅训练新增参数进行 uptrain，以复现原始注意力并实现 10.7x 每 token cache 缩减；在 1K 到 16K 上下文实现 2.5x 到 7.4x 解码吞吐提升，并保持 97% 以上数学/知识/推理准确率与 96-100% 长上下文检索准确率

Abstract: Looped language models apply the same stack of layers T times to each token, which deepens the model without adding parameters but multiplies its key-value (KV) cache by T. The larger cache limits how many sequences a GPU can decode at once and slows each decoding step, which reads the whole cache. We propose Hybrid Latent Attention (HLA), which keeps exact keys and values within a sliding window of W recent tokens and stores each older token as a compact latent that the query of each loop reads directly, without reconstructing keys and values. We uptrain HLA on Ouro looped models (T=4) with 1.4B and 2.6B parameters, keeping the pretrained weights frozen and training only the added parameters to reproduce the original attention. The cache shrinks by 10.7x per token, fitting 4.0-8.8x as many concurrent sequences per GPU, and decoding throughput improves by 2.5x at 1K-token contexts and by up to 7.4x at 16K. HLA retains over 97% of the original accuracy on math, knowledge and reasoning benchmarks, and 96-100% on long-context retrieval up to 16K tokens. After supervised fine-tuning, it performs on par with the fine-tuned original model on competition-level math.

</details>


### [5] [Towards In-Parameter Memory Augmentation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08630)
*Haoyu Huang,Zhongwei Xie,Jiaxin Bai,Yisen Gao,Hong Ting Tsang,Wuganjing Song,Huihao Jing,Yufei Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 系统综述了为LLM注入参数化记忆的方法，按参数放置位置（Embedding/Attention/FFN/混合）和获取时间（在线/离线）分类，讨论了与上下文学习的互补关系及开放挑战，适合需要为推荐系统构建可复用记忆模块的工程师参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在部署或推理阶段通过参数化记忆（参数、适配器或其他参数类对象）为LLM注入预训练后获取的知识，以补充上下文学习，避免上下文容量消耗和重复离散编码成本。

Method: 本文为综述，未提出单一端到端架构。它系统组织已有方法：将记忆信息表示为可复用的参数对象（如参数、适配器），在推理时将其组合到前向传播中，无论该对象是部署前获取还是部署中获取。方法按两个正交轴分类：参数放置（嵌入层、注意力层、前馈网络层或混合）和参数获取时间（在线或离线）。文章澄清概念边界、进行对比，并讨论开放问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出按参数放置位置（Embedding、Attention、FFN或混合）分类的方法体系；提出按参数获取时间（部署中在线获取或部署前离线获取）分类的维度；厘清边界并系统比较不同参数化记忆方法；讨论干扰、安全、与上下文学习协同设计及递归自我改进等开放方向

Abstract: Recently Large Language Models (LLMs) and LLM-based agents increasingly need to incorporate knowledge acquired after pretraining, e.g., domain facts, user preferences, documents, and interaction experience. In-context learning (ICL) and ICL-based agent harness remain flexible, but they consume context capacity and incur repeated discretized encoding cost that grows with context length. \textbf{In-parameter memory} offers a complementary substrate: reusable memory information is represented in model parameters, adapters, or other parameter-like objects that are composed into the forward pass at inference time. This survey focuses on methods that augment LLMs with such parametric memory at deployment: a memory-bearing parameter object is plugged into the forward pass during inference, whether it is acquired before or during deployment. We organize the landscape with two orthogonal axes: \textbf{Parameter Placement}, which includes Embedding, Attention, FFN layers, or Hybrid when two or more layers are used; and \textbf{Parameter Acquisition Time}, which distinguishes methods whose memory object is acquired during deployment (online) from those acquired before it (offline). We clarify boundaries, conduct comparisons, and discuss open directions in interference, safety, co-design with ICL, and recursive self-improvement.

</details>


### [6] [Denoising Hierarchical Representations: Joint Continuous Diffusion for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08738)
*Mathias Ollu,Nikos Komodakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出H-CDLM分层连续扩散语言模型框架，通过并行扩散token与粗粒度聚类模态，以极小开销提升连续扩散语言模型生成质量。应用于CoBit得到H-CoBit，在LM1B和OWT上显著降低GenPPL，并在GSM8K上超过此前连续扩散与流模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何以极小的计算和参数开销改进连续扩散语言模型，使其在保持并行、顺序无关生成优势的同时提升生成质量并在推理任务上表现更好。

Method: H-CDLM在连续扩散语言模型的基础上引入分层多模态联合扩散。输入文本首先被表示为token序列，同时利用预训练token嵌入进行聚类，得到粗粒度簇表示，形成两个语义粒度模态：细粒度token模态与粗粒度簇模态。框架对这两个模态并行扩散，并允许为每个模态配置独立的采样器和噪声调度，使不同粒度之间能够相互引导。训练目标仍为扩散/流匹配的生成目标，但扩展到多模态联合扩散；推理时从噪声出发，通过各模态采样器协同去噪，最终生成token序列。将H-CDLM应用于CoBit得到H-CoBit，应用于FLM得到H-FLM，表明该框架可插入现有连续扩散/流匹配模型，以极小的计算和参数开销获得生成质量提升。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "MAUVE", "value": "improves", "setting": "dataset entropy, LM1B/OWT"}, {"metric": "GenPPL", "value": "49.4", "setting": "LM1B, dataset entropy"}, {"metric": "GenPPL", "value": "50.4", "setting": "OWT, dataset entropy"}, {"metric": "GenPPL improvement over baseline", "value": "24.2 points", "setting": "LM1B"}, {"metric": "GenPPL improvement over baseline", "value": "20.7 points", "setting": "OWT"}, {"metric": "GSM8K accuracy", "value": "27.4%", "setting": "文本生成/推理基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出分层连续扩散语言模型H-CDLM，在多个语义粒度模态上并行扩散：token本身与基于预训练token嵌入聚类得到的粗粒度簇；允许每个模态拥有独立的采样器和调度器，增强模态间交互；将框架应用于CoBit得到H-CoBit，并推广到流匹配模型FLM得到H-FLM，证明跨连续生成范式通用

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) hold the promise of order-agnostic, parallel text generation. Recently, continuous diffusion and flow matching models have seen substantial gains, driven by carefully crafted token representations and diffusion/flow spaces. In this work, we introduce Hierarchical Continuous Diffusion Language Models (H-CDLMs), a simple framework that further improves continuous DLMs with minimal compute and parameter overhead. Drawing on the discrete DLM and continuous image diffusion literature on joint diffusion, we diffuse multiple modalities in parallel. These modalities represent tokens at different semantic granularities: in our instantiation, the tokens themselves and coarser clusters obtained by clustering pretrained token embeddings. We propose a general setup that allows per-modality samplers and schedules to enhance the interplay between modalities. Applied to CoBit, this yields H-CoBit, which delivers large empirical gains across benchmarks. At dataset entropy, H-CoBit improves MAUVE and reaches a generative perplexity (GenPPL) of 49.4 on LM1B and 50.4 on OWT, improving on the baseline by 24.2 and 20.7 points and surpassing even discrete DLMs of comparable size. On GSM8K, it reaches 27.4% accuracy, outperforming prior continuous diffusion and flow-based models. We further apply H-CDLM to the flow matching model FLM, obtaining consistent gains with H-FLM and demonstrating that the framework generalizes across continuous generative paradigms. Our code will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [7] [Holdout Best-of-N: Unbiased Evaluation and Its Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08719)
*Shrey Shah,Yinheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 从理论上分析Best-of-N评估中复用选择分数导致的偏差，提出Holdout无偏估计器，给出J<K的完全无偏充要条件、高斯下的minimax风险阶σ²/√K（有偏可改进至σ²/K）、两候选尖锐常数1/(π√2)以及O(MK log M)的循环平均算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复用用于挑选Best-of-N获胜者的分数会高估期望奖励；需要研究在固定K个独立候选分数矩阵下，如何对使用J个新分数选择策略的期望评判奖励进行无偏且低风险的估计。

Method: 论文并非提出神经网络训练架构，而是提出一个评估估计器架构。输入为一个固定的分数矩阵，其中每个候选有K个独立评判分数，池大小为M；选择策略使用J个新鲜分数选出Best-of-N获胜者。核心数据流为：先按策略用J个新鲜分数选出获胜者，再由Holdout估计器使用与选择分数不相交的保留分数对该获胜者的期望评判奖励进行评估。关键模块包括：（1）选择器：基于J个新鲜分数从N个候选中选出获胜者；（2）Holdout估计器：仅使用固定矩阵中未被选择过程使用的分数计算奖励期望的无偏估计；（3）循环平均/子集与平局处理：通过O(MK log M)的循环平均减少估计方差。训练目标/优化为在候选特定分数律稳定且独立时最小化无偏估计的minimax风险。主要理论结果包括：完全无偏当且仅当J<K；在J=K-1时选择器随K加深，独立高斯同方差下Holdout达到σ²/√K的无偏minimax风险；允许偏差时风险改进为σ²/K；两候选情形给出最小方差无偏估计器与尖锐渐近常数1/(π√2)，Holder无需方差即可达到；固定选择器深度下有界分数循环评估风险为O(K⁻¹)且关于池大小一致。推理/服务流程为：在获得固定分数矩阵后，在线或离线使用Holdout重新评估胜者，无需训练额外模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最优无偏minimax风险阶", "value": "σ²/√K", "setting": "独立高斯分数、共同方差、固定M≥N≥2、J=K-1的Holdout估计器"}, {"metric": "允许有偏时的风险阶", "value": "σ²/K", "setting": "同上设定但允许偏差"}, {"metric": "两候选渐近无偏minimax常数", "value": "1/(π√2)", "setting": "两候选N=2、独立高斯、已知或未知方差，Holdout可达该常数"}, {"metric": "循环平均计算复杂度", "value": "O(MK log M)", "setting": "子集与平局循环平均的计算"}, {"metric": "固定选择器深度下循环评估风险阶", "value": "O(K⁻¹)", "setting": "有界分数，风险关于池大小一致"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证明在任意池大小M≥N≥2和稳定独立候选特定分数律下，仅基于固定分数矩阵的单估计器对所有策略完全无偏当且仅当J<K，并给出不可能性结果；提出Holdout估计器：在J=K-1时随K增大选择器加深，并在独立高斯同方差设定下达到阶为σ²/√K的无偏minimax风险；在允许有偏时，将风险率改进为σ²/K，并刻画偏差-代价的权衡；针对两候选情形，推导已知方差下的最小方差无偏估计器与尖锐渐近无偏minimax常数1/(π√2)，且Holdout无需已知方差即可达到该常数；给出子集与平局循环平均的O(MK log M)计算方法；在固定选择器深度下，对有界分数的循环评估获得关于池大小一致收敛的O(K⁻¹)风险；指出不可能性仅针对固定矩阵：额外一个新鲜获胜者分数即可对all-K策略进行无偏评估

Abstract: Reusing the scores that select a Best-of-$N$ winner can overstate its expected reward. We study evaluation from a fixed matrix of $K$ independent scores per candidate for a policy that selects using $J$ fresh scores. A single estimator based only on this matrix is exactly unbiased for expected judge reward under every independent, stable collection of candidate-specific score laws if and only if $J<K$, for every pool size $M\ge N\ge2$. At $J=K-1$, the selector deepens as $K$ grows. For independent Gaussian scores with common variance and fixed $M\ge N\ge2$, the unbiased minimax risk in this regime is of order $\sigma^2/\sqrt K$, attained by Holdout; allowing bias improves the rate to $\sigma^2/K$. For two candidates, we derive the minimum-variance unbiased estimator at known variance and the sharp asymptotic unbiased minimax constant $1/(\pi\sqrt2)$, which Holdout attains without knowing the variance. The cyclic average over subsets and ties can be computed in $O(MK\log M)$ operations. At fixed selector depth, cyclic evaluation of bounded scores has $O(K^{-1})$ risk uniformly in pool size. The impossibility result concerns the fixed matrix: one additional fresh winner score permits unbiased evaluation of the all-$K$ policy.

</details>

*Bingxi Hou,Guochao Jiang,Guofeng Quan,Weiqing Li,Wenfeng Feng,Guohua Liu,Yuewei Zhang*

TL;DR: 论文研究跨分词器在线策略蒸馏，发现严格1:1对齐已覆盖大部分学生token，仅在严格位置用top-16共享词表反向KL即可匹敌全词表OPD，而扩大对齐覆盖（如对不匹配跨度加MSE）反而因梯度冲突降低准确率，主张从最大化对齐覆盖转向优先监督可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在教师与学生使用不同分词器时，在线策略蒸馏（OPD）需要在序列和词表两级对齐，但扩大对齐覆盖范围是否总能提升学习效果尚不明确。

Method: 论文探讨跨分词器在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的监督信号设计。输入为学生模型自身生成的响应序列，教师模型对相同序列进行前向计算，得到教师的token级概率分布。由于学生和教师使用不同分词器，需要在序列级别和词表级别分别进行对齐：序列对齐确定哪些学生token与教师token严格对应，词表对齐确定共享词表中的词汇。在严格1:1对齐位置，论文提出将反向KL损失限制在学生概率最高的共享词表top-16子集上，形成紧凑监督；同时计算全共享词表的OPD作为对比。对于不匹配组，论文尝试添加对跨度（span）对数概率的均方误差（MSE）监督，以覆盖所有学生token，实现完全监督覆盖。训练目标为最小化上述蒸馏损失。推理时学生模型独立生成。论文还通过梯度诊断分析了严格损失和跨度损失的梯度方向一致性，发现跨度梯度与严格梯度方向一致性弱或负相关，且幅度较大，可能解释加入跨度监督后准确率下降的现象。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "与全共享词表OPD相当，优于评估的跨分词器基线", "setting": "在数学推理和代码生成任务上，三种异构教师-学生对"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "降低（具体数值未披露）", "setting": "在不匹配组添加跨度对数概率MSE监督后"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统分析跨分词器OPD中对齐覆盖与监督可靠性的关系，发现严格1:1对齐位置已覆盖大部分学生生成token。；提出将反向KL限制在学生选择的共享词表top-16子集上，在严格对齐位置实现紧凑监督，精度与全共享词表OPD相当，并优于评估的跨分词器基线。；对不匹配组引入跨度对数概率的均方误差监督以实现完全覆盖，但实验表明反而降低准确率。；通过梯度诊断解释跨度监督导致性能下降的原因：跨度梯度与严格梯度方向一致性弱或负相关，且幅度增大。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own generations using teacher feedback. With different tokenizers, comparing teacher and student predictions requires alignment at both sequence and vocabulary levels. In this paper, we examine whether expanding this alignment coverage improves learning. Across three heterogeneous teacher--student pairs on mathematical reasoning and code generation, strict 1:1 groups already cover most student-generated tokens despite substantial vocabulary mismatch. On responses sampled from the students before distillation, the shared vocabulary retains nearly all teacher and student probability mass at strictly aligned positions on average. Restricting reverse KL to a student-selected top-16 subset of the shared vocabulary at each strict position achieves accuracy comparable to full shared-vocabulary OPD, outperforming the evaluated cross-tokenizer baselines. Adding mean squared error supervision on span log-probabilities in mismatch groups gives complete supervision coverage, yet reduces accuracy. At checkpoints from training with only the strict loss, the span gradients show weak or negative directional agreement with the strict gradients and grow in magnitude relative to them. These diagnostics may help explain the accuracy drop from adding span supervision. Our findings motivate a shift from maximizing alignment coverage to prioritizing supervision reliability: compact supervision at strict positions can be more effective than broader coverage that introduces weakly aligned or conflicting training signals.

</details>

Abstract: Long-term conversational memory is becoming an integral component of modern LLM systems. Proposed architectures group records by topics and events, construct hierarchies and graphs, and connect facts through causal and temporal relations. We experimentally study the interaction between two memory parameters: structural depth and the width of context supplied to the answer model. Using EverMemBench, we evaluate depths D1-D4, core budgets of 1,024/2,048/4,096 tokens, and additional Production and Oracle conditions up to the full archive. Increasing width from 1K to 4K improves Accuracy by 10.11-17.98 percentage points, whereas increasing depth provides no monotonic gain. Beyond 8-16K, Production performance reaches a plateau while tokens per correct answer continue to increase; Oracle preserves quality on full archives of 68-71K tokens. These results motivate further investigation of large, coherent context blocks instead of progressively deeper memory structures.

</details>

*Jinhyeok Kim,Hye-Young Jung*

TL;DR: 研究 LLM 在无有效选项的多选题中是否可靠弃权，发现高 MCQA 准确率不能保证弃权可靠性，揭示标准准确率未覆盖的模型可靠性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 MCQA 准确率无法保证模型在无有效选项时可靠弃权，且即使有明确指令和惩罚仍会出现无效强制选择。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是在评测层面改造 MMLU-Pro 数学子集：移除标注的正确选项，使模型可在剩余选项中选择或输出 ABSTAIN，并对无效强制选择施加惩罚。评估流程包括标准无有效选项设置与 correct-conditioned 设置（仅对模型原本答对的实例评估弃权），通过惩罚性评分对比不同指令与评分机制下的弃权可靠性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 penalty-framed no-valid-option MCQA 设定，移除正确选项并允许 ABSTAIN；引入 correct-conditioned 分析，仅在模型原本答对的实例上评估弃权行为；使用惩罚性评分衡量无效强制选择的下游代价

Abstract: Multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) is commonly used to evaluate large language models under the assumption that one of the provided options is correct, typically using answer-selection accuracy. However, in real deployments, users or retrieval systems may provide invalid option sets in which none of the listed choices is correct, and selecting one of them may incur downstream cost. We study this setting as penalty-framed no-valid-option MCQA. Using the mathematics subset of MMLU-Pro, we remove the labeled correct option, allow models to either choose a remaining option or output ABSTAIN, and penalize invalid forced-choice responses. We further introduce correct-conditioned analysis, evaluating abstention only on instances that the model originally answered correctly. Experiments show that high MCQA accuracy does not fully guarantee abstention reliability: even under explicit no-valid-option-aware instructions and penalty-based scoring, models still produce invalid forced-choice responses for a subset of originally correct instances. These results show that penalty-framed no-valid-option MCQA reveals an aspect of model reliability not captured by standard answer-selection accuracy.

</details>


### [11] [Dynamic Positional Attention Modulation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07848)
*Dayan Pan,Jingyuan Wang,Xie Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DyPAM提出一种参数高效微调方法，通过输入条件、维度级以及头/层级的动态调制来调整位置信息对注意力的贡献，以适配RoPE位置结构，在数学和常识推理任务上优于现有PEFT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法通常采用统一且静态的适配，未考虑注意力在维度、头、层和输入token上的结构化异质性，尤其是RoPE引入的维度相关位置结构，导致均匀适配次优。

Method: DyPAM在不修改预训练骨干的前提下，作用于注意力机制中的query和key表示。其核心是对query和key进行输入条件化、维度级的调制，同时引入头级和层级的结构调制，从而动态调整位置信息（由RoPE编码）对注意力权重的贡献。该方法旨在适配注意力表示在维度、头、层以及输入token上的非均匀行为，使参数高效微调更贴合RoPE诱导的维度相关位置结构。训练时仅优化新增的调制参数，保持预训练权重冻结。论文在数学和常识推理基准上对多种骨干模型进行了实验，表明DyPAM持续优于现有强PEFT基线。但摘要未披露具体调制公式、模块细节、训练目标、数据规模或推理开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出DyPAM，一种直接作用于query和key表示、动态调制位置信息对注意力贡献的PEFT方法。；结合输入条件、维度级调制与头级、层级的结构调制，实现与RoPE位置结构对齐的细粒度位置注意力适配，且不修改预训练骨干。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has become a standard approach for adapting large language models to downstream tasks. However, most existing PEFT methods rely on uniform and static adaptations, without accounting for the structured heterogeneity of attention across dimensions, heads, layers, and input tokens. In practice, attention representations exhibit non-uniform behavior, and positional encoding mechanisms such as rotary positional embeddings (RoPE) induce dimension-dependent positional structure, making uniform adaptation suboptimal. In this work, we propose DyPAM (Dynamic Positional Attention Modulation), a PEFT method that adapts how positional information contributes to attention by operating directly on the query and key representations. DyPAM combines input-conditioned, dimension-wise modulation with head-wise and layer-wise structural modulation, performing fine-grained adaptation of positional attention aligned with the RoPE-induced structure without modifying the pretrained backbone. Extensive experiments on mathematical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks across multiple backbone models demonstrate that DyPAM consistently outperforms existing strong PEFT baselines.

</details>


### [12] [Nucleus Speculative Decoding: Plausibility-Aware Verification Beyond Exact Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07822)
*Shuhao Li,Fanghua Ye,Wanyu Lin,Tianyu Yuan,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NSD 通过把目标模型的 nucleus 合理性纳入推测解码验证，放宽标准接受规则，提升接受长度，在多模型多草稿机制下实现最高 5.16× 于自回归、3.15× 于标准推测解码的吞吐加速，并给出分布偏差与序列保真度理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准推测解码的接受规则只做精确分布校正，当草稿模型对目标模型下仍然高度合理的 token 赋予过高概率时会拒绝这些 token，导致每次验证前向保留的草稿 token 较少，限制了解码加速。

Method: NSD 建立在推测解码框架之上：轻量草稿模型自回归地提出多个候选 token，目标模型对这些候选并行执行一次前向验证。标准接受规则基于草稿与目标分布的概率比决定是否接受 token，并在拒绝时进行精确分布校正。NSD 放松该规则：对每个草稿 token，若满足标准接受规则，或该 token 落在目标模型当前步的 nucleus（按目标分布累积概率截断的高概率集合）内，则接受该 token；否则按推测解码流程拒绝并重新采样。该设计提高了每次目标验证前向之后保留的草稿 token 数（即接受长度），使更多输出 token 分摊一次目标前向的开销。论文对 NSD 的分布偏差做了理论分析：单步误差由目标 nucleus 内草稿模型的超额概率精确决定，并进一步给出序列级保真度边界，刻画局部偏差在自回归生成中的累积。实验中在多个目标模型和多种草稿/proposal 机制上评估吞吐量与任务性能。代码公开。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对自回归解码的吞吐量加速", "value": "最高 5.16×", "setting": "多个目标模型与多种 proposal 机制上的实验"}, {"metric": "相对标准推测解码的吞吐量加速", "value": "最高 3.15×", "setting": "多个目标模型与多种 proposal 机制上的实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Nucleus Speculative Decoding (NSD)：若草稿 token 满足标准接受规则，或属于目标模型的 nucleus，则接受该 token。；理论刻画 NSD 引入的分布偏差，证明单步误差恰好由目标 nucleus 内草稿模型的超额概率决定。；推导序列级保真度边界，量化局部偏差在自回归解码中的累积。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by using a lightweight draft model to propose multiple tokens that are verified by a target model in parallel. However, the standard acceptance rule focuses on exact distribution correction and rejects tokens that remain highly plausible under the target model when the draft model assigns excess probability. This conservative verification limits the number of draft tokens retained after each verification forward pass. We introduce Nucleus Speculative Decoding (NSD), a relaxed verification method that incorporates target-model plausibility into speculative decoding. NSD accepts a draft token if it satisfies the standard acceptance rule or belongs to the target model's nucleus. We theoretically characterize the distributional deviation introduced by our method and show that the single-step error is exactly determined by the draft model's excess probability within the target nucleus. We further derive sequence-level fidelity bounds that quantify how local deviations accumulate over autoregressive decoding. Experiments across multiple target models and proposal mechanisms demonstrate that NSD consistently improves speculative decoding efficiency while maintaining competitive task performance. Our method achieves throughput speedups of up to $5.16\times$ over autoregressive decoding and up to $3.15\times$ over standard speculative decoding. These improvements coincide with longer accepted lengths, allowing more output tokens to share the cost of each target verification pass. Analysis shows that plausibility-aware verification provides an effective approach for relaxed verification and speculative decoding efficiency. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [13] [APEX: Speculate smarter, not deeper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07780)
*Manvi Jha,Zach Zhang,Zhichao Xu,Linbo Liu,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Vinayak Arannil*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: APEX is a learned controller for speculative decoding that performs request-level expert selection among EAGLE-3, n-gram, and draft-model speculation, plus block-level draft-depth adaptation using causal decoding signals and verifier feedback. Integrated into vLLM with Qwen3-8B, it reports up to 5.24x speedup over autoregressive decoding and a 41.0% relative reduction in wasted tokens compared with fixed n-gram speculation at k=16.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Speculative decoding effectiveness depends on the proposal mechanism and draft depth, but fixed configurations cannot adapt to changes in predictability, repetition, and acceptance during generation, causing wasted computation without proportional speedup.

Method: APEX is a learned controller integrated into vLLM that adapts speculative decoding at two levels: request-level expert selection (APEX-Router) and block-level draft-depth adaptation (APEX-Depth). Inputs are the target LLM (Qwen3-8B) and causal decoding signals from the ongoing generation, including recent verifier feedback. APEX-Router selects among three speculation mechanisms per request: EAGLE-3, n-gram, and draft-model speculation. APEX-Depth then adjusts the draft length at each verification block using causal decoding signals and recent verifier acceptance feedback. The controller models accepted draft length as censored survival feedback and learns position-wise rejection hazards, block execution costs, and an action utility that balances throughput, accepted progress, and wasted tokens. Training optimizes this utility while using the target model's standard verification procedure unchanged, so inference follows speculative decoding: a proposal mechanism drafts tokens, the target model verifies them, and APEX dynamically chooses proposal type and draft depth based on observed generation dynamics.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "speedup over autoregressive decoding", "value": "up to 5.24x", "setting": "Qwen3-8B across six workloads, integrated into vLLM"}, {"metric": "aggregate speedup", "value": "4.27x", "setting": "APEX-S, Qwen3-8B across six workloads"}, {"metric": "aggregate speedup", "value": "3.27x", "setting": "APEX-B, Qwen3-8B across six workloads"}, {"metric": "relative reduction in wasted-token percentage", "value": "41.0%", "setting": "APEX-B compared with fixed n-gram speculation at k=16"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: APEX-Router: request-level expert selection among EAGLE-3, n-gram, and draft-model speculation.；APEX-Depth: block-level draft-length adaptation using causal decoding signals and recent verifier feedback.；Modeling accepted draft length as censored survival feedback with position-wise rejection hazards, block execution costs, and an action utility balancing throughput, accepted progress, and wasted tokens.；Integration into vLLM while retaining the target model's verification procedure.

Abstract: Speculative decoding reduces large language model inference latency by drafting multiple tokens before target-model verification, but its effectiveness depends on both the proposal mechanism and draft depth. Fixed configurations cannot respond to changes in predictability, repetition, and acceptance during generation, so deeper drafting can increase wasted computation without proportional speedup. We introduce APEX, a learned controller that balances decoding speed and draft-token waste through request-level expert selection and block-level depth adaptation. APEX-Router selects among EAGLE-3, n-gram, and draft-model speculation for each request, while APEX-Depth adjusts draft length at each verification block using causal decoding signals and recent verifier feedback. APEX models accepted draft length as censored survival feedback, learning position-wise rejection hazards, block execution costs, and an action utility that balances throughput, accepted progress, and wasted tokens. This allows the controller to adapt speculation while retaining the target model's verification procedure. We integrate APEX into vLLM and evaluate it with Qwen3-8B across six workloads, achieving up to 5.24X speedup over autoregressive decoding. Across the aggregate evaluation, APEX-S achieves 4.27X speedup, while APEX-B achieves 3.27X speedup with a 41.0% relative reduction in wasted-token percentage compared with fixed n-gram speculation at k=16, providing distinct operating points for balancing acceleration and draft-token utilization.

</details>


### [14] [Does Steering Break Your Model? A Multi-Dimensional Evaluation Suite for LLM Steering Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07722)
*Haotian Yang,Huikang Jiang,Yucheng Wu,Wen-Jie Jiang,Chenpeng Wang,Yibin Lou,Liangming Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出SteerScope评估套件，通过15个指标系统评估LLM激活引导方法的效果与副作用，基准测试23种方法，发现现有方法尚未超越提示基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅碎片化地覆盖激活引导的各个维度，缺乏对引导效果与副作用之间权衡的系统性刻画。

Method: SteerScope是一个两轴多维度评估套件。第一轴评估引导结果，涵盖目标效果（目标行为是否达成）和副作用（语言质量、任务能力、安全与可靠性）；第二轴评估方法属性，包括泛化性和数据依赖性，具体通过样本效率与样本敏感性指标衡量。评估方法包括23种方法，分属4个家族，并包含prompting、LoRA和SFT作为基线。在匹配模型、任务和评估协议下运行所有方法，联合分析效果与副作用之间的权衡。提供可扩展代码库。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出SteerScope，一个双轴、多维度的评估套件，通过15个指标联合刻画引导结果和方法属性。；在匹配模型、任务和评估协议下，对4个家族共23种方法（包括prompting、LoRA、SFT基线）进行基准测试，并发布扩展代码库。；发现当前激活引导方法尚未在整体权衡上超过Prompt Steering基线，且引导效果与副作用存在一致耦合。；揭示OOD提示下目标效果通常保持，但副作用（如指令相关性下降、流利度降低）更为明显。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight and flexible way to control large language model (LLM) behavior. However, effective steering requires more than inducing the intended behavior: it should also limit unintended changes and remain robust across inputs and training data. Existing evaluations cover these dimensions only in fragments. As a result, the trade-offs between efficacy and side effects have not been systematically characterized. We introduce SteerScope, a two-axis, multi-dimensional evaluation suite that jointly characterizes steering outcomes and method properties through 15 metrics. We score target efficacy and side effects on language quality, task capabilities, and safety and reliability, and further assess generalization and data dependence through steering-specific metrics for sample efficiency and sample sensitivity. Rather than comparing methods at a single operating point, we characterize the trade-offs between efficacy and side effects. Under matched models, tasks, and evaluation protocols, we benchmark 23 methods spanning 4 families, including prompting, LoRA, and SFT as baseline methods, and release the suite as an extensible codebase. We find that current activation steering methods do not yet surpass the Prompt Steering baseline in their overall balance between steering efficacy and side effects: across both model scales, no evaluated activation steering method achieves higher efficacy without incurring greater composite side effects. We further uncover a consistent coupling between steering efficacy and side effects. Under OOD prompts, target efficacy is often preserved, whereas side effects tend to become more pronounced, particularly through declines in instruction relevance and fluency. Methods also exhibit sharply different sample-efficiency profiles.

</details>


### [15] [SanSi: A Looped Typed Decision Model for System 1.5 Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07730)
*Shuyu Gan,Young-Jun Lee,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SanSi将预训练循环语言模型转化为类型化决策模型，通过循环复用层与逐循环proper scoring rule训练，使单模型一次训练即可支持1-8个循环的推理预算。在10,027个测试决策上达到72.0%准确率，比同形非循环模型高13.5点，并可在深度受控任务外推。无需黄金答案即可作为RL裁判，提升生成器F1 7.7点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在一次前向传播（System 1）与生成式推理之间取得平衡：让模型在承诺答案前以低成本方式修订隐藏状态，实现多预算推理与类型化概率决策输出。

Method: SanSi以预训练的循环语言模型（looped LM）为基础，将其改造为类型化决策模型。输入是一个已声明的问题及其选项集合，模型不生成文本。前向过程中，同一组层被递归应用多次（looping），每次循环后模型修订隐藏状态，并在每个循环结束时通过一个决策头读出每个选项的概率。训练采用proper scoring rule对每一循环的读出进行监督，因而单次训练运行即可得到一个能在推理时按任意预算（1至8个循环）使用的模型：预算低时接近System 1的快速直觉，预算高时接近更审慎的推理。在深度受控任务上，循环结构使模型能够解决训练深度之外的问题，而更大的单通道模型无法外推。推理时按需选择循环次数，在单次前向中完成类型化概率输出，无需自回归生成。此外，SanSi可充当RL策略优化的裁判：利用其选项概率作为奖励信号，无需黄金答案即可提升生成器的F1 7.7点。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "72.0%", "setting": "10,027个测试决策，来自59个来源"}, {"metric": "accuracy relative gain vs non-looped same-shape", "value": "+13.5 points", "setting": "相同架构与训练配方但不循环"}, {"metric": "accuracy relative gain vs newer non-looped same-size", "value": "+5.3 points", "setting": "更新但同规模的非循环模型"}, {"metric": "accuracy relative gap vs 3x size model", "value": "-1.8 points", "setting": "三倍参数的模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将预训练循环语言模型转化为类型化决策模型，单次前向即可返回声明选项的概率分布；在每个循环后读取选项概率并用proper scoring rule训练，使单个模型在一次运行中支持1到8个循环的任意预算；深度受控任务上，循环可外推至训练未见深度，而更大的单通道模型失败；无需黄金答案即可作为RL策略优化的裁判，提升生成器F1 7.7点

Abstract: Typed decision models answer a declared question without generating text: a decision head returns a probability for each of the declared options in a single forward pass. A single pass is fast, intuitive System 1 thinking. We study what lies between one pass and generated reasoning: looping, in which the same layers are recursively applied several times before one typed readout. Each loop lets the model revise its hidden state before it commits to an answer, without generating a token; we call this System 1.5 thinking. We propose SanSi, which turns a pre-trained looped language model into a typed decision model. The option probabilities are read after every loop, and every loop is trained with a proper scoring rule, so that one model serves every budget from one loop to eight in a single run. On 10,027 test decisions from 59 sources, SanSi reaches 72.0% accuracy: 13.5 points above a non-looped model of the same shape trained with the same recipe, 5.3 points above a newer non-looped model of its size, and 1.8 points below one with three times the parameters. On two depth-controlled tasks, loops extend the solvable depth beyond the depths seen in training, where the larger single-pass model fails. Used as the judge for policy optimization with reinforcement learning, without gold answers, SanSi raises the generator's F1 by 7.7 points.

</details>


### [16] [DLoop: Looped Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07659)
*Geonmo Gu,Byeongho Heo,HeeJae Jun,Yoohoon Kang,Sangmin Lee,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DLoop 提出循环式投机解码，在验证前自适应执行多轮草稿并一次性验证所有累积 token，配合 loop-aware training 让并行草稿模型在额外阶段保持可靠，在多种投机解码方法上实现 5% 到 41% 的无损墙钟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行草稿模型的投机解码中，即使草稿模型非常有信心、可继续生成更多 token，每个草稿阶段后仍强制进行一次验证，导致目标模型的冗余前向传播；自适应草稿长度方法主要适用于自回归草稿模型，对并行草稿模型无法直接延长草稿。

Method: DLoop 在标准投机解码流程上引入循环结构。标准投机解码中，草稿模型先并行或自回归地生成一段草稿 token，目标模型随后验证。DLoop 允许在单次验证之前重复多个草稿阶段：当草稿模型对其生成的 token 保持较高置信度时，系统继续执行下一轮草稿，而不是立刻触发目标模型验证。由于并行草稿模型在生成后续草稿 token 时需要目标模型对尚未验证草稿 token 的隐藏状态，DLoop 采用 loop-aware training 将草稿模型暴露于自身对未验证 token 的隐藏状态，从而使其在额外草稿阶段仍能可靠工作。所有累积草稿 token 最终被一起送去验证，从而减少目标模型的前向传播次数。该方法以增加草稿模型前向传播为代价，换取目标模型验证成本的下降，实现无损解码前提下的墙钟加速，并可集成到 EAGLE-3、DFlash、Domino、DSpark 和 multi-token prediction 等多种草稿架构中。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "wall-clock speedup", "value": "5% to 41%", "setting": "在 EAGLE-3、DFlash、Domino、DSpark 以及 multi-token prediction 模块等多种投机解码方法上，保持无损解码"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出循环式投机解码 DLoop，在验证前自适应执行多个草稿阶段，将所有累积的草稿 token 一次性验证；提出 loop-aware training，让草稿模型在额外草稿阶段中接触自身对未验证草稿 token 的隐藏状态，保持其可靠性；通过增加草稿模型前向传播换取目标模型验证前向次数减少，实现无损解码下的墙钟时间加速

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation in large language models. In each drafting stage, a lightweight draft model proposes tokens that the target model subsequently verifies. With increasingly capable draft models, we find that the target model frequently accepts all tokens produced in a drafting stage. A verification nevertheless follows each drafting stage, resulting in unnecessary target-model forward passes even when drafting could have continued. Adaptive draft length methods decide during decoding how many draft tokens precede a verification, but they raise the speedup only for autoregressive draft models. For a parallel draft model, drafting further requires target-model hidden states for draft tokens that have not been verified. We propose DLoop, a looped form of speculative decoding that adaptively performs multiple drafting stages before verification. DLoop continues drafting while the draft model remains confident and verifies all accumulated draft tokens together. Loop-aware training keeps the draft model reliable in the additional drafting stages by exposing it to its own hidden states for unverified draft tokens. By spending additional draft-model forward passes, DLoop reduces the number of target-model forward passes required for verification. Across diverse speculative decoding methods including EAGLE-3, DFlash, Domino, DSpark, and multi-token prediction modules, DLoop improves the wall-clock speedup by 5 to 41 percent while preserving lossless decoding. Code will be available at this https URL .

</details>


### [17] [Monte Carlo Estimation for KV Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07643)
*Ahsan Bilal,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Muhammad Umer,Wajih Hassan Raza,Atta Ul Asad,Young D. Kwon,Michal Valko,Dean F. Hougen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LORE-KV是一种无需训练的未来感知KV缓存淘汰方法，通过从冻结模型采样短自回归延续来估计提示令牌的未来效用，在LongBench和RULER上取得显著提升，但存在1.46-2.77倍推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定预算下进行未来感知的KV缓存淘汰，即预测哪些缓存记忆在解码回答时更重要，而非仅基于提示窗口的重要性。

Method: LORE-KV是一种无需训练的未来感知KV缓存淘汰方法。输入为提示词和固定的KV预算B。首先，从冻结的目标模型采样短的短自回归延续（即伪未来轨迹），这些延续模拟可能的模型条件查询轨迹。对于每个采样的未来轨迹，使用其响应侧的查询状态来估计提示令牌的效用。具体地，计算投影留一注意力输出删除代价：对于每个提示令牌，评估将其从注意力计算中删除后对注意力输出的影响，通过投影到查询状态空间进行评分。然后，跨采样的未来轨迹聚合这些分数，可选地使用可靠性加权（例如轨迹权重）。聚合后的分数用于选择top-B令牌保留在KV缓存中。在最终解码前，丢弃所有临时采样产生的延续，仅使用选定的提示令牌KV缓存进行实际生成。整个过程不涉及辅助模型或训练。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "LongBench平均分", "value": "45.49 -> 48.24 (+2.75)", "setting": "Qwen2.5-14B, B=128"}, {"metric": "16K RULER平均分", "value": "45.20 -> 51.05 (+5.85)", "setting": "Mistral-7B, B=128"}, {"metric": "单响应延续相对提示窗口控制的增益恢复", "value": "约89%", "setting": "B=128"}, {"metric": "单样本墙钟时间", "value": "1.46-2.77倍 AnDPro", "setting": "一次性压缩开销，六种dense和hybrid-attention骨干"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将未来感知淘汰形式化为模型条件查询轨迹上的分布估计；提出LORE-KV：从冻结目标模型采样短自回归延续，利用响应侧查询状态估计提示令牌效用；使用投影留一注意力输出删除代价评分，并在采样的未来上聚合；无需辅助模型或训练，采样后丢弃临时延续

Abstract: Most KV-cache eviction methods ask, in effect, which memory appeared important while reading the prompt? We instead ask, which memory will matter while answering? Since decoding queries are unavailable at eviction time, prior future-aware methods rely on pseudo-responses or synthetic future-query estimates. We cast fixed-budget future-aware eviction as distributional estimation over plausible model-conditional query trajectories and introduce LORE-KV (Lookahead Output-perturbation with Reliability-weighted Ensembles for Key-Value caches), a training-free method that samples short autoregressive continuations from the frozen target model and uses their response-side query states to estimate prompt-token utility. Tokens are scored by projected leave-one-out attention-output deletion cost and aggregated across sampled futures with optional trajectory weighting. The temporary continuations are discarded before final decoding, requiring no auxiliary model or training. Ablations isolate the mechanism: at B=128, a single response-side continuation recovers about 89% of the gain over the prompt-window control, while additional futures provide smaller improvements. At B=128, LORE-KV raises the LongBench average on Qwen2.5-14B from 45.49 to 48.24 (+2.75) and the 16K RULER average on Mistral-7B from 45.20 to 51.05 (+5.85). Gains diminish at larger cache budgets and coexist with task-level regressions. LORE-KV incurs 1.46-2.77x AnDPro's per-sample wall-clock time as a one-time compression overhead across six dense and hybrid-attention backbones.

</details>


### [18] [Turnslide: Scalable Multi-Turn Data Synthesis by Walking a Finite-State Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07070)
*Aaron Fainman,Gabriela Kadlecová,Maciej Gryka,Bartosz Kruszczyński,Usman Zafar,Cédric Archambeau,Aaron Klein,David Salinas,Selim Nowicki,Jacek Golebiowski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Turnslide提出基于有限状态机的多轮工具调用数据合成框架，通过抽象状态保证工具序列合法性，单次LLM调用生成完整对话，显著降低合成成本。在SLM微调下游任务上达到70.7%全准确率，优于基线63.4%和53.7%，且token用量减少3.6-6.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在多轮工具调用场景下基座模型能力不足，且缺少按API定制的微调数据；现有合成方法需要模拟运行环境且每轮对话消耗多次LLM调用，成本过高，难以大规模生成高质量多轮工具调用数据。

Method: 该框架将每个API建模为有限状态机（FSM），抽象状态决定每个工具何时可被调用，从而生成状态合法的工具调用序列。生成的工具序列通过单次LLM调用转化为完整的多轮对话示例。训练目标为设定关于对话轮数、工具序列和任务复杂度的目标分布，而非优化多样性。微调时使用生成轨迹训练小语言模型，推理时模型按多轮工具调用格式执行。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "full accuracy", "value": "70.7%", "setting": "在生成轨迹上微调SLM的下游全准确率"}, {"metric": "full accuracy", "value": "63.4%", "setting": "对比基线方法的下游全准确率"}, {"metric": "full accuracy", "value": "53.7%", "setting": "未突变基线（unmutated baseline）的下游全准确率"}, {"metric": "token 使用量", "value": "减少 3.6-6.6 倍", "setting": "与现有工作对比生成相同质量数据所需的token数"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将每个API建模为有限状态机（FSM），以抽象状态定义工具调用规则，生成状态合法的工具序列；通过单次LLM调用将FSM生成的工具序列转化为完整的多轮对话示例，大幅降低合成成本；设定关于轮数、工具序列和任务复杂度的目标分布（而非优化多样性）来指导数据生成

Abstract: Small language models are inexpensive to serve and can run on private infrastructure, but base models are often not good enough at multi-turn tool calling, and fine-tuning them needs per-API data that rarely exists. Existing synthesis methods are too expensive for high-scale fine-tuning, as they often require mock operational environments for different domains and multiple LLM calls per generated conversation turn. We introduce a fully automated, lightweight synthesis framework that models each API as a finite-state machine, representing the system as abstract states that determine when each tool may be called, producing state-valid sequences of tools; sequences are translated into complete examples with a single LLM call. Rather than optimize diversity, we set a target distribution over the number of turns, the tool sequence and task complexity. We measure data quality by fine-tuning SLMs on generated trajectories, showing that our FSM-based generation significantly improves downstream accuracy over an unmutated baseline and, against existing works, reaches 70.7% full accuracy over 63.4% and 53.7% with 3.6-6.6$\times$ fewer tokens.

</details>


### [19] [WavePrune: One period is often enough for RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06963)
*Guancheng Du,Luotian Huang,Shaowen Wang,Si Li,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WavePrune提出将RoPE每个通道限制在第一个旋转周期内，消除位置混叠，提升长上下文性能并在32K上下文下实现预填充1.15倍、解码1.24倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE的周期性旋转导致相对位置混叠（位置别名），即距离为一个完整旋转周期的相对位置难以区分，从而引入注意力图的干扰并损害长上下文性能。

Method: WavePrune作用于Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE)。原始RoPE将查询和键向量中的每个二维通道对按通道特定频率旋转，以编码位置信息。WavePrune的核心修改是限制每个通道的旋转角度不超过第一个完整旋转周期（即滑动窗口范围），从而避免相对位置相隔完整周期时产生混叠。这种限制使得注意力计算中仅需关注局部窗口内的位置关系，诱导出细粒度的稀疏注意力模式。基于该稀疏性，作者实现了硬件对齐的CUDA内核，在预填充阶段跳过窗口外的注意力计算，在解码阶段利用稀疏性减少内存访问和计算，从而提升推理速度。模型无需额外调优即可直接应用WavePrune，或在从头预训练时使用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "HELMET score", "value": "35.7 -> 40.0", "setting": "Qwen3-8B模型，无需额外调优"}, {"metric": "HELMET score", "value": "在5个模型中的4个上提升", "setting": "无需额外调优"}, {"metric": "验证损失", "value": "外推长度下更低", "setting": "从头预训练模型对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出WavePrune，将每个通道限制在第一个旋转周期内，消除位置混叠带来的注意力干扰；利用滑动窗口诱导的细粒度稀疏性，设计硬件对齐的CUDA内核实现预填充和解码加速；验证RoPE的周期性结构在第一个旋转周期之外大多是冗余的

Abstract: Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) encodes token positions by rotating each two-dimensional channel of the query and key vectors at a channel-specific frequency, making the attention logits invariant to a common shift of positions. However, this rotation is periodic, and it leads to position aliasing where relative positions separated by a full rotation period become hard to tell apart. To address this, we propose WavePrune, which restricts each channel to its first rotation period. We show that it removes the distractions in attention maps created by position aliasing and improves overall long-context performance. Specifically, WavePrune raises the HELMET score on four of five models we test without any extra tuning (e.g., 35.7 -> 40.0 on Qwen3-8B). When pretraining models from scratch, WavePrune also achieves lower validation loss at extrapolated lengths than pretraining without it. Because WavePrune restricts each channel to a sliding window, it induces a fine-grained sparsity that our hardware-aligned CUDA kernels exploit for 1.15x prefill and 1.24x decoding speedups over FlashAttention-2 at 32K context. Together, these results show that RoPE's periodic structure, widely regarded as essential, is largely redundant beyond the first rotation period.

</details>


### [20] [Capacity, Responsiveness and Alignment: What Makes a Latent Structure Actionable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06897)
*Or Shafran,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过将语言模型激活空间的因果影响力分解为容量、响应性和对齐三个因子，揭示因果有效性需要三者均高且受上下文影响，提出因果探针方法，在引导任务上实现17%-118%的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语言模型的激活空间中，什么样的潜在结构是因果有效的（可操作的）？

Method: 该研究分析语言模型激活空间中的方向性结构。对于每个概念和上下文，首先识别与概念相关的激活方向（如通过线性探针）。然后定义三个因子：容量（输出对沿该方向移动的敏感度）、响应性（给定上下文下概念的可提升程度）和对齐（结构与上下文特定概念表示的匹配度）。通过干预实验（沿方向激活或抑制）测量因果效果。实验跨越4个LM家族和50个概念，观察到因果有效性需要三个因子都高；低容量和低响应性分别降低因果效果84%和95%，低对齐可反转效果。进一步发现因果有效方向形成低维子空间且随上下文变化。基于此，提出因果探针：将线性探针的训练限制在该子空间内，从而提升引导性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "引导改进", "value": "17%-118%", "setting": "跨4个LM家族和50个概念，因果探针相比传统线性探针"}, {"metric": "概念检测下降", "value": "3%", "setting": "因果探针相比传统线性探针"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将因果影响力分解为容量、响应性和对齐三个可解释因子；发现因果有效方向形成随上下文变化的低维子空间；提出因果探针，将线性探针训练限制在该子空间内，提升引导效果

Abstract: Localizing latent structures in the activation space of language models (LMs) is central to understanding and controlling their behavior. Yet, localized structures can differ substantially in their causal influence, raising the question of what makes a structure actionable. We tackle this question by casting causal influence as a product of three factors and showing empirically that they act as interpretable, distinct constraints: capacity, measuring the sensitivity of the model's output to movement along the structure, responsiveness, capturing how promotable the concept is given the current context, and alignment, reflecting how well the structure aligns with the context-specific representation of the concept. Across 4 LM families and 50 concepts, we observe that causal effectiveness requires all factors to be high; low capacity and responsiveness reduce it by 84% and 95%, respectively, while low alignment can reverse it, suppressing concept expression. Moreover, we find that causality is context-dependent rather than an intrinsic property of the structure, with causally effective directions forming a low-dimensional subspace that varies across contexts. By restricting the training of linear probes to this subspace, we introduce causal probes that achieve 17%-118% improvement in steering across models, with only 3% reduction in concept detection.

</details>


### [21] [Tree Navigation Without LLM Summaries: A Matched-Cost Study of Hierarchical Retrieval for Long-Document QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06902)
*Priyank Jayraj,Poonam Goyal,Navneet Goyal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出NavTree，一种无需LLM摘要的层次检索器，通过确定性平衡段树和混合词法-稠密前沿游走来导航文档块。在长文档问答的匹配成本评估中，NavTree优于RAPTOR式摘要树和BM25，且索引零LLM调用，表明分层树的主要收益来自导航结构而非摘要内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAPTOR式摘要树在长文档检索中依赖LLM生成摘要，索引成本高；论文试图证明分层树的主要收益来自导航结构而非生成的摘要内容，并探索零LLM调用的树导航检索能否达到或超越摘要树的效果。

Method: NavTree在索引阶段将文档切分为块（chunks），并构建一棵确定性的平衡段树（segment tree），叶节点对应原始文档块，内部节点通过确定性规则（如合并子节点）形成，但不使用LLM生成摘要。查询时，首先用混合词法-稠密检索获得顶部叶节点作为锚点，然后从根节点开始执行混合词法-稠密前沿游走（frontier walk），在每一层根据得分选择要下降的分支，最终仅输出叶节点块给阅读器。训练目标未涉及模型训练，仅依赖现成的检索器评分；推理流程为：查询编码→顶部叶节点检索→树游走→叶块输出。该设计通过树结构导航弥补平坦检索可能聚集在单一区域、遗漏互补证据的问题，同时避免LLM摘要带来的索引成本。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "检索性能（匹配成本）", "value": "NavTree是最强的匹配成本分层检索器，并与最强平坦基线持平", "setting": "匹配成本评估网格，对比平坦检索器与RAPTOR提取式复现"}, {"metric": "显著优于BM25", "value": "在类对类基础上是唯一显著优于BM25的分层方法", "setting": "长文档多跳问答数据集"}, {"metric": "匹配阅读器下的胜出", "value": "抽象式RAPTOR变体在每个多块预算下均低于NavTree，且NavTree索引成本为零", "setting": "匹配阅读器复现，给定强聚类摘要"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出NavTree：仅叶节点检索器，在文档块上构建确定性平衡段树，索引时零LLM调用；树仅作为导航支架：基于顶部检索叶节点的混合词法-稠密前沿游走，从根下降，仅输出叶块给阅读器；在匹配成本评估下，与平坦检索器和RAPTOR提取式复现相比，NavTree是最强的分层检索器且与最强平坦基线持平；在长文档多跳问答中，是唯一在类对类基础上显著优于BM25的分层方法，并通过无阅读器的检索召回检查验证；匹配阅读器复现表明，即使给定强聚类摘要，抽象式RAPTOR变体在每个多块预算下仍劣于NavTree，且NavTree索引成本为零

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation grounds language models in external context, but for long documents flat top-$k$ retrieval can cluster on a single region and miss complementary evidence. RAPTOR-style summary trees address this by recursively clustering chunks and using a language model to summarize each cluster at indexing time, then ranking summary nodes alongside raw chunks at query time. We show the main benefit of summary trees in long-document QA can come from navigation rather than the generated summary content. We introduce NavTree, a leaves-only retriever that builds a deterministic balanced segment tree over chunks (zero language-model calls at indexing) and uses the tree purely as a navigation scaffold: a hybrid lexical-and-dense frontier walk, anchored on top retrieved leaves, descends from the root and emits only leaf chunks to the reader. On a matched-cost evaluation against flat retrievers and an extractive re-implementation of RAPTOR, NavTree is the strongest matched-cost hierarchical retriever in our evaluated grid and ties the strongest flat baseline. On long-document multi-hop QA, it is the only hierarchical method that significantly beats BM25 on a class-vs-class basis, corroborated by a reader-free retrieval-recall check. A matched-reader replication of the published abstractive RAPTOR variant, given strong cluster summaries, still loses to NavTree at every multi-chunk budget, at zero indexing cost. The ranking carries across stronger and open-weight readers, a stronger encoder, and a full factorial that isolates leaves-only emission as the structural lever.

</details>
