*Arnav Kundu,Zhaoyang Xu,Bairu Hou,Chang Gao,Reed Li,Tao Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将弹性嵌套子网络与稀疏 MoE 路由统一到一个 LLM 架构中，输入上下文与目标效率规格共同决定激活容量，使单模型覆盖 1–4B 参数并在知识密集型任务上比同规模稠密模型高 2–5%，延迟相当，适合按设备内存与算力灵活部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在单个模型中同时适配不同部署约束（内存、算力、磁盘）与任务需求，实现细粒度的精度—效率权衡，同时保持输入自适应的稀疏计算。

Method: 论文提出一个统一框架，将弹性（elastic）结构与稀疏门控（sparsely gated, MoE）架构结合。模型主体为一条骨干网络，其输入既包括上下文（context），也包括目标效率规格（target efficiency specification），即模型以“任务输入 + 目标容量/效率条件”为条件进行前向计算。在骨干内部，参数被组织为弹性嵌套子网络（elastically-nested sub-networks），配合稀疏门控模块按输入自适应地激活任务相关参数；通过在嵌套子网络中共享参数，同一套权重可支撑多个容量点（1/2/3/4B）。训练上，模型学习在给定效率规格下激活对应的子网络与专家，从而在推理时可依据设备 DRAM、算力和磁盘限制选择容量点，实现精度—效率的连续可调。推理路径为：接收上下文与效率规格 → 条件化骨干 → 稀疏路由选择子网络参数 → 输出结果；共享参数降低设备磁盘占用，运行延迟与稠密模型相当。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "知识密集型基准准确率", "value": "比同等 dense 模型高 2–5%", "setting": "在 1/2/3/4B 弹性容量点下与 dense 对照模型对比"}, {"metric": "延迟", "value": "与静态模型/稠密模型相当", "setting": "与同规模静态版本对比的延迟指标"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 统一弹性结构与稀疏门控 MoE 的框架，使单一模型可同时适应部署约束和任务需求；骨干网络以上下文和目标效率规格为条件，实现推理时对精度—效率权衡的细粒度控制；在弹性嵌套子网络内学习激活任务相关参数，使单模型覆盖多个容量点（1/2/3/4B）并保持输入自适应路由；参数共享以节省设备磁盘空间，并支持按可用 DRAM 与算力灵活服务

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) is resource-intensive, and adapting them for diverse deployment scenarios with varying computational constraints remains challenging. While elastic architectures enable flexible model deployment and sparsely activated models allow input-adaptive computation, existing approaches treat these dimensions independently. Moreover, models catered towards on-device edge inference need to conform to the memory and compute limitations of the serving devices. In this paper, we introduce a unified framework that combines elastic structures with sparsely gated architectures to create models that adapt simultaneously to both deployment constraints and task requirements. Our approach employs a model backbone that conditions on both the context and target efficiency specifications, enabling fine-grained control over the accuracy-efficiency trade-off at inference time. The model learns to activate task-relevant parameters within elastically-nested sub-networks, allowing a single model to span multiple capacity points while maintaining input-adaptive routing. Through experiments we demonstrate that we can create a model that allows the flexibility to use 1,2,3,4 billion parameters while being more accurate than their dense counter-parts (2-5\% on knowledge-intensive benchmarks) and at par with their static versions while delivering similar latency metrics as dense models. Overall, we save on device disk space by sharing the model parameters, allow flexibility of serving based on DRAM and compute available while delivering more accurate results.

</details>

*Bolian Li,Ting-Yao Hu,Cheng-Yu Hsieh,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Oncel Tuzel,Raviteja Vemulapalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究 MoE 智能体 RL 后训练中的专家路由结构，提出分层路由控制与熵门控机制，使 turn 级路由对齐 agentic 操作、token 级保持局部一致，在评测基准上成功率提升超 10 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长程 LLM 智能体的 RL 后训练中，标准 RL 算法忽略 MoE 专家路由与 agentic 操作结构之间的对齐，导致任务性能和推理效率受限。

Method: 论文围绕 MoE 大模型在 agentic 任务上的 RL 后训练展开。输入为多轮 agentic 轨迹；基础模型为稀疏 MoE 模型，其 token 级路由决定每个 token 激活哪些专家。观察到现成 MoE 模型中，执行语义相似操作（如 READ、UPDATE）的 turn 之间专家路由重叠度更高，说明路由已隐含与操作对齐的结构。标准 RL 后训练未控制该路由，导致性能与推理效率下降。为此提出分层路由控制框架：turn 级别鼓励专家选择对齐 agentic 操作类型，token 级别正则化以维持局部路由一致性；同时引入熵门控控制机制以稳定后训练。训练目标在 RL 目标之外加入上述路由控制与正则项。推理时沿用 MoE 稀疏激活路径，但路由受训练得到的结构约束，以期提升任务成功率与推理效率。摘要未披露具体模型规模、数据量、硬件、延迟等细节。

Conclusion: 发现现成 MoE 模型的专家选择在 agentic 轨迹中呈现与操作类型（如 READ、UPDATE）对齐的专门结构。；提出分层路由控制框架：在 turn 级别激励专家选择对齐 agentic 操作，在 token 级别正则化专家选择以维持局部一致性。；引入熵门控控制机制，缓解后训练中的不稳定问题。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents are frequently implemented using sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, yet the co-design of agentic behavior and MoE structures remains underexplored. In this work, we comprehensively study the connections between agentic post-training and MoE expert selection. In off-the-shelf MoE models, we observe expert selection exhibits a specialized structure that naturally aligns with agentic trajectories. Specifically, expert routing overlaps more between turns where the agent performs semantically similar operations (e.g., READ, UPDATE) than between turns with differing operations. However, standard RL algorithms ignore this specialization, allowing the MoE routing to go uncontrolled during training, which empirically limit task performance and inference efficiency. To address this, we introduce a hierarchical routing control framework for agentic tasks. We explicitly encourage turn-level expert selections to align with agentic operations while regularizing token-level expert selections to maintain local consistency. To resolve stability issues that arise during post-training with the proposed methods, we further introduce an entropy-gated control mechanism. Overall, our routing control framework achieves over 10-point improvements in success rate on all evaluated benchmarks. These results demonstrate that agentic trajectory structure provides an effective signal for optimizing MoE capacity during RL post-training.

</details>


### [51] [Learning What to Distill: Bilevel Top-K Token Selection for Self-Distillation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07247)
*Heng Liang,Xinwen Zhang,Hongchang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiToK-SD 提出一种基于双层优化的 Top-K token 选择方法，用于 on-policy 自蒸馏，使模型自适应地学习在哪些 token 位置进行蒸馏，从而提升紧凑语言模型的数学推理能力，且额外计算开销轻量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 on-policy 自蒸馏方法通常对所有 token 位置均匀蒸馏，或使用固定启发式选择 token，且对选中位置赋予相同蒸馏强度，无法自适应地学习哪些 token 位置最有利于蒸馏。

Method: BiToK-SD 将 on-policy 自蒸馏中的 token 选择构建为双层优化问题。输入为学生模型在当前策略下生成的序列及其 token 表示。下层问题将 Top-K token 选择参数化为可微的阈值松弛，使得选择过程可随学生策略的更新而动态演化，从而学习哪些 token 位置对蒸馏最有益。上层问题仅在经下层选出的 token 位置上执行知识蒸馏，将学生模型与自身或教师分布对齐，优化学生模型的推理能力。训练时两层交替或联合优化：下层根据当前学生策略调整选择阈值与选中集合，上层在选定位置上计算蒸馏损失并更新学生参数。推理阶段无需额外选择模块，直接使用蒸馏后的学生模型生成结果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将 Top-K token 选择建模为可微的阈值松弛，使被选位置能随学生策略演化而自适应调整；提出双层优化框架 BiToK-SD：下层学习 Top-K token 选择，上层仅在选中位置执行知识蒸馏；在数学推理基准上取得最佳平均表现，且仅需轻量额外计算

Abstract: Large language models have shown strong reasoning capabilities, but their high inference costs make knowledge distillation an important approach for transferring such capabilities to compact models in resource-constrained scenarios. On-policy self-distillation further reduces the reliance on external large teacher models while improving the reasoning ability of compact language models. However, existing methods typically either distill all token positions uniformly or select tokens using fixed heuristic criteria, assigning the same distillation strength to the selected positions rather than adaptively learning which tokens are most beneficial for distillation. To address these limitations, we propose BiToK-SD (Bilevel Top-K Token Selection for Self-Distillation), a bilevel-optimization-based token selection method that learns where distillation should be applied during on-policy self-distillation. Specifically, BiToK-SD is formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, where the lower-level problem models Top-K token selection as a differentiable threshold-based relaxation, allowing the selected positions to adapt as the student policy evolves, while the upper-level problem performs knowledge distillation on the selected positions. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that BiToK-SD achieves the best average performance among all compared methods while requiring only lightweight additional computation.

</details>


### [52] [Distributionally Robust Mixture-of-Experts Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07207)
*Xin Teng,Muxiao Li,Hongyi Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DRMoET，一种面向稀疏MoE Transformer的分布鲁棒训练目标，通过熵正则化softmax更新层内专家分布来优化高损失路由结果而非仅均衡流量。在FLAME-MoE 746M和10.3B规模上，10.3B/67B tokens设置下七任务平均从0.6625提升至0.6767，优于auxiliary-loss-free基线0.6431，并显示错误路由下超额损失降低4.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏MoE Transformer中路由不完美时，负载均衡模型仍可能将token分配给对其输入训练不足的专家，导致可靠性问题；仅平衡流量无法保证高损失路由结果被优化。

Method: DRMoET在标准MoE Transformer基础上增加分布鲁棒训练目标。输入token经路由网络得到专家分配概率，每层专家作为内生鲁棒性分组；在训练中按EMA平滑、激活加权的专家损失，通过熵正则化softmax规则更新每层专家分布，使高损失路由结果（包括非top路径）获得更强优化，同时保持标准MoE的前向计算与稀疏激活机制不变。实验在FLAME-MoE训练配方下进行，覆盖746M和10.3B总参数量规模，训练67B tokens，优化标准语言建模损失并加入该鲁棒目标。推理时仍使用原始MoE路由与专家计算，不增加额外参数或改变模型结构。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "七任务平均", "value": "0.6767", "setting": "10.3B总参数量，67B训练tokens，对比标准FLAME-MoE 0.6625，auxiliary-loss-free基线0.6431"}, {"metric": "七任务平均提升", "value": "0.6625 -> 0.6767", "setting": "10.3B总参数量，67B训练tokens，对比标准FLAME-MoE"}, {"metric": "强制mid-k错误路由下的超额损失降低", "value": "4.3%", "setting": "与基线对比，具体基线未在摘要中说明"}, {"metric": "专家损失方差", "value": "降低", "setting": "机制分析，均值损失几乎不变"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将每层专家视为内生鲁棒性分组，构建分布鲁棒优化目标，优化高损失路由结果而非仅均衡流量。；提出基于EMA平滑、激活加权的专家损失，通过熵正则化softmax规则更新每层专家分布，强化合理的非top路由路径。；保持标准MoE计算不变，作为drop-in目标可集成到现有训练流程。；在FLAME-MoE配方下746M和10.3B总参数量规模验证，并给出强制mid-k错误路由下的超额损失分析。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) transformers scale capacity by activating only a few experts per token, but this sparsity creates a hidden reliability problem: when routing is imperfect, load-balanced models may send tokens to experts that are insufficiently trained for the assigned inputs. We propose Distributionally Robust MoE Training (DRMoET), a drop-in objective that treats layer-wise experts as endogenous robustness groups and optimizes high-loss routing outcomes rather than merely equalizing traffic. DRMoET updates a per-layer expert distribution by an entropy-regularized softmax rule on EMA-smoothed, activation-weighted expert losses, strengthening plausible non-top routing paths while preserving standard MoE computation. Under the FLAME-MoE recipe at 746M-total and 10.3B-total scales, DRMoET improves downstream averages over both standard FLAME-MoE and auxiliary-loss-free balancing. At 10.3B total parameters and 67B training tokens, DRMoET improves the seven-task average from 0.6625 to 0.6767, while the auxiliary-loss-free baseline achieves 0.6431. Mechanistic analyses show lower expert-loss variance with nearly unchanged mean loss, 4.3% lower excess loss under forced mid-$k$ misrouting, and improved domain-expert specialization. These results position routing robustness-not only utilization balance-as a practical objective for reliable sparse MoE scaling. Project page and code are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [53] [Exact Unlearning via Quantized Sufficient Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07197)
*Ami Tavory,Shripad Gade,Tal Sarig,Noam Touitou,Ido Guy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QSS 提出一种通过量化充分统计量实现精确遗忘的方法，将模型分为冻结模式与可和分解内容，删除即减法，概率性重建保证一致性。在 15 个数据集上，删除延迟比 SISA 降低 4–483 倍，精度损失在 2 个百分点内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已部署的预测模型中，如何以低延迟实现精确遗忘（exact unlearning），同时保证删除后的模型与从头重建的模型完全一致。

Method: QSS 将预测模型分解为两个组件：一个冻结的全局模式（schema）和可变的内容（content）。schema 学习全局结构，在训练后保持冻结；content 以可和分解的充分统计量存储局部预测修正，按量化区域索引。删除内容时，直接从统计量中减去对应样本的贡献，实现精确遗忘；若删除导致 schema 覆盖不足，则触发完整重建。QSS 提供两种变体：QSS-L 仅精确移除标签但保留未标记输入；QSS-E 通过在不含可删除样本上学习 schema，精确移除输入和标签。删除操作以概率 1-ρ 走算术快速路径，以概率 ρ 触发完整重建。推理时，模型结合 schema 与 content 统计量进行预测。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "expected deletion latency", "value": "4–483× lower", "setting": "在低类别数任务上，ρ=0.5%，与 SISA 对比"}, {"metric": "accuracy difference vs. SISA", "value": "within 2 percentage points", "setting": "15个视觉、文本和表格数据集中的11个任务，ρ=0.5%，QSS-L"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Quantized Sufficient Statistics (QSS)，将模型分为冻结的全局模式（schema）与可变的、可和分解的内容（sufficient statistics），从而将删除操作转化为精确的算术减法。；区分两种保证：QSS-L 精确移除标签但保留未标记输入；QSS-E 通过在不含可删除样本上学习模式，精确移除输入和标签。；设计概率性重建机制：以概率 1-ρ 走算术快速路径，以概率 ρ 触发完整重建，所有报告的延迟均包含两种事件。；在 15 个视觉、文本和表格数据集上验证，在 ρ=0.5% 时 QSS-L 在 11 个任务上精度与 SISA 差距在 2 个百分点以内，并在低类别任务上实现 4–483 倍的期望删除延迟降低。

Abstract: Exact unlearning requires a deployed predictor to match one rebuilt without the information named by a deletion request. Existing general-purpose exact methods localize retraining through disjoint shards, but every request still invalidates a model, and smaller shards reduce the data available to each constituent predictor. We introduce Quantized Sufficient Statistics (QSS), which separates a small frozen schema from mutable, sum-decomposable content. The schema learns global structure; the content stores local prediction corrections as additive statistics indexed by quantized regions. Deleting content is therefore exact subtraction rather than optimization. We distinguish two guarantees: QSS-L exactly removes a label while retaining the unlabelled input, whereas QSS-E exactly removes both input and label by learning the schema without deletable examples. A deletion takes the arithmetic fast path with probability $1-\rho$ and triggers a full rebuild with probability $\rho$; all reported expected latencies include both events. Across 15 vision, text, and tabular datasets at $\rho=0.5\%$, QSS-L is within 2 percentage points of SISA on 11 tasks and provides 4--483$\times$ lower expected deletion latency on the low-class-count tasks where a compact schema is effective. QSS-E quantifies the additional accuracy cost of removing every trace of an input.

</details>


### [54] [SoloQ: Calibration-Free Quantization for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07121)
*Donghyun Lee,Arkapravo Ghosh,Varun Manjunath,Bumjoon Kyle Rhee,Hyunho Kook,Shiting Xiao,Youngeun Kim,Priyadarshini Panda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoloQ提出免校准的低比特权重量化框架，通过归一化旋转基与K-RPBH旋转使激活分布可预测，从而支持数据无关量化与NVFP4；在LLaDA、Dream、Fast-LLM v2、Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion上4-bit精度优于校准基线，NVFP4峰值显存最高降低2.61倍、推理最高加速2.24倍，并含commit-time KV-cache量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（尤其full-sequence与block-diffusion两类）模型规模大、推理成本高；现有后训练量化方法依赖校准数据，但dLLM激活分布会随masking状态和去噪步变化，导致校准不可靠。

Method: SoloQ面向扩散语言模型的低比特权重-激活量化。输入为dLLM的权重和激活；其核心是将权重和激活映射到归一化旋转基，使旋转后的激活分布具有可预测的边缘分布，从而无需校准数据即可确定量化参数。框架采用结构化K-RPBH旋转，并配合轻量rescaling correction来校正量化误差。由于旋转后分布可预测，SoloQ既能构建distribution-matched codebooks，也能直接支持硬件原生NVFP4格式。对于block-diffusion dLLM，SoloQ额外对KV-cache执行commit-time量化：在某一去噪块被提交为持久状态时才压缩其KV-cache，避免干扰当前正在去噪的活跃块，从而在压缩持久状态的同时保持生成质量。推理时，量化后的权重和激活以低比特执行前向计算，并配合commit-time KV-cache管理；在NVFP4下可降低峰值显存并加速端到端推理。训练目标与优化细节未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "4-bit量化精度", "value": "保持准确率并优于基于校准的基线", "setting": "LLaDA、Dream（full-sequence dLLM）以及Fast-LLM v2、Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion（block-diffusion dLLM）在知识密集和推理密集基准上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 免校准量化框架SoloQ：将权重和激活映射到归一化旋转基，形成可预测的边缘分布，实现数据无关量化；结构化K-RPBH旋转加轻量rescaling correction，实现免校准量化；利用旋转后可预测分布支持distribution-matched codebooks与硬件原生NVFP4；针对block-diffusion模型提出commit-time KV-cache量化，压缩持久状态且不扰动当前去噪块

Abstract: Diffusion large language models dLLMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive language models through bidirectional diffusion-based token generation. However, their growing model sizes and high inference costs make efficient deployment challenging: full-sequence denoising repeatedly invokes compute-intensive forward passes, while block-diffusion models additionally introduce a memory-intensive KV-cache. Low-bit weight-activation quantization is therefore attractive, yet existing dLLM post-training quantization methods rely on calibration data despite activation distributions shifting across masking states and denoising steps. We present SoloQ, a calibration-free quantization framework that maps weights and activations into a normalized rotated basis with a predictable marginal distribution, enabling data-independent quantization. SoloQ combines a structured K-RPBH rotation with a lightweight rescaling correction for calibration-free quantization. Its predictable post-rotation distribution supports both distribution-matched codebooks and hardware-native NVFP4. For block-diffusion models, SoloQ further applies commit-time KV-cache quantization to compress persistent states without perturbing the actively denoised block. Across full-sequence dLLMs (LLaDA and Dream) and block-diffusion dLLMs(Fast-dLLM v2 and Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion), SoloQ retains accuracy under 4-bit quantization and outperforms calibration-based baselines on knowledge- and reasoning-intensive benchmarks. With NVFP4, SoloQ reduces peak memory by up to 2.61X and accelerates end-to-end inference by up to 2.24X.

</details>


### [55] [Will the Judge Flip? Predicting Position-Sensitive LLM Judgments from Residual Stream Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07115)
*Hashmath Shaik,Gnaneswar Villuri,Alex Doboli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究如何预测 LLM 评判者的位置翻转，利用判决前残差流激活训练线性探针，在 JudgeBench 和 MT-Bench 上取得 AUROC 0.621-0.853，优于非激活基线，可潜在地将双倍评判成本减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 评判者的判决会受到候选回答呈现顺序的影响（位置翻转），而检测这种翻转通常需要对每对回答进行正反两次评判，导致评判成本翻倍。能否利用初始判决前的残差流激活来预测该翻转，从而避免重复评判？

Method: 论文提出一种基于残差流激活的翻转预测方法。输入为 LLM 评判者在给出初始判决前最后一层的残差流激活向量，以及候选回答对。使用正则化线性探针（linear probe）在该激活向量上训练二分类器，预测该判决是否会因交换候选顺序而翻转。评估采用嵌套分组交叉验证：外层用于评估泛化，内层用于超参数选择，分组按问题划分以避免泄漏。训练目标为二元交叉熵。对比基线结合了言语化置信度、判决标签 logits、回答长度和初始选择等非激活特征。此外，将在 JudgeBench 上训练的探针冻结后直接应用于 MT-Bench 比较，无需在 MT-Bench 上拟合或调整，以测试跨数据集泛化。推理时，只需记录一次初始判决前的激活，通过线性探针输出翻转概率，若概率高则可选择进行反向评判以节省一次完整判决。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AUROC", "value": "0.621-0.850", "setting": "JudgeBench 534 对，Qwen3 与 Llama-3.1-8B 评判者，嵌套分组交叉验证"}, {"metric": "AUROC 提升", "value": "0.062-0.113", "setting": "相比结合了言语化置信度、判决标签 logits、回答长度和初始选择的基线"}, {"metric": "AUROC", "value": "0.685-0.853", "setting": "冻结探针在 MT-Bench 1802 个比较上的评估，无微调或重新校准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次提出利用 LLM 评判者做出初始判决前的残差流激活来预测位置敏感翻转。；在 JudgeBench 上使用嵌套分组交叉验证训练正则化线性探针，AUROC 达到 .621-.850。；线性探针在 JudgeBench 上训练后冻结，直接迁移到 1802 个 MT-Bench 比较上，无需重新拟合或校准，AUROC 达到 .685-.853。

Abstract: The order in which candidate responses are presented can change an LLM judge's verdict. Detecting such a position flip ordinarily requires judging each pair in both orders, which doubles the number of judgments. We investigate whether residual stream activations recorded immediately before the initial verdict can predict a flip. We use nested grouped cross-validation to evaluate regularized linear probes on 534 JudgeBench pairs for three Qwen3 judges and Llama-3.1-8B. The linear probes achieve AUROCs of .621-.850 and outperform a combined baseline that uses verbalized confidence, verdict-label logits, response lengths, and the judge's initial choice by .062-.113 AUROC. Linear probes trained on JudgeBench and then frozen achieve AUROCs of .685-.853 on 1,802 MT-Bench comparisons without MT-Bench fitting or recalibration. These results show that pre-verdict activations support prediction of susceptibility to candidate order and outperform the non-activation predictors evaluated here.

</details>


### [56] [SchemaFill: Efficient LLM Tool Calling via Slot-Parallel Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07086)
*Zhi-Kai Chen,Song-Yan Li,De-Chuan Zhan,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SchemaFill提出槽位并行推测解码框架，用于加速LLM工具调用中结构化调用与参数的生成。通过并发生成未来槽位候选并在真实前缀下由目标模型验证，仅提交验证通过的token，在Glaive和BFCL上实现最高4.05倍端到端吞吐提升，同时保持与目标模型输出一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM工具调用模型需要逐token自回归生成多个工具调用及其参数，在多调用或参数字段较多时延迟很高；如何在保证与目标模型输出一致的前提下并行生成结构化调用中的槽位值以降低延迟。

Method: SchemaFill的输入包括用户请求、对话上下文和工具模式目录。工具调用模型需要选择工具并生成其参数，可能在一个响应中产生多个调用。标准自回归解码逐token生成这些调用，导致多调用或参数字段多时延迟高。SchemaFill利用参数结构进行槽位并行推测解码：并发生成未来槽位值作为候选，不要求提前知道实际调用序列或参数值；将跨越多个字段和调用的候选拼接，并在实际输出前缀下由目标模型验证。仅验证通过的token被提交，目标模型在候选不一致时提供纠正。该流程将目标验证应用于槽位和调用间的并行生成，从而在保持目标模型输出一致性的同时提升端到端吞吐。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "端到端吞吐", "value": "最高4.05倍提升", "setting": "在Glaive和BFCL基准上与自回归解码对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 槽位并行推测解码框架，并发生成未来槽位值作为候选，无需预先知道实际调用序列或参数值；跨越多个参数字段和多个调用的候选拼接与在真实输出前缀下的目标模型验证；仅提交验证通过的token，候选不一致时由目标模型提供纠正，从而在槽位和调用间利用并行性并保持目标一致性

Abstract: LLM agents interact with external systems by generating structured tool calls. Given a user request, conversational context, and a catalog of tool schemas, a tool-calling model must select tools and generate their arguments, potentially producing multiple calls in a single response. Standard autoregressive decoding generates these calls token by token, incurring substantial latency for requests involving multiple calls or many argument fields. The explicit argument structure offers opportunities for parallel generation, but later argument values may depend on preceding fields and calls, so independently generated values can differ from the target model's output. We present SchemaFill, a framework for efficient LLM tool calling through slot-parallel speculative decoding. SchemaFill generates future slot values concurrently as candidates, without requiring advance knowledge of the actual call sequence or argument values. Candidates spanning multiple fields and calls are concatenated for verification by the target model under the actual output prefix. Only verified tokens are committed, and the target supplies corrections when candidates disagree. This applies target verification while exploiting parallelism across slots and calls. On Glaive and BFCL, SchemaFill achieves up to a 4.05$\times$ improvement in end-to-end throughput over autoregressive decoding. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [57] [Learning to Simulate Individuals from Macro Social Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07062)
*Yining Zhao,Bushi Liu,Haofei Yu,Zhengyang Qi,Shanyong Wang,Chuyue Li,Yuxiang Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: macro2mind从预测市场价格轨迹中学习社会行为推理，用GRPO训练语言模型进行群体级用户模拟，并在多个基准上取得有竞争力的零样本迁移效果，作为数据生成器还能提升下游模拟器准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的用户行为模拟能力要么依赖预训练继承，要么依赖个体级标注，行为多样性有限且缺乏对行为推理本身的监督。

Method: 未在摘要中给出完整端到端架构细节。已知输入为预测市场价格轨迹与相关新闻事件，输出为群体行为反应聚合后的价格预测。模型通过社会行为分解将预测过程分为：推断代表性市场参与者群体、预测每个群体如何解读新闻并更新信念、推理群体间交互、聚合响应形成价格。训练使用GRPO强化学习，以市场信号为奖励，并采用后见之明-遗憾课程与难度感知采样，重点训练后见之明识别群体能显著改善预测且当前策略仍可学习的过渡样本。学习到的行为推理可在不额外训练的情况下迁移到用户模拟任务。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "方向准确率与相关性", "value": "state-of-the-art", "setting": "SWM-Bench, Polymarket预测市场"}, {"metric": "零样本用户模拟性能", "value": "competitive among zero-shot methods", "setting": "Humanual, OvertonBench, PRISM, CAD四个用户模拟基准"}, {"metric": "下游模拟器对未见用户准确率提升", "value": "15.5 points", "setting": "macro2mind作为数据生成器"}, {"metric": "相比其backbone生成数据的提升", "value": "13.2 points", "setting": "下游模拟器未见用户准确率"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从预测市场（Polymarket）价格轨迹中学习宏观人群行为推理；社会行为分解：把行为推理显式建模为群体推断、新闻解读、信念更新、群体交互和价格聚合步骤；使用GRPO以市场信号为奖励进行训练；后见之明-遗憾课程与难度感知采样聚焦可学习过渡样本

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate how individuals respond to new situations, yet the behavioral reasoning behind these responses is either inherited from pretraining or learned from individual-level annotations, which offer limited behavioral diversity and little supervision of the reasoning itself. We propose to learn behavioral reasoning from prediction markets, whose price trajectories record how populations respond to real-world events at scale. We introduce macro2mind, which trains a language model with GRPO using market signals. A social behavioral decomposition makes behavioral reasoning an explicit step of forecasting: the model infers representative groups of market participants, predicts how each interprets the news and updates its beliefs, reasons about their interactions, and aggregates these responses into a price. A hindsight-regret curriculum with difficulty-aware sampling focuses training on transitions where hindsight-identified groups substantially improve the forecast while prioritizing examples that remain learnable for the current policy. The learned reasoning applies to user simulation without further training. On SWM-Bench, macro2mind achieves state-of-the-art directional accuracy and correlation on Polymarket. Trained on market data, it transfers zero-shot to four user-simulation benchmarks (Humanual, OvertonBench, PRISM, and CAD) and has competitive performance among zero-shot methods. Used as a data generator, macro2mind also raises a downstream simulator's accuracy on unseen users by 15.5 points, outperforming data generated by its backbone by 13.2 points.

</details>


### [58] [TRIAGE: Direction-Aware Mismatch Stabilization of Native NVFP4 Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07043)
*Zhen Li,Shuai Zhang,Yanggan Gu,Yiming Zhang,Yang Yu,Mingfa Feng,Congkai Xie,Shuang Yu,Junjie Lai,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对NVFP4低精度RL训练中learner和sampler执行不匹配导致策略梯度方向失稳的问题，提出方向感知的TRIAGE方法，通过片段级诊断重平衡更新并施加有界修复，在Qwen3-4B/30B-A3B上稳定优化并达到全精度性能，rollout吞吐较BF16提升最高2.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在原生NVFP4 W4A4低精度执行下，learner与sampler执行之间的数值不匹配会破坏策略梯度方向，导致策略优化不稳定；仅靠不匹配幅度无法识别哪些更新会被局部放大或收缩。

Method: TRIAGE在原生NVFP4 W4A4（4-bit权重与激活）前向执行下工作，sampler和learner均保持4-bit前向。方法首先进行片段级诊断，识别策略梯度更新中局部放大与收缩的区域，尤其关注负优势、负间隙更新的早期不平衡及其尾部token在响应片段中的集中。基于诊断结果，TRIAGE选择性地重平衡策略梯度更新，并对残余的严重不匹配施加有界修复。该过程修改优化目标，但不改变底层NVFP4前向执行路径，从而在稳定策略优化的同时保留低精度rollout的吞吐优势。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "rollout吞吐量", "value": "up to 2.3x higher than BF16", "setting": "native NVFP4 with TRIAGE on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-30B-A3B"}, {"metric": "数学推理基准性能", "value": "达到全精度水平（full precision level performance）", "setting": "五个数学推理基准，Qwen3-4B及Qwen3-30B-A3B，评估训练周期内"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 刻画了不匹配与策略梯度方向之间的相互作用，区分局部放大与收缩的更新贡献；发现NVFP4下早期正负优势/间隙更新之间的放大区域不平衡，尾部token在响应片段中集中；提出TRIAGE方向感知稳定化方法，通过片段级诊断选择性重平衡策略梯度更新；对残余严重不匹配应用有界修复，同时保持sampler和learner的原生NVFP4 W4A4前向执行

Abstract: Low-precision execution can substantially accelerate reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models, but discrepancies between learner and sampler execution can destabilize policy optimization. In this paper, we characterize the interaction between mismatch and the policy-gradient direction, distinguishing locally amplifying from contracting update contributions that mismatch magnitude alone cannot identify. In native NVFP4 runs, we observe an early imbalance between the two amplifying regions, favoring negative-advantage, negative-gap updates. Their tail tokens become concentrated in a small fraction of response segments before mismatch spreads globally. Motivated by these findings, we introduce TRIAGE, a direction-aware stabilization method that uses segment-level diagnosis to selectively rebalance policy-gradient updates and applies bounded repair to residual severe mismatch. TRIAGE modifies the optimization objective while retaining native NVFP4 weight-and activation 4-bit (W4A4) forward execution on both the sampler and learner. Experiments on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-30B-A3B show stable optimization throughout the evaluated training horizon and achieve full precision level performance across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, while native NVFP4 with TRIAGE provides up to 2.3x higher rollout throughput than BF16.

</details>


### [59] [DART-ES: Difficulty-Aware Reweighting and Targeted Replay for Fine-Tuning LLMs with Evolution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06993)
*Zhishen Sun,Hongzhan Wang,Sizhe Dang,Guang Dai,Haishan Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DART-ES 提出难度感知重加权与定向回放，改进进化策略在LLM全参数微调中的奖励聚合与数据分配。在五个基座模型、五个数学推理基准及指令/代码任务上平均准确率由72.07%提升至73.53%，超过GRPO的73.26%；相比GRPO，每步运行时间降低15.2%–50.2%，峰值显存降低21.1%–51.1%，且优于GRPO+LoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准进化策略（ES）在LLM全参数微调中均匀平均奖励，且将问题级种群反馈压缩为单一标量，无法捕捉每个问题的学习价值随模型能力的变化，导致扰动方向评估与训练数据分配低效。

Method: DART-ES 基于进化策略（ES）的全参数微调框架。核心流程：在每个训练步骤中，对模型参数施加一组扰动（perturbation population），对每个问题使用扰动后的模型进行前向计算得到奖励，无需反向传播。标准ES将每个问题的种群奖励均匀平均并压缩为单一标量来评估扰动方向。DART-ES 首先从每个问题在扰动种群中的通过率（pass rate）估计其局部可解性（local solvability），然后聚合历史观测构建一个动态难度状态（dynamic difficulty state）。该共享状态被用于两个目的：（1）连续难度重加权：根据问题的当前学习价值动态调整其在奖励聚合中的权重，使扰动方向评估更关注有学习价值的问题；（2）稀有可解样本回放（targeted replay）：优先回放那些在当前模型能力下尚可解但稀有的样本，以改善训练数据分配。整个机制不引入额外的难度模型，也不使用反向传播，仅依赖前向计算。训练/推理路径：训练时仅前向传播，通过种群扰动和奖励反馈更新参数；推理时直接使用微调后的模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "73.53%", "setting": "五个基座模型上的微调，对比ES的72.07%与GRPO的73.26%"}, {"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "49.20%", "setting": "五个高难度数学推理基准，对比ES的48.34%"}, {"metric": "每步运行时间降低", "value": "15.2%–50.2%", "setting": "与GRPO相比"}, {"metric": "每GPU峰值显存降低", "value": "21.1%–51.1%", "setting": "与GRPO相比"}, {"metric": "运行时间与显存", "value": "低于GRPO+LoRA", "setting": "全参数微调下，对比GRPO+LoRA"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出基于扰动种群通过率的局部可解性估计，聚合历史观测构建动态难度状态；利用共享难度状态同时引导连续难度重加权与稀有可解样本回放（targeted replay）；无需额外难度模型或反向传播，仅用前向计算实现难度感知的优化；在保持ES内存效率优势的同时，显著降低运行时间与显存占用（相比GRPO）

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) enable memory efficient full parameter fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) using only forward computation. However, standard ES uniformly averages rewards across problems and compresses problem level population feedback into a single scalar, making it difficult to capture how the learning value of each problem changes with model capability. To address this limitation, we propose Difficulty-Aware Reweighting and Targeted Replay for Evolution Strategies (DART-ES). DART-ES estimates the local solvability of each problem from its pass rate across the perturbation population and aggregates historical observations to construct a dynamic difficulty state. This shared state jointly guides continuous difficulty reweighting and rare solvable sample replay, thereby improving perturbation direction evaluation and training data allocation without introducing an additional difficulty model or backpropagation. Extensive experiments show that DART-ES achieves good fine-tuning performance. DART-ES outperforms ES on all five base models and improves the average accuracy from 72.07\% to 73.53\%, exceeding the 73.26\% achieved by GRPO on GSM8K. Across five challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, DART-ES achieves an average accuracy of 49.20\%, compared with 48.34\% for ES and remains competitive with strong 7B models trained with RL. Further experiments show consistent gains in instruction tuning, code generation and the Countdown task with a 14B model, demonstrating strong generalization across tasks and scalability to larger models. Beyond performance gains, DART-ES also shows clear advantages in system efficiency. It reduces runtime per step by 15.2\%--50.2\% and peak memory usage per GPU by 21.1\%--51.1\% compared with GRPO. Despite performing full parameter fine-tuning, DART-ES also requires less runtime and GPU memory than GRPO+LoRA.

</details>


### [60] [Learning to Decide, Not to Reason: Parameter-Efficient Decision Operators via Low-Rank Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06950)
*Ran Li,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种参数高效的决策算子，仅需23K~330K参数即可匹配或超越百万参数RL算子，将长推理链压缩为6个token的决策，并在5个任务、3个基座模型上验证了迁移性和组合性，揭示了初始化与梯度流对可训练性的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向冻结语言模型注入技能的方法需要百万级参数和强化学习流程，成本高昂，且难以训练。论文旨在以极低参数量和行为克隆训练高效决策算子，将长推理链压缩为极短决策，并保证性能不降。

Method: 论文提出一个System-1决策算子，通过行为克隆训练，注入到冻结的语言模型中。算子架构包含一个共享的低秩骨干网络和一个零初始化的输出投影。输入为语言模型的隐藏状态（来自晚期层），低秩骨干对隐藏状态进行低秩变换，输出投影将变换结果映射回模型隐藏空间，从而引导模型沿已有晚期层通路放大答案。训练目标为行为克隆：给定输入，算子学习输出一个6-token的短决策序列，而非完整推理链。推理时，算子可以热插拔，且多个技能算子可以线性组合（加法、插值）。梯度流探针表明，零初始化输出投影在第一步即获得梯度，避免了先前算子中两个大因子矩阵梯度为零的问题，这是可训练性的关键。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "参数量", "value": "默认算子330K", "setting": "匹配1.33M参数RL训练算子"}, {"metric": "决策长度", "value": "6 token", "setting": "将3,685-token deliberation压缩为6-token decision，无精度损失"}, {"metric": "参数量", "value": "23K (rank-4)", "setting": "为最强已发表技能算子的1/58"}, {"metric": "LiveMath表现", "value": "23/57点超越基座模型best-of-8采样", "setting": "非思维链压缩的直接证据"}, {"metric": "泛化性", "value": "跨5个任务、3个基座模型迁移", "setting": "OOD增益在训练后数月发布的LiveMath问题上持续"}, {"metric": "训练效率", "value": "行为克隆 vs 强化学习", "setting": "参数量降低约两个数量级"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出System-1决策算子，使用共享低秩骨干网络和零初始化输出投影，仅需23K~330K参数即可匹配或超越1.33M参数RL训练算子。；通过梯度流探针证明，零初始化输出投影在第一步即获得梯度，而先前算子的初始化导致两个大因子矩阵梯度为零，从优化角度解释了可训练性差异。；将技能组合视为近似线性算子，支持加法、插值和推理时热插拔。；通过logit-lens探针揭示算子沿模型已有晚期层通路放大答案，而非提前写入，因此并非简单的思维链压缩。

Abstract: Injecting skills into a frozen language model currently costs a million parameters and a reinforcement-learning pipeline. We introduce \method{}, a System-1 decision operator trained by behavior cloning that lowers this cost by roughly two orders of magnitude. The default operator uses 330K parameters to match a 1.33M-parameter operator trained with reinforcement learning, exceeds or achieve comparable performance, while collapsing 3,685-token deliberation into a 6-token decision with no loss in accuracy. A rank-4 variant with 23K parameters, 1/58 of the strongest published skill operator, suffices for SearchQA and near-suffices for LiveMath, where higher rank still helps; the same recipe transfers across five tasks and three backbones, with out-of-distribution gains persisting on LiveMath problems released months after training. The gap to prior work is trainability, and it is set jointly by initialization and architecture: the initialization of prior operators zeroes the gradient of both large factor matrices at the first optimization step, whereas our zero-initialized output projection inside a shared low-rank backbone receives a gradient immediately, which a gradient-flow probe confirms directly. The gain isn't chain-of-thought compression: 23 of 57 LiveMath points beat the base model's best-of-8 sampling, and a logit-lens probe shows the operator amplifies the answer along the model's existing late-layer pathway, not writing it earlier. Gains track the base model's headroom across 13 base--task pairs, and skills compose as approximately linear operators that can be added, interpolated, and hot-swapped at inference time. Code on this https URL .

</details>

### [61] [Confidence-Ordering Reversal under Contextual Priors in Neural Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08229)
*Xinyu Zhang,Sichao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现语音检索中上下文先验融合会导致置信度排序反转：初始排名靠后的错误修复后 margin 受限，而残余错误可能产生大 margin。通过因果干预验证，并提出分开读取局部和先验分数来改进选择性解码，在 MEG-MASC 上覆盖率达 74.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文先验通过加法浅融合重塑候选分数后，从融合分数读取的置信度会发生排序反转，导致错误预测的置信度不可靠，且准确率无法揭示该问题。

Method: 论文的端到端架构如下：输入为神经信号（MEG-MASC 和 MOUS 数据集）和候选词汇。首先，基于局部解码分数生成初始候选排名。然后，通过加性浅融合（additive shallow fusion）将上下文先验（如语言模型）与局部分数结合，得到融合后的候选分数。置信度定义为融合后 top-two 候选分数的 margin（差值）。在选择性解码阶段，分别读取局部分数和先验分数来估计置信度，而非仅依赖融合分数。训练目标为优化解码准确率。推理时，根据置信度决定是否输出答案，若输出则给出候选集合。关键模块包括局部解码器、上下文先验模型、分数融合模块和置信度估计模块。该设计旨在解决上下文先验导致置信度不可靠的问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "pooled correctness AUROC", "value": "0.87", "setting": "MEG-MASC 数据集，基于局部解码分数、加性浅融合上下文先验和融合 top-two margin 置信度"}, {"metric": "AUROC separating repairs from residual errors at initial ranks 2-3", "value": "0.70", "setting": "MEG-MASC 数据集"}, {"metric": "AUROC separating repairs from residual errors at initial ranks 21-50", "value": "0.39", "setting": "MEG-MASC 数据集"}, {"metric": "proportion of post-fusion errors starting beyond rank 20", "value": "46.6%", "setting": "MEG-MASC 数据集"}, {"metric": "decoder answers on windows", "value": "74.5%", "setting": "使用分开读取局部和先验分数的置信度估计，对比基线 56.7%"}, {"metric": "output sets containing correct candidate", "value": "92%", "setting": "同上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现置信度排序反转现象：初始排名靠前的候选修复可能性随 margin 增大而增加，而排名靠后的候选则相反。；提出分数级解释：修复需要弥补初始差距导致最终 margin 受限，而残余错误可能在两个错误候选间形成大 margin。；通过因果干预改变融合权重，验证反转现象随权重变化向更深排名移动。；提出分开读取局部和先验分数的置信度估计方法，提升选择性解码覆盖率（从 56.7% 到 74.5%），同时保持 92% 的输出包含正确候选。

Abstract: Contextual priors improve neural-to-language decoding by reshaping candidate scores. However, confidence is read from the same reshaped scores, so the errors a prior leaves behind can become more confident with no change in accuracy to reveal it. We study how a prior shapes confidence in speech retrieval on MEG-MASC and MOUS using local decoding scores, a contextual prior combined by additive shallow fusion, and the fused top-two margin as confidence. Among initially incorrect predictions, we find a confidence-ordering reversal: a larger margin makes a repair more likely when the correct candidate starts near the top of the local ranking, but less likely when it starts lower. On MEG-MASC, pooled correctness AUROC is 0.87, yet AUROC separating repairs from residual errors falls from 0.70 at initial ranks 2-3 to 0.39 at ranks 21-50. Errors starting beyond rank 20, inside the reversed region, make up 46.6% of all post-fusion errors. We propose a score-level account: a repair must first close the correct candidate's initial deficit, limiting its final margin, whereas a residual error can build a large margin between two incorrect candidates. A causal intervention that changes only the fusion weight moves the reversal to deeper ranks as predicted. Under a word-level LM prior, it keeps moving after accuracy gain peaks, so a weight chosen for accuracy does not settle confidence. Reading local and prior scores separately improves selective decoding: the decoder answers on 74.5% of windows instead of 56.7%, while 92% of output sets still contain the correct candidate. Confidence after contextual fusion should retain the local and contextual evidence behind each prediction, not just the fused scores. Project website: this https URL Code: this https URL

</details>


### [62] [Self-Retrospection Distillation: Turning Post-hoc Experiences into Prior Foresight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08077)
*Haoxiang Zhang,Qinglin Chen,Hiroaki Hayashi,Zhuofeng Li,Siming Zhang,Jiaxin Zhang,Jixuan Chen,Fang Wu,Pan Lu,Silvio Savarese,Julian McAuley,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出自回溯蒸馏SRD，用完整交互轨迹的后验知识监督策略在行动前的前瞻预测，在RLVR奖励对比消失时仍能提取学习信号；在10个智能体任务上最高带来24.2 pp增益，2B全失败场景下将成功率从0%提升到60.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）的群组相对目标下，当同一组rollout获得相同奖励（全成功或全失败）时标量奖励信号消失，宝贵轨迹经验无法转化为学习信号。

Method: SRD建立在RLVR与自蒸馏框架之上。输入是同一策略在环境中对任务产生的多个rollout轨迹；训练流程为：先让策略与工具环境交互得到完整轨迹，同时利用可验证奖励获得结果信号用于标准RLVR更新。在群组相对目标失效（组内奖励相同）的情况下，SRD额外引入一个前瞻分支：将已完成的完整轨迹（特权后验视角，包含成功的做法与失败的陷阱）作为教师信号，监督同一策略在只看到交互前任务描述（轨迹盲视角）时对未来过程与潜在失败点的预测。该前瞻预测只在训练阶段产生并用于计算蒸馏损失，与RLVR损失互补共同优化策略参数；推理阶段策略只按正常流程生成动作，不需要生成前瞻内容，因此不改变部署形态。这样设计的目的是把稀疏标量奖励无法表达的过程性信息，压缩进策略的先验预测表示中，从而在奖励对比缺失时仍能获得梯度信号。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务成功率提升", "value": "最高24.2 pp", "setting": "10个工具集成推理与长程智能体任务，相对于RLVR与自蒸馏基线"}, {"metric": "2B模型成功率", "value": "0.0% vs 60.6%", "setting": "98% rollout组为全失败、相同rollout预算下，RLVR vs RLVR+SRD"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 预测式学习（prospective learning）：用交互后的后验经验监督交互前视角的前瞻预测；自回溯蒸馏（SRD）：将完成轨迹中蕴含的特权后验知识蒸馏为同一策略的轨迹盲前瞻表示；前瞻仅作为训练目标、推理时无需额外生成，不增加推理开销；在奖励对比稀缺（奖励均匀组占比37%–98%）时仍可从采样轨迹中提取学习信号

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) turns agent experience into learning signals primarily through scalar outcome rewards after interaction. For group-relative objectives, however, this signal vanishes when all rollouts receive the same reward, even though their trajectories may reveal useful information about what the task requires and how the agent fails. We ask a complementary question: can hindsight teach an agent what it could have anticipated before acting? We introduce prospective learning, which uses post-hoc experience to supervise foresight predictions from the pre-interaction view, and instantiate it with Self-Retrospection Distillation (SRD). Intuitively, a completed trajectory reveals knowledge that would have been useful and pitfalls that should be avoided; SRD distills this privileged hindsight into trajectory-blind foresight of the same policy. Foresight serves only as a training target and need not be explicitly generated at inference time. Across 10 tool-integrated reasoning and long-horizon agentic tasks, SRD complements RLVR and self-distillation baselines with gains of up to $24.2$ pp. Its advantage is especially pronounced when reward contrast is scarce: when $37$--$98\%$ of rollout groups are reward-uniform across model scales, yet SRD can still exploit learning signal from sampled trajectories. In the 2B setting, where $98\%$ of groups are all-failure, the RLVR training ends up at $0.0\%$ success, while adding SRD reaches $60.6\%$ under the same rollout budget. Our results suggest that post-hoc agent experience is useful not only for evaluating or improving behavior, but also for shaping predictive representations before available interaction.

</details>


### [63] [Agent in a Bottle: Can LLM Agents Turn Their Capabilities Into Cheap, Scalable Artifacts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08775)
*Ankit Sonthalia,Haritz Puerto,Alexander Rubinstein,Martin Gubri,Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 BOTTLED 基准，考察 LLM Agent 能否在固定预算下把通用能力“装瓶”为可复用的廉价方案。实验显示零样本能力强不代表 bottling 能力强，但成功时成本可大幅降低，例如在查询-商品相关性分类上保留约 82% macro-F1 而成本约低 657 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定时间、算力与 LLM API 预算下，LLM Agent 能否把通用能力自主“装瓶”为面向特定大规模重复工作负载的廉价、可复用方案（如小模型或程序），并在答案质量与摊销成本之间取得平衡。

Method: BOTTLED 是一个评估 LLM Agent “bottling” 能力的基准框架。输入为一个完整未标注的任务工作负载（例如查询-商品相关性分类的批量查询），而非逐条查询；Agent 在固定时间、算力与 LLM API 预算约束下运行，自主决定并执行解决方案，可选项包括训练一个小模型或编写可复用程序。执行后，系统在相同任务上评估所产出方案的答案质量（如 macro-F1）与摊销成本，并与其零样本逐条调用大模型的结果以及小模型蒸馏基线、专用廉价推理模型 Jev 进行对比。其目标是衡量 Agent 是否能把通用能力转化为面向大规模重复工作负载的可复用制品，而非仅评估单次零样本任务准确率。摘要未披露 Agent 的具体内部模块、训练目标或推理服务细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "macro-F1 保留率与成本", "value": "Opus 5 在查询-商品相关性分类上保留约 82% 零样本 macro-F1，报告成本约低 657 倍", "setting": "BOTTLED 基准，查询-商品相关性分类任务，零样本性能为基线"}, {"metric": "macro-F1 与全工作负载成本", "value": "Opus 5 恢复约 94% Jev 的 macro-F1，成本约为 Jev 预估全工作负载成本的四分之一", "setting": "同一查询-商品相关性任务，与专为廉价重复推理构建的系统一模型 Jev 对比"}, {"metric": "低于零样本置信区间下限的运行数", "value": "60 次 bottling 运行中有 48 次低于其模型零样本性能 95% 置信区间下限", "setting": "十个模型、三个任务"}, {"metric": "低于蒸馏基线的运行数", "value": "60 次运行中有 31 次在相同 token 预算下低于两个小模型蒸馏基线中较强的那个", "setting": "十个模型、三个任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 BOTTLED 基准：给 Agent 整个未标注工作负载，要求其在固定时间、算力与 API 预算内自主选择方案（训练小模型或编写可复用程序）；定义并度量“bottling”能力：区分零样本任务能力与将能力转化为可复用方案的能力；揭示强零样本性能并不稳定转化为强 bottling 能力，并给出与蒸馏基线、Jev 系统一的成本-质量对比

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can solve many narrow tasks, but querying them separately for millions of related instances can be prohibitively expensive. Can LLM agents autonomously create cheaper solutions for such workloads? We call this ability "bottling": the ability to turn general capabilities into task-specific solutions that balance answer quality and amortised cost. We introduce BOTTLED, a benchmark in which agents receive an entire unlabelled workload and must complete it under fixed time, compute and LLM API budgets. Agents choose their own approach, such as training a small model or writing a reusable program. Across ten models and three tasks, we find that strong zero-shot task performance does not reliably translate into strong bottling capabilities. Models with similar zero-shot scores can differ substantially after bottling, and 48 of 60 bottling runs score below the lower bound of the 95% confidence interval of their model's zero-shot performance. Moreover, 31 of 60 runs underperform the stronger of two small-model distillation baselines with the same token budget. Nevertheless, bottling can yield substantial savings: on query-product relevance classification, Opus 5 retains about 82% of its zero-shot macro-F1 at roughly 657 times lower reported cost. Bottling is also competitive with Jev, a "system one" model built especially for cheap, repetitive inference: Opus 5 on the same task recovers about 94% of Jev's macro-F1 at a quarter of Jev's projected full-workload cost. BOTTLED provides a basis for evaluating and improving agents' ability to invest limited resources in reusable solutions for large, repetitive workloads.

</details>


### [64] [Sherpa: Teaching LLMs to Teach Adaptively](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08778)
*Weixian Xu,Yanzhe Zhang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Changyu Chen,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Sherpa 提出多轮强化学习框架训练 LLM 教师适应不同学生原型，平均提升学生学习表现 20.5 个百分点，MathTutorBench 教学法分数从 52.5% 升至 79.2%，人工偏好胜率 79.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何训练 LLM 教师根据个体学生的学习偏好自适应地调整教学策略，以直接最大化学生的学习效果，而非依赖演示、偏好数据或预定义教学标准。

Method: Sherpa 是一个多轮强化学习框架。输入为教学对话上下文，包含教师模型生成的指令和学生模型的反馈。系统实例化多个学生原型，每个原型由 LLM 根据不同的学习偏好条件化生成，以模拟多样化的学习者。教师模型在多轮交互中生成教学回复，学生原型基于自身学习偏好对教师回复进行响应并产生学习成果信号。训练目标是通过强化学习直接最大化模拟学生的学习成果，从而优化教师模型的自适应教学策略。具体 RL 算法、奖励函数细节未在摘要中披露。推理时，训练后的教师模型根据学生特征调整教学。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "学生表现平均提升", "value": "20.5 百分点", "setting": "所有学生原型，训练后的教师模型指导下的模拟学生"}, {"metric": "MathTutorBench 总体教学法分数", "value": "从 52.5% 提升至 79.2%", "setting": "MathTutorBench 评估"}, {"metric": "人工 pairwise 偏好胜率", "value": "79.6%", "setting": "人工研究，训练教师 vs 基础模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Sherpa 多轮强化学习框架，实例化多个具有不同学习偏好的学生原型，并用 LLM 模拟学生行为；教师模型通过直接最大化模拟学生的学习成果来优化教学策略；在 MathTutorBench 上评估教学法分数提升，并进行人工 pairwise 偏好研究

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become increasingly capable problem solvers, but being able to solve a problem is not the same as being able to teach it. Existing approaches to training LLMs as teachers rely on demonstrations, preference data, or predefined pedagogical criteria that specify what good teaching looks like. However, these signals are often not grounded in individual student learning outcomes, where effective teaching strategies can vary substantially across learners. To address this, we introduce Sherpa, a multi-turn reinforcement learning framework that instantiates multiple student archetypes with LLMs conditioned on distinct learning preferences and trains a teacher model to adapt its instruction by directly maximizing their learning outcomes. Teacher LLMs trained with Sherpa improve instructed students' performance across all archetypes by an average of 20.5 percentage points. Under MathTutorBench's evaluation, Sherpa raises the overall pedagogy score from 52.5% to 79.2%, indicating better teaching responses. Our human studies show that the trained teacher is preferred over the base model in 79.6% of pairwise comparisons. Together, Sherpa trains LLM teachers to adapt to diverse simulated students and become better aligned with human teachers, paving the road towards AI tutors teaching real students.

</details>


### [65] [SquidAgent: Parallelize Wisely, Coordinate Efficiently](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08647)
*Yexiong Lin,Shanshan Ye,Yu Yao,Zhen Fang,Bo Han,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对并行多智能体系统常比串行更慢的问题，论文识别出再探索成本与对齐成本，提出以预测输出token为度量、逐层判断是否并行化的准则，并据此构建SquidAgent：单步估算token预算、worker从orchestrator会话分叉、预生成共享约定块、确定性调度器逐层决策，相对Claude Code取得2.2×吞吐与2.6×墙钟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行多智能体系统由于再探索成本与对齐成本两个隐性开销，实际往往比单智能体串行执行更慢，需要一种判断何时并行、如何并行以真正获得加速的准则。

Method: SquidAgent以LLM orchestrator为中心：首先在单一规划步骤中估算所有任务的token预算，据此获得各层的成本估计；由于LLM对墙钟时间估计不校准，系统改用预测输出token数作为成本代理。调度时确定性调度器逐层应用准则：仅当某层的关键路径成本加上再探索与对齐开销低于串行成本时才并行化该层。并行执行时，每个worker直接从orchestrator的会话状态分叉（fork），继承上下文以避免重复重建，从而消除再探索成本。对齐方面，系统预先生成一个共享约定块并分发给所有worker，使独立产出之间的一致性约束在前置阶段以有界成本完成，避免事后昂贵的reconciliation。最终worker输出返回orchestrator汇总。论文未披露具体模型规模、训练数据或推理硬件细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "throughput improvement", "value": "2.2× mean", "setting": "相对Claude Code"}, {"metric": "wall-time speedup", "value": "2.6× mean", "setting": "相对Claude Code"}, {"metric": "throughput improvement", "value": "2.0×", "setting": "相对最强多智能体基线"}]}

Conclusion: 提出基于预测输出token数（而非墙钟时间）的成本准则，判断某一层是否应并行化；fork机制：每个worker直接从orchestrator会话分叉，消除再探索成本；预生成共享约定块（shared convention block）将事后对齐转为有界的前置成本；确定性调度器逐层应用该准则进行并行决策

Abstract: LLM-based agents solve complex multi-step tasks, but sequential execution incurs substantial latency. In principle, parallelizing work across multiple agents should yield near-linear speedups. Yet existing parallel multi-agent systems often run slower than a single-agent baseline. We attribute this gap to two hidden costs that parallel execution incurs but a serial agent avoids. First, there is a re-exploration cost: redundant effort spent by parallel workers reconstructing context that the orchestrator already possesses, such as prior decisions, that would otherwise be inherited implicitly in a serial execution. Second, there is an alignment cost: the overhead required to reconcile inconsistencies across independently generated outputs. We thus derive a principled decision criterion: a layer should be parallelized only when its critical-path cost, plus re-exploration and alignment overheads, is lower than the corresponding serial cost. While this criterion is naturally expressed in wall-clock time, we observe that LLMs are poorly calibrated when asked to estimate task duration. To address this, we instead measure cost in predicted output tokens, which we empirically find LLMs can estimate substantially more reliably than wall-clock time. Building on this token-based criterion, we propose SquidAgent. It estimates all token budgets in a single planning step, forks each worker directly from the orchestrator's session to eliminate re-exploration cost, and replaces post-hoc reconciliation with a pre-generated shared convention block that converts alignment into a bounded upfront cost. A deterministic scheduler then applies the criterion layer by layer. Empirically, SquidAgent achieves a 2.2$\times$ mean throughput improvement and a 2.6$\times$ mean wall-time speedup over Claude Code, and a 2.0$\times$ throughput improvement over the strongest multi-agent baseline.

</details>


### [66] [Adaptive Power Sampling for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08563)
*Bingnan Xiao,Chenhao Yang,Bingcong Li,Wei Ni,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出自适应幂采样（APS），通过理论分析自奖励差距，在测试时逐查询调整锐化指数，无需训练即可提升 LLM 在 MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA 等推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的序列级幂采样方法对所有查询使用统一的锐化指数，忽略了查询难度和基模型自身处理能力的差异，导致推理效果次优。

Method: APS 在测试时对基座 LLM 的输出分布进行自适应幂采样。输入为查询，模型首先通过采样生成一组候选响应，并计算答案一致性以及模型对每个响应的自奖励。基于自奖励差距的理论分析，APS 动态调整每个查询的锐化指数，使得在模型本身表现较好的查询上不过度锐化，在困难查询上适当增加锐化。最终从调整后的锐化分布中采样以获得答案，整个过程无需训练，仅增加测试时计算。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推理准确率", "value": "持续优于固定锐化指数的幂采样", "setting": "MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA等推理基准测试"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从理论上揭示了进一步锐化的收益取决于正确与错误响应之间的自奖励差距；提出自适应幂采样（APS），在测试时根据答案一致性与模型自奖励的关系，逐查询调整锐化指数；无需额外训练即可在MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA等推理任务上超越固定指数的幂采样

Abstract: Sequence-level power sampling has recently emerged as a training-free approach to reasoning by sampling from a sharpened output distribution of a base large language model (LLM). Nevertheless, existing methods typically sharpen the base model distribution uniformly across queries, overlooking variations in query difficulty and in how well the base model already handles each query. The goal of this work is to equip power sampling with query adaptivity. Theoretically, we show that the benefits of further sharpening are determined by the self-reward gap between correct and incorrect responses. Based on this insight, we propose \emph{Adaptive Power Sampling} (APS), which adjusts the sharpening exponent on a per-query basis at test time using the relationship between answer agreement and the model's self-reward. Experiments across diverse reasoning tasks, including MATH500, HumanEval, and GPQA, show that APS consistently outperforms power sampling with a fixed sharpening exponent, without additional training.

</details>


### [67] [How Much Evidence Should a Coding Agent's Self-Correction Carry? Adaptive Dirichlet Evidence for Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08514)
*Yunbo Long,Guangya Hao,Yuhan Liu,Yiting Duan,Longyan Tan,Yunchen Long,Hao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EESD 用 Dirichlet 证据框架为编码智能体自校正样本分配不确定性惩罚权重，在代码任务上提升 Pass@1 并降低 NLL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何为编码智能体基于执行反馈的自校正样本分配合理的学习权重，使其反映执行支持程度与证据量。

Method: EESD 将执行反馈驱动的自校正学习形式化为证据加权问题。输入为编码智能体执行轨迹产生的过渡样本，每个样本带有归一化执行相关性（决定相对过渡支持）和有效伪计数质量（决定证据量）。二者分别建模后，通过 Dirichlet 后验产生不确定性惩罚权重，用于 KL 锚定的校正学习目标：以固定模型为锚，用该权重调节对自生成校正的蒸馏强度。理论上，对称先验下改变质量保持类别排序不变，有效质量给出的监督系数不超过其匹配的固定质量对应值。推理与服务路径未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "future-outcome NLL", "value": "降低 55.0-59.3%", "setting": "四个模型-领域历史扫描，可见观测从 1 增至 8"}, {"metric": "NLL", "value": "有效质量低于固定质量（4/4 比较）", "setting": "八个观测下"}, {"metric": "all-tests Pass@1", "value": "15.0% -> 20.4%", "setting": "DeepSeek/CodeARC，一轮校正学习后，配对 95% 源 bootstrap 区间 [+2.8, +8.0] 个百分点"}, {"metric": "argmax 预测一致", "value": "3,000 示例全部一致", "setting": "DeepSeek/RunBugRun 匹配研究"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将执行相关性与有效伪计数质量分离表示；使用 Dirichlet 后验产生不确定性惩罚权重；KL 锚定的自蒸馏校正学习框架

Abstract: Execution feedback lets coding agents revise programs and learn from their own corrections. A correction's learning weight should reflect both the transitions supported by its executions and the amount of evidence behind that support. We introduce Effective-Evidence Self-Distillation (EESD), which represents these quantities separately. Normalized execution relevance determines relative transition support and an effective pseudo-count mass; a Dirichlet posterior then produces an uncertainty-penalized weight for KL-anchored correction learning. Under a symmetric prior, changing mass preserves category ordering, and effective mass yields a supervised coefficient bounded by its matched fixed-mass counterpart. Across four model-domain history sweeps, increasing visible observations from one to eight reduces future-outcome NLL by 55.0-59.3%. At eight observations, effective mass achieves lower NLL than fixed mass in all four comparisons. In the primary matched DeepSeek/RunBugRun study, argmax predictions agree on all 3,000 examples, with the largest NLL gain under concentrated relevance. After one correction-learning round, DeepSeek/CodeARC all-tests Pass@1 increases from 15.0% to 20.4%, with a paired 95% source-bootstrap interval of [+2.8, +8.0] percentage points. The twelve-setting downstream evaluation establishes the model-domain scope of this update. These results show how separating evidence support from evidence mass changes probability estimation and correction learning in coding agents.

</details>


### [68] [Transect: Retaining Observability for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08364)
*Toby D. Pilditch,Konstantinos Voudouris,Alexandra Abbas,Cozmin Ududec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Transect 是一个基于 Inspect Scout 的开源 LLM agent 长程评测可观测性工具：通过可复用的评测族配置定义任务上下文与行为词表，将事件、token 用量、子 agent 活动与模型生成的行为标签对齐到同一 turn 时间线，并支持溯源与导出表格做跨 run 分析。文中展示了一个约 1300 万 token 的 AI R&D 评测案例，发现智能体主要投入在执行性工作和论文撰写，而缺少持续假设生成阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-horizon, multi-agent LLM evaluations produce transcripts that can span hundreds of pages, narrowing the observability envelope; LLM-assisted classification increases human analytical degrees of freedom and threatens reproducibility and auditability of transcript analysis.

Method: Transect is an open-source transcript-analysis pipeline built on Inspect Scout. Inputs are long agent-run transcripts from open-ended, agentic, multi-agent evaluations. Users first define a reusable evaluation-family configuration containing task context and a behavioural vocabulary; judge models and analysis settings are supplied separately, keeping the interpretation schema decoupled from the specific judge implementation. The pipeline processes the run and produces navigable reports in which recorded events, token use, sub-agent activity, and model-generated behavioural labels are aligned on a common turn-based timeline. Reviewers use this combined view to reconstruct a run's narrative, inspect where work time and tokens were spent, trace any behavioural label or event back to its source turns for verification, and export the underlying data tables for cross-run analysis. The abstract describes a demonstration on an AI R&D evaluation of almost 13 million tokens, where the agents' work was divided into behavioural phases aligned with research-skill classifications, sub-agent delegations and interactions, and token use; the combined view showed a focus on operational work and manuscript production with little evidence of a sustained hypothesis generation stage. No training objectives, parameter scales, hardware, latency, throughput, memory, or cost figures are disclosed.

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Reusable evaluation-family configuration separating task context and behavioural vocabulary from judge models and analysis settings；Navigable reports that align recorded events, token use, sub-agent activity and model-generated behavioural labels on a common turn-based timeline；Source-turn tracing for every behavioral label or event to support interpretation checking and auditability；Exportable underlying data tables for cross-run analysis；Open-source implementation built on Inspect Scout

Abstract: Frontier AI evaluations increasingly use open-ended, agentic, long-horizon tasks whose transcripts can span hundreds of pages of outputs and actions from complex multi-agent networks. The observability envelop-the range of what evaluators can reliably infer about an agent's behaviours-is therefore narrowing. Language model assistants can help classify and interpret agent behaviour but also afford human evaluators significant analytical degrees of freedom, threatening the reproducibility and auditability of language-model-based transcript analysis. Transect is an open source package built on Inspect Scout to help evaluators understand how a long agent run unfolded, identify behaviour worth investigating, and check interpretations against the transcript. Users specify task context and behavioural vocabulary in a reusable evaluation-family configuration, with judge models and analysis settings supplied separately. Transect's navigable reports align recorded events, token use, sub-agent activity, and model-generated behavioural labels on a common turn-based timeline. Reviewers can quickly grasp a run's narrative, trace any label or event to its source turns, and export the underlying data tables for cross-run analysis. We demonstrate the workflow on an AI R&D evaluation that generated almost 13 million tokens, dividing the agents' work into behavioural phases aligned with research-skill classifications, sub-agent delegations and interactions, and token use. The combined view shows a focus on operational work and manuscript production, with little evidence of a sustained hypothesis generation stage-arguably a necessary component for high-quality scientific outputs. Transect's flexible, customisable transcript-analysis pipeline will enable evaluators to keep pace with longer, more complex, more frequent AI evaluations while supporting scientific rigour, transparency, and reproducibility.

</details>


### [69] [OSFP4: Joint Optimization of Diagonal Smoothing and Block Scales for NVFP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08231)
*Neriah Ben David,Ori Meir,Or Ordentlich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OSFP4 通过优化对角平滑矩阵与块缩放因子的联合方案，在保持 NVFP4 推理高吞吐的同时提升量化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 NVFP4 格式下保持 LLM 推理精度，同时维持接近厂商实现的高吞吐率。

Method: OSFP4 是一种针对 NVFP4 数据类型的量化方案，用于 LLM 推理。对于每一个线性投影层，该方案引入一个对角平滑矩阵，其对角线元素被优化以最小化在 NVFP4 格式下矩阵乘法的量化误差。优化过程同时考虑平滑矩阵条目和块缩放（block scales）的联合优化，并针对实际使用的舍入过程（舍入到最近或 GPTQ 式连续干扰消除）进行建模。为了便于联合优化，作者分析了一种乘性抖动 FP4 量化器代替固定的确定性量化器。实验表明，OSFP4 在相应量化设置下取得了最高的平均精度，同时保留了约 94-97% 的厂商 NVFP4 预填充吞吐量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均精度", "value": "在评估的竞品中最高", "setting": "对应量化设置下的精度对比"}, {"metric": "预填充吞吐量", "value": "约 94-97% 的厂商 NVFP4 吞吐量", "setting": "实测工作负载"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 针对每个线性投影引入对角平滑矩阵，并对角线元素进行优化以最小化 NVFP4 下的矩阵乘积量化误差。；将平滑参数与块缩放因子进行联合优化，并考虑舍入过程（舍入到最近或 GPTQ 式连续干扰消除）。；通过分析乘性抖动 FP4 量化器来简化联合优化问题。

Abstract: NVFP4 is an attractive datatype for large language model (LLM) inference, offering compact storage and native tensor-core acceleration. However, preserving accuracy using NVFP4 requires careful quantization. In this work we develop a novel quantization scheme called Optimized Smoothing and Scaling for NVFP4 (OSFP4). For each linear projection it uses a diagonal smoothing matrix whose entries are optimized to minimize the squared matrix-product quantization error under NVFP4, taking into account the rounding procedure that is used (either round-to-nearest, or GPTQ-style successive interference cancellation). This requires performing joint optimization on the smoothing entries as well as the block scales, which is facilitated by analyzing a multiplicative-dither FP4 quantizer instead of the fixed deterministic one. Experiments show that OSFP4 achieves the highest average accuracy among the evaluated competitors in the corresponding quantization settings, while retaining approximately 94-97\% of vendor NVFP4 prefill throughput on the measured workloads. Our code is available in this https URL

</details>


### [70] [Learn2Play Bench: How Well Do LLM Agents Learn from Experience in Unfamiliar Environments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08215)
*Yibo Li,Jinhang Qiu,Zhi Zheng,Qianyun Guo,Jiaying Wu,Shuo Ji,Bryan Hooi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Learn2Play Bench 是一个评估 LLM 智能体在陌生动态环境中从交互经验学习能力的基准，采用规则新颖或反直觉的文本游戏，提供可复现反馈与自动评分，并通过变化实例测试泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以区分 LLM 智能体是在真正从交互经验中学习，还是仅凭预训练知识或指令中给出的规则进行推理。

Method: 该基准由一组新设计的文本游戏组成，其规则是新颖或反直觉的，要求智能体通过与环境交互来学习。环境为每次尝试提供可复现的反馈，并自动评分，从而在重复尝试中控制变量地评估学习效果。游戏实例会发生变化，以测试智能体能否将所学知识泛化到新情境。论文评估了骨干模型、自我进化方法和智能体 harness 对学习能力的影响，并报告了三个发现：保留完整的行动与反馈记录比将其总结为规则或策略更能支持有效学习；顶尖人类玩家的峰值分数高于被评估的智能体，人类探索更多样化的策略且重复动作更少；在固定骨干模型的情况下，改变 harness 可以提高性能并降低估计的推理成本。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 设计全新的文本游戏规则，这些规则新颖或反直觉，迫使智能体必须通过交互获取知识。；提供可复现的反馈和自动评分，支持在重复尝试中控制变量地评估学习效果。；通过变化游戏实例，测试智能体能否将所学知识迁移到新情境。

Abstract: Learning from experience is essential for LLM agents to adapt to unfamiliar and dynmaic environments. Evaluating this ability is therefore important for understanding how effectively agents acquire and use new knowledge. Existing benchmarks have sought to evaluate this ability, but they primarily evaluate tasks whose rules are provided in the instructions or already familiar to pretrained models, making it difficult to distinguish learning from interactions from reasoning with existing knowledge. To address this, we introduce Learn2Play Bench, a benchmark of newly designed text-based games, whose rules are novel or counterintuitive, requiring agents to acquire knowledge through interaction rather than rely solely on pretrained knowledge. These games provide reproducible feedback and automatic scoring, enabling controlled evaluation of learning across repeated attempts. We also vary game instances to test whether agents can apply what they have learned to new situations. Therefore, we evaluate how backbone models, self-evolving methods, and agent harnesses affect agents' learning ability, revealing three findings: (1) Experience retention: Retaining complete records of actions and feedback can support more effective learning than summarizing these experiences into rules or strategies. (2) Human agent gap: Top-performing human players achieve higher peak scores than the evaluated agents. Human explore more varied strategies, and repeat actions less. (3) Harness matters: With the backbone fixed, changing the harness can improve performance while reducing estimated inference cost. Together, these findings provide insights into how LLM agents learn from experience and suggest directions for future work to improve their learning ability. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [71] [Test-Time Agent Evolution for Long-Horizon Legal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08138)
*Haotian Chen,Shuaicheng Niu,Haocong Rao,Kaisong Song,Jun Lin,Lizhen Cui,Zhiqi Shen,Yonghui Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A training-free test-time agent adaptation method for long-horizon legal reasoning. It couples Test-Time Memory Evolution, which retrieves, adapts, and consolidates experience from prior cases, with Rubric-Aligned Collaboration, which verifies and revises role-specific actions for cross-role and cross-stage coordination. Experiments on J1-EVAL and LegalWorld across five backbone models show consistent gains over reasoning and agent baselines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Static agent strategies fail under the case heterogeneity and cross-role, cross-stage interdependence of real-world long-horizon legal processes; the paper studies training-free test-time adaptation so agents can exploit deployment-time signals without updating model parameters.

Method: The paper proposes a training-free test-time agent adaptation framework for long-horizon legal reasoning. Inputs are evolving case states and multi-role procedural contexts. The first component, Test-Time Memory Evolution, retrieves reusable experience from previous cases, adapts it to the current factual and procedural context, and consolidates accumulated experience for subsequent decision-making. The second component, Rubric-Aligned Collaboration, verifies and revises role-specific actions according to behavioral and procedural requirements, enabling coordinated decision-making across roles and stages. Both components operate at inference time without updating model parameters. The abstract reports extensive experiments on J1-EVAL and LegalWorld across five backbone models showing consistent improvements over representative reasoning and agent baselines with reasonable interaction and computational costs; ablation and case studies indicate the two components provide complementary benefits.

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Test-Time Memory Evolution: retrieve reusable experience from preceding cases, adapt it to the current factual and procedural context, and consolidate accumulated experience for subsequent decisions.；Rubric-Aligned Collaboration: verify and revise role-specific actions against behavioral and procedural requirements to coordinate decisions across roles and stages.；A training-free test-time agent adaptation framework that avoids parameter updates while adapting to deployment-time signals.

Abstract: Legal intelligence aims to support reliable decision-making across long-horizon legal processes involving evolving case states and multiple roles. However, real-world legal deployment exhibits substantial case heterogeneity in facts, evidence, and procedural contexts, exposing the limitations of static agent strategies. Moreover, legal reasoning is inherently interdependent across roles and procedural stages, making global reliability fundamentally different from isolated role competence. To address these challenges, we study training-free test-time agent adaptation, where agents continuously exploit deployment-time signals from preceding cases and ongoing interactions without updating model parameters. We propose \method, which introduces \emph{Test-Time Memory Evolution} to retrieve reusable experience from previous cases, adapt it to the current factual and procedural context, and consolidate accumulated experience for subsequent decision-making. Further, \emph{Rubric-Aligned Collaboration} verifies and revises role-specific actions according to behavioral and procedural requirements, enabling coordinated decision-making across roles and stages. Extensive experiments on J1-EVAL and LegalWorld across five backbone models demonstrate consistent improvements over representative reasoning and agent baselines with reasonable interaction and computational costs. Ablation and case studies further show that the two components provide complementary benefits in experience adaptation and cross-role coordination, improving the reliability and efficiency of long-horizon legal reasoning.

</details>


### [72] [DAEDALUS: Bootstrapping Agent Memory from Self-Generated Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08048)
*Antoine Edy,Max Conti,Victor Xing,Marc-Antoine Allard,Nawfal Benhamdane,Gautier Viaud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DAEDALUS bootstraps reusable agent memory from self-generated tasks without human-written guidelines or oracle verifiers. It pairs an explorer that creates challenging but solvable tasks with a solver that attempts them; heuristics derived from solver failures are accepted only after repeated success with the heuristic in context, and accepted heuristics form a test-time memory bank. On AppWorld, τ²-bench, and AutomationBench it improves mean success by up to 15.9 points and pass^5 by up to 2.2x over a no-memory baseline, is competitive with training-task methods at lower inference cost, and transfers across model families.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agents operating in new environments lack operational knowledge about tool behaviors and environment conventions, repeat mistakes across tasks, and existing solutions require human-written guidelines, training tasks, or oracle verifiers that assume prior environment knowledge.

Method: DAEDALUS bootstraps reusable agent memory without pre-existing tasks or oracle verifiers. It pairs two agents: an explorer and a solver, both interacting with the same target environment. Inputs are the environment, current memory bank (initially empty), and solver outcomes. The explorer generates tasks intended to be challenging yet solvable. The solver attempts each generated task; when it fails, a heuristic is derived from the failure. A candidate heuristic is not immediately stored: it is only accepted after the solver repeatedly succeeds with that heuristic provided in context. The solver's success/failure outcomes also serve as feedback to the explorer, which refines the difficulty of future generated tasks. Accepted heuristics are consolidated into a memory bank. At test time, the memory bank is available for use by agents solving benchmark tasks. The paper further shows that solver traces provide the key information needed for deriving effective heuristics, and that factorizing early discoveries makes exploration more cost-efficient. Generated tasks can also act as a proxy benchmark for ranking model performance.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean success rate", "value": "up to +15.9 points", "setting": "over a no-memory baseline across AppWorld, τ²-bench, and AutomationBench"}, {"metric": "pass^5", "value": "up to 2.2x", "setting": "over a no-memory baseline across AppWorld, τ²-bench, and AutomationBench"}, {"metric": "performance gains", "value": "emerge with a small exploration budget", "setting": "exploration budget ablation"}, {"metric": "cross-model transfer", "value": "heuristics benefit agents from other model families", "setting": "cross-family evaluation"}, {"metric": "ablation", "value": "solver traces are key for deriving effective heuristics; factorizing early discoveries improves exploration cost-efficiency", "setting": "component ablations"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Bootstrapping reusable agent memory without existing tasks or oracle verifiers by pairing a task-generating explorer with a solver.；Deriving heuristics from solver failures and accepting a heuristic only after the solver repeatedly succeeds with that heuristic in context.；Using solver outcomes as feedback for the explorer to adapt task difficulty.；Consolidating accepted heuristics into a memory bank for test-time use.；Showing generated tasks can serve as a proxy for benchmark tasks when ranking models.

Abstract: LLM agents often lack the operational knowledge to act reliably in new environments, as they must discover specific tool behaviors or environment conventions on their own. Without memory of past attempts, they repeat the same mistakes across tasks, leading to more task failures and longer trajectories. To address this, agentic systems typically rely on human-written guidelines or on procedural memory built from training tasks and an oracle verifier, both of which require prior knowledge of the environment. We present DAEDALUS, a method for bootstrapping reusable agent memory from self-generated practice without existing tasks or oracle verifiers. DAEDALUS pairs two agents: an explorer that interacts with the environment to generate challenging yet solvable tasks, and a solver that attempts them. A heuristic is derived from each solver failure and accepted only after the solver repeatedly succeeds with that heuristic in context. These outcomes also provide feedback for the explorer to refine the difficulty of future tasks. Accepted heuristics are then consolidated into a memory bank for test-time use. Across AppWorld, $\tau^2$-bench, and AutomationBench, DAEDALUS improves mean success rates by up to 15.9 points and pass^5 by up to 2.2x over a no-memory baseline, and is competitive with methods using training tasks, at a lower inference cost than most. We show that performance gains already emerge with a small exploration budget, and that its heuristics also benefit agents from other model families. Our ablations further reveal that solver traces provide the key information needed to derive effective heuristics, while factorizing early discoveries makes exploration more cost-efficient. Beyond memory construction, we find that the tasks generated by DAEDALUS can serve as a proxy for benchmark tasks when ranking models by performance. Code and artifacts: this http URL .

</details>


### [73] [Continuous Memory Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07907)
*Ciaran Regan,Kai Arulkumaran,Luke Darlow,Stefania Druga,Sebastian Risi,Llion Jones*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CMM 是一种新型循环架构，通过分离矩阵值的短期和长期记忆状态，并结合 Transformer 进行双向读写，在多种任务上超越基线并保持可解释性，为记忆增强网络提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环神经网络将短期计算和长期保留压缩到单一向量状态中，缺乏生物学中同时具备快速神经元级处理和长期记忆保留的机制。

Method: CMM 是一种循环架构，包含矩阵值的短期记忆和长期记忆两个状态。短期记忆追踪近期神经活动，每个神经元具有独立参数化的模型，学习使用这些活动模式进行计算。长期记忆持久存储信息以供后续使用。一个 Transformer 模块联合更新两个记忆存储，提供表达性的双向读写机制，允许每个存储重组自身内容并相互读写。在算法、上下文学习和循环推理任务上，CMM 超越了多个基线，比之前的记忆增强网络具有更强的泛化能力，同时保留了 CTM 的可解释注意力模式。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出矩阵值的短期和长期记忆状态，分别承担近期神经活动追踪和持久信息存储的不同功能角色；基于 Continuous Thought Machine (CTM) 引入参数化的神经元级模型，学习利用短期活动模式进行计算；采用 Transformer 联合更新两个记忆存储，实现双向读写机制，使得两个存储可以重组自身内容并相互读写

Abstract: Recurrent neural networks typically compress information into a single vector-valued recurrent state, forcing short-term computation and long-term retention to share the same representation. Past extensions alleviate this bottleneck by increasing the memory capacity or separating timescales, but lack the combination of rapid neuron-level processing and longer-term retention found in biology. To that end, we introduce the Continuous Memory Machine (CMM), a recurrent architecture with matrix-valued short- and long-term memory states serving distinct functional roles. Building on the Continuous Thought Machine (CTM), the CMM's short-term memory tracks recent neural activity, with uniquely parameterized neuron-level models learning to use these activity patterns for computation. A persistent long-term memory stores information for later use, with a Transformer jointly updating both memory stores, providing an expressive bidirectional read--write mechanism such that each store can reorganize its own contents and both read from and write to the other. Across algorithmic, in-context learning, and recurrent reasoning tasks, the CMM outperforms a broad suite of baselines, exhibiting stronger generalization than prior memory-augmented networks while preserving the CTM's interpretable attention patterns. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [74] [WorkflowOps: Learning Agent Collaboration Priors for Multi-Agent Workflow Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07860)
*Qi Cheng,Shengyu Chen,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xiaowei Jia,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorkflowOps 提出学习智能体协作先验的多智能体工作流编排框架：用转移概率矩阵指导DAG构建与并行优化，按需生成新智能体并即时注入协作矩阵，并用分层语义匹配把LLM路由调用降低80%以上。在代码、数学与问答混合任务上端到端通过率优于近期工作流构建基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体编排层基本无记忆：每个新任务都从零开始分解、分配和执行，无法复用历史成功工作流中的智能体协作模式，导致编排低效且新能力需求无法快速覆盖。

Method: WorkflowOps 是面向多智能体工作流编排的框架，整体流程围绕历史工作流学习到的协作先验展开。输入为任务描述与历史工作流记录；编排层首先用转移概率矩阵刻画历史工作流中智能体两两协作的频率，该矩阵作为软引导参与DAG工作流构建：通过层内排序优化确定同一层内智能体的顺序，通过概率阈值化边建议补充候选协作边，再通过传递归约去除冗余边以最大化可并行执行度。其次，系统维护一个充分性驱动的智能体创建循环：当能力缺口检测模块通过语义匹配分数发现现有智能体池无法覆盖任务所需能力时，调用LLM生成新的专用智能体，并同步把新智能体及其预测协作关系注入转移概率矩阵，使其在后续工作流中可被立即调度。第三，能力匹配采用分层语义匹配策略：第一层用预训练句向量做快速、确定性的语义匹配，仅当匹配置信度低时才进入第二层调用LLM进行验证与裁决，从而在保持匹配质量的同时把LLM路由调用量相比纯LLM方案降低80%以上。训练或优化方面，论文主要依赖历史工作流统计与LLM生成，而非端到端梯度训练；推理或服务路径为：任务进入后先做能力语义匹配与缺口检测，按需创建智能体并更新协作矩阵，然后基于转移概率矩阵软引导构建DAG工作流，最后按DAG节点调度各智能体执行并汇总结果。该设计解决的核心问题是让编排层具备记忆与复用能力，把历史成功的智能体交接模式迁移到新任务，同时以低成本路由实现能力扩展。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "端到端通过率（pass rate）", "value": "优于近期工作流构建基线，在结构化、可分解任务上增益最大", "setting": "混合代码、数学与问答测试套件"}, {"metric": "LLM路由调用次数", "value": "相比纯LLM方法减少超过80%", "setting": "分层语义匹配策略对比纯LLM路由"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于历史工作流构建智能体两两协作频率的转移概率矩阵，并在DAG工作流构建中以软引导方式使用：层内排序优化、概率阈值化边建议、传递归约以最大化并行度；充分性驱动的智能体创建循环：通过语义匹配分数检测能力缺口，用LLM生成专用智能体，并同步注入协作矩阵，使新智能体立即可用且带有预测的协作先验；分层语义匹配策略：先用预训练句向量做快速确定性能力匹配，仅对低置信度情况调用LLM验证，相较纯LLM方法减少80%以上LLM路由调用

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed for complex knowledge work, yet their orchestration layers remain largely memoryless: each new task is decomposed, assigned, and executed from scratch with no benefit from prior successful executions. We present WorkflowOps, a multi-agent workflow orchestration framework that learns agent collaboration priors from historical workflows and expands its agent pool on demand to cover new capability requirements. Our approach introduces three coupled mechanisms. First, a transition probability matrix captures pairwise agent collaboration frequencies from past workflows and applies them as soft guidance during DAG workflow construction through intra-layer ordering optimization, probability-thresholded edge suggestion, and transitive reduction for parallelism maximization. Second, a sufficiency-driven agent creation loop detects capability gaps via semantic matching scores, generates specialized agents through an LLM, and simultaneously injects them into the collaboration matrix, so that newly created agents are immediately usable with predicted collaboration priors. Third, a layered semantic matching strategy uses pre-trained sentence embeddings for fast, deterministic capability matching as a first pass, invoking LLM verification only for low-confidence cases, thereby reducing LLM routing calls by over 80\% compared to pure-LLM approaches. Experiments on mixed code, math, and question-answering suites show that WorkflowOps improves end-to-end pass rates over recent workflow-construction baselines, with the largest gains on structured, decomposable tasks where past agent handoff patterns transfer.

</details>


### [75] [SIGMA: Self-Improving Alignment Generalization from a Model Spec](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07935)
*Jingyu Zhang,Shruti Palaskar,Daniel Khashabi,Benjamin Van Durme,Leon A. Gatys,Joseph Yitan Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIGMA 提出从 Model Spec 出发的 LLM 对齐自改进流水线：模型自己合成对齐困境任务，再通过 SFT 与基于 rubric 的强化学习进行自评判对齐训练，在仅使用单轮聊天数据的情况下，提升多轮 agent 安全性并保留通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current LLM alignment relies on external supervision such as stronger models or curated data, creating a supervision bottleneck; meanwhile capability self-improvement can outpace safety alignment. The paper asks whether models can improve their own safety alignment from only a Model Spec and generalize out of distribution.

Method: SIGMA is a data generation and training pipeline for alignment self-improvement from a Model Spec. The input is a Model Spec stating the model's desired behavior. In the first stage, spec-guided task synthesis, the candidate model acts as a task designer agent: it generates diverse alignment dilemma scenarios and converts them into training tasks that stress-test its understanding of the Model Spec. In the second stage, self-judged alignment training, the model undergoes supervised fine-tuning and rubric-based reinforcement learning in which the model itself serves as the reward model. The reported training data are single-turn chat data, but the trained model is evaluated in multi-turn agentic environments.
