Analyses indicate that a Model Spec balancing harmlessness and helpfulness, test-time reasoning for safety deliberation, and high-quality rubrics from the task designer agent are crucial for effective self-improvement.

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: SIGMA pipeline for alignment self-improvement from only a Model Spec, without external alignment supervision.；Spec-guided task synthesis where the candidate model acts as a task designer agent to generate diverse alignment dilemma scenarios and training tasks that stress-test its understanding of the spec.；Self-judged alignment training combining supervised fine-tuning with rubric-based reinforcement learning, using the model itself as the reward model.；Demonstration that single-turn chat training generalizes to multi-turn agentic safety settings and outperforms Deliberative Alignment and Constitutional AI baselines.；Analysis identifying a harmlessness-helpfulness balanced Model Spec, test-time reasoning for safety deliberation, and high-quality rubrics from the task designer agent as crucial for effective self-improvement.

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly capable of executing complex tasks and of recursively improving themselves on easy-to-verify objectives such as software engineering and mathematics. Since alignment is much harder to verify, this creates a growing risk of capabilities increasing without appropriate safety alignment, especially as capabilities expand to auto-research and cybersecurity. Existing approaches focus on capability self-improvement using verifiable feedback or on alignment training with supervision from stronger models or curated data, creating an external supervision bottleneck for alignment. We ask whether current models can improve their own safety alignment, and propose SIGMA, a data generation and training pipeline enabling alignment self-improvement that generalizes to out-of-distribution settings. Given only a "Model Spec" stating the model's desired behavior, SIGMA leverages a model's reasoning capabilities to strengthen its own safety reasoning. SIGMA first performs spec-guided task synthesis, using the candidate model as a task designer agent to generate diverse alignment dilemma scenarios and convert them into training tasks that stress-test its understanding of the Model Spec. Next, SIGMA conducts self-judged alignment training through supervised fine-tuning and rubric-based reinforcement learning with the model itself as the reward model. Despite training only on single-turn chat data, SIGMA improves safety alignment in multi-turn agentic environments (AgentHarm harmfulness decreases from 22.6 to 14.8; Agentic Misalignment decreases from 79.1 to 3.8), outperforms Deliberative Alignment and Constitutional AI baselines, and retains general capability. Analyses show that a Model Spec balancing harmlessness and helpfulness, test-time reasoning for safety deliberation, and high-quality rubrics from SIGMA's task designer agent are crucial for effective self-improvement.

</details>


### [76] [Confidence Reasoning Graphs: Structured Confidence Estimation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07948)
*Brendan King,Farima Fatahi Bayat,Jean-Flavien Bussotti,Pouya Pezeshkpour,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduces Confidence Reasoning Graphs (CRGs), an inference-time method for estimating calibrated confidence in an LLM agent's task success from a single trajectory. CRGs decompose the success claim into evidence-grounded sub-claims, estimate confidence per terminal claim, and aggregate them. Across three agentic benchmarks and multiple models/frameworks, CRGs yield better calibration and risk-aware decisions than baselines, without needing privileged access or training data, and provide auditable evidence.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Estimating calibrated confidence in an LLM agent's success from a single trajectory without privileged model access or training data, to enable informed decisions about trust or intervention.

Method: A CRG begins with the claim that the agent accomplished its task. It decomposes this claim into contextualized sub-claims grounded in trajectory evidence (e.g., observations, actions, intermediate results). Confidence is estimated for each terminal sub-claim using an inference-time procedure that does not require privileged model access or training data. Finally, these terminal confidences are aggregated into an overall confidence estimate for the top-level task success claim. The graph structure captures dependencies among sub-claims and evidence, enabling the final estimate to reflect the distributed and interdependent nature of success evidence. The framework outputs not only a confidence score but also the claims and supporting evidence, allowing audit at decision time. Ablations show that claim-level confidence estimation and aggregation drive improvements, not graph construction alone.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "calibration and risk-aware decision making", "value": "outperforms verbalized, sampling-based, and white-box surrogate baselines across three agentic benchmarks, three backbone models, and three agent frameworks", "setting": "offline evaluation on agentic benchmarks"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Inference-time framework that decomposes the claim of task success into contextualized sub-claims grounded in trajectory evidence and aggregates terminal claim confidences into an overall estimate.；No need for privileged model internals, costly rollouts, or training data, making it practical for frontier LLM deployments.；Exposes claims and trajectory evidence for decision-time auditing, providing interpretability.；Demonstrates that calibration error alone can be misleading; discrimination performance must also be considered.

Abstract: When using an LLM agent in a consequential domain, making an informed decision about whether to trust its output or intervene requires calibrated confidence in the agent's success. Confidence estimation for agents is difficult because evidence about success is distributed across heterogeneous, interdependent steps of an agent's trajectory. Practical agentic deployments introduce further challenges: frontier LLMs often provide limited access to internal signals, agent roll-outs are costly, and training data may be unavailable or quickly become outdated. To address these challenges, we introduce Confidence Reasoning Graphs (CRGs), an inference-time framework that estimates the probability an agent accomplished its task from a single trajectory, without privileged model access or training data. Rather than compressing an execution into a single holistic judgment, a CRG begins with the claim that the agent accomplished its task, decomposes it into contextualized sub-claims grounded in trajectory evidence, estimates confidence for each terminal claim, and finally aggregates these into an overall confidence estimate. Across three agentic benchmarks, three backbone models, and three agent frameworks, CRGs yield better-calibrated confidence and stronger risk-aware decision making than verbalized, sampling-based, and white-box surrogate baselines. We further find that calibration error alone can be misleading: a white-box surrogate baseline appears well calibrated while providing near-chance discrimination. Ablations attribute CRG's improvements to claim-level confidence estimation and aggregation rather than graph construction alone. Finally, a CRG exposes the claims and trajectory evidence underlying each confidence estimate, enabling it to be audited at decision time.

</details>


### [77] [RA-MoWE: Workflow-Affinity Embeddings for Query Clustering and Agentic Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07851)
*Qi Cheng,Shengyu Chen,Wei Cheng,Yiqun Xie,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen,Xiaowei Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RA-MoWE 提出用参考工作流在查询上的表现构建 workflow-affinity embedding，对查询聚类并生成可复用的专家工作流；训练 encoder 从文本预测嵌入，使新查询无需执行参考工作流即可选择专家。在数学、科学、编程四个基准的 300 查询测试集上平均任务得分相对参考工作流选择提升 4.04 个百分点，同时推理语言模型调用减少 27.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为整批任务统一优化的 agentic workflow 会忽略不同查询所需的推理策略差异，而逐查询搜索新工作流又会重复昂贵的优化过程。

Method: RA-MoWE 将每个查询表示为一个 workflow-affinity embedding：固定一组参考工作流，在查询上执行并记录各参考工作流的表现得分，由此得到反映“哪些推理策略对该查询有效”的向量。基于这些嵌入对查询进行聚类，使共享有效推理策略的查询归入同一簇。对每个聚类，使用该簇的查询及平均嵌入作为初始条件，通过执行反馈迭代初始化并精炼出一个专用 expert workflow。为免于对新查询执行全部参考工作流，框架训练一个 embedding encoder，从查询文本直接预测其 workflow-affinity embedding；推理时仅用预测嵌入选择对应的已生成 expert workflow 执行，从而减少语言模型调用次数并提升任务得分。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "average task score", "value": "+4.04 percentage points", "setting": "300-query test set from four benchmarks spanning mathematics, science, and programming, compared to selecting among reference workflows"}, {"metric": "language-model calls at inference", "value": "27.7% fewer", "setting": "same 300-query test set, compared to reference workflow selection"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: workflow-affinity embedding：用固定参考工作流集合在查询上的表现构成查询嵌入，揭示查询共享的有效推理策略；基于亲和嵌入的查询聚类，并用集群查询与平均嵌入初始化、精炼专用工作流；训练 embedding encoder 从查询文本直接预测亲和嵌入，使新查询无需执行参考工作流即可选择已生成的专家工作流

Abstract: Agentic workflows enable large language models (LLMs) to solve complex tasks by coordinating reasoning, tool use, and verification. However, a workflow optimized for an entire task collection can overlook differences in the reasoning strategies that individual queries need, while searching for a new workflow for every query repeats costly optimization. To address this tradeoff, we introduce RA-MoWE, a framework that uses workflow-affinity embeddings to cluster queries and guide the generation of reusable expert workflows. Each embedding records how well a fixed set of reference workflows solves a query, revealing similarities in which reasoning strategies are effective. RA-MoWE uses each cluster's queries and average embedding to initialize and refine a specialized workflow through execution feedback. An embedding encoder predicts these embeddings from query text, allowing new queries to select a generated expert without first executing the reference workflows. On a 300-query test set drawn from four benchmarks spanning mathematics, science, and programming, RA-MoWE improves average task score by 4.04 percentage points over selecting among the reference workflows, while using 27.7% fewer language-model calls at inference.

</details>


### [78] [Do I Need the Cloud? Uncertainty-Aware Step-Level Handoff for Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07816)
*Abolfazl Younesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STEPGATE提出步骤级不确定性感知的本地SLM与云端强模型交接框架，对每个智能体动作打分并选择性升级困难步骤。在单步BFCL衍生测试集上达到82.7%成功率（30.8%升级），优于本地-only（67.3%）和随机升级（75.4%）；多轮评估中在30%云动作率下取得69.0%轨迹成功率与84.0%动作成功率，优于随机升级和查询级路由。评估限于单一模型家族、脚本任务和小规模测试集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由器通常在查询级别一次性选择模型，但智能体在顺序决策过程中每一步的难度会随中间观察动态变化，导致固定的本地/云端选择要么错误频发，要么过度调用云端模型。

Method: STEPGATE的输入是智能体在每一步的上下文与候选动作。其核心模块为：1）本地小语言模型（SLM，例如Qwen2.5-1.5B）作为主控制器，逐步生成动作或工具调用；2）不确定性评分模块，对本地SLM的当前动作输出进行不确定性打分，作为该步骤难度的代理指标；3）风险分层与升级决策模块，根据评分将步骤划分为不同风险层级，决定仅由本地执行还是将当前步骤升级到更强后端（Qwen2.5-7B）；4）云端强模型执行模块，对被升级的步骤生成动作，其结果返回智能体循环。数据流为：每一步本地SLM先产生动作并打分，若风险超过阈值则向云端发送当前步骤上下文并获取更强动作，随后执行并进入下一步。训练与优化：论文未描述对模型进行额外训练或路由器的监督训练细节，机制主要依赖不确定性评分与阈值决策。推理路径上，框架按步骤动态决定本地执行或云端升级，从而在保持较低云动作比例（单步30.8%、多轮30.0%）的同时提升任务成功率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务成功率", "value": "82.7%", "setting": "52任务留出单步BFCL衍生测试集，Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B模型对，30.8%升级率"}, {"metric": "任务成功率", "value": "67.3%", "setting": "同上，仅本地SLM"}, {"metric": "任务成功率", "value": "75.4%", "setting": "同上，随机升级，升级率33.8%"}, {"metric": "轨迹成功率", "value": "69.0%", "setting": "多轮评估，仅30.0%云端动作"}, {"metric": "动作成功率", "value": "84.0%", "setting": "多轮评估，仅30.0%云端动作"}, {"metric": "轨迹成功率", "value": "48.0%", "setting": "多轮评估，仅本地SLM"}, {"metric": "动作成功率", "value": "70.5%", "setting": "多轮评估，仅本地SLM"}, {"metric": "轨迹成功率", "value": "60.0%", "setting": "多轮评估，随机升级"}, {"metric": "动作成功率", "value": "78.2%", "setting": "多轮评估，随机升级"}, {"metric": "轨迹成功率", "value": "57.0%", "setting": "多轮评估，查询级路由"}, {"metric": "动作成功率", "value": "77.1%", "setting": "多轮评估，查询级路由"}, {"metric": "轨迹成功率", "value": "82.0%", "setting": "多轮评估，仅强模型，100%云端动作"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 步骤级的不确定性感知升级框架STEPGATE，对本地SLM的每个动作打分并选择性升级到更强模型；基于不确定性分数的风险分层机制，用于判断当前步骤是否需要云端协助；在单步与多轮智能体任务上验证步骤级升级相比本地-only、随机升级和查询级路由的效果与云调用率权衡

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are attractive as local agent controllers because they reduce remote inference, latency, and deployment footprint, yet structured tool errors can cause an agent step to fail. Existing routers typically select a model once per query. However, agents expose sequential decision points whose difficulty dynamically changes based on intermediate observations. We propose STEPGATE, an uncertainty-aware handoff framework that scores each local SLM action and selectively escalates challenging steps to a stronger model. On a 52-task held-out single-step BFCL-derived test split, the Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B pair attains 82.7% task success with 30.8% escalation, versus 67.3% local-only and 75.4% random escalation (which uses 33.8% escalation). In a separate multi-turn evaluation, STEPGATE achieves 69.0% trajectory success and 84.0% action success using only 30.0% cloud actions, compared with 48.0%/70.5% local-only, 60.0%/78.2% random escalation, and 57.0%/77.1% query-level routing (strong-only achieves 82.0% trajectory success at 100% cloud actions). These results suggest that step-level escalation recovers a large share of the performance gap to the stronger Qwen2.5-7B backend at a matched cloud-action rate while transmitting fewer tokens remotely. However, our evaluation is limited to one model family, a single stronger backend, and scripted tasks. Furthermore, the test sets are small, multi-turn comparisons rely on paired intervals and statistical tests, and our risk tiers serve as research annotations rather than formal safety guarantees.

</details>


### [79] [OOPMAS: Object-Oriented Multi-Agent Systems for Query-Level Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07787)
*Qi Cheng,Shengyu Chen,Wei Cheng,Yiqun Xie,Xiaowei Jia,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OOPMAS提出训练无关的面向对象多智能体框架，在单查询粒度生成智能体集合与协调工作流，并用动态技能库从执行反馈中积累结构化经验，实现无需微调的持续改进；在代码、数学、QA混合任务基准上达到89.6%准确率，超过最强基线18.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统自动化设计方法多在任务级别生成单一固定工作流并统一应用于所有查询，无法适应同一任务内查询难度差异大以及真实工作负载混合异构任务类型的现实条件。

Method: OOPMAS是一个训练无关框架，在单个查询粒度生成智能体集合与协调工作流。输入是任意查询，可来自代码、数学、QA等异构任务类型。框架不使用固定任务级工作流，而是为每个查询动态构建一组智能体。每个智能体被表示为面向对象类定义，类中封装专用角色、可调用工具以及持久状态，使智能体在执行过程中保持并复用上下文。协调工作流被表达为可执行的主函数，该主函数遍历这些智能体对象，按生成的调用顺序和交互逻辑执行。优化过程无需梯度更新或微调：框架维护一个动态技能库，跨优化轮次从执行反馈中积累结构化经验教训；后续轮次通过这些结构化经验进行上下文内改进，从而调整后续查询的智能体集合与工作流生成策略。推理路径为：接收查询后，框架基于当前技能库生成或调整智能体类定义并组合出可执行主函数，运行智能体对象完成查询，执行反馈被提取为结构化经验写回技能库，供下一轮使用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "89.6%", "setting": "混合任务基准，查询覆盖代码、数学和QA"}, {"metric": "accuracy improvement", "value": "18.1 percentage points", "setting": "OOPMAS 89.6% 对比最强基线"}, {"metric": "accuracy", "value": "92.4%", "setting": "模型替换研究中使用最强LLM骨干模型时"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出训练无关的OOPMAS框架，在单个查询粒度上同时生成智能体集合与协调工作流，而非任务级固定工作流。；将智能体表示为面向对象类定义，每个智能体拥有专用角色、工具和持久状态；工作流表达为在这些智能体对象之上可执行的主函数。；引入动态技能库，跨优化轮次从执行反馈中积累结构化经验教训，实现无需梯度更新或微调的上下文内改进。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) powered by large language models have shown strong performance across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and question answering. However, existing methods for automating MAS design mostly operate at the task level, producing a single fixed workflow per benchmark that is applied uniformly to all queries. This assumption fails under realistic conditions. Query difficulty varies widely within a task, and real-world workloads mix heterogeneous task types. We introduce OOPMAS, a training-free framework that generates both the agent set and the coordination workflow at the granularity of individual queries. Agents are represented as object-oriented class definitions with dedicated roles, tools, and persistent state, and workflows are expressed as executable main functions over these agent objects. A dynamic skill library accumulates structured lessons from execution feedback across optimization rounds, enabling in-context improvement without any gradient updates or fine-tuning. On a mixed-task benchmark of queries spanning code, math, and QA, OOPMAS achieves 89.6% accuracy, outperforming the strongest baseline by 18.1 percentage points. A model-swap study across four LLM backbones shows consistent scaling, reaching 92.4% with the strongest model.

</details>


### [80] [Evidence Before Sampling: Interpretable Implicit Negative Candidate Discovery for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07708)
*Shreya Rajpal,Sonia Sharma,Swapnil Parekh,Lisa Li,Jeyendran Balakrishnan,Nagaraj Janardhana,Andrew Mattarella-Micke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出证据驱动的隐式负样本候选发现方法，通过符号规则和LLM解释实现可解释、业务对齐的负样本选择，在工业B2B场景和公开数据集上验证了候选质量提升和下游PR-AUC改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中缺失交互不能直接作为负样本，缺乏可解释且与业务目标对齐的隐式负样本候选发现方法。

Method: 方法将未观测交互的隐式负样本候选发现问题形式化为寻找有观测客户行为支持的交互。首先从用户-物品交互数据中挖掘行为模式，并将其编码为符号规则；然后对规则基于支持度、信息量和产品相关性进行评分，按证据强度排序保留。接着，LLM接收保留的规则，结合业务目标和领域知识生成解释；最后将LLM解释与统计证据合并为最终报告，用于负样本候选的质量评估和下游训练。训练目标关注候选质量与下游推荐性能的分离评估，推理时通过规则评分和LLM解释支持可解释的负样本选择。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "PR-AUC", "value": "12.5%", "setting": "工业B2B任务中，符号选择相比随机选择，在四个负样本每正样本设置下，下游测试PR-AUC提升12.5%"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 形式化隐式负样本候选发现问题，要求未被观测的交互行为有观测客户行为作为证据支持。；将行为模式编码为符号规则，并基于支持度、信息量和产品相关性打分，按证据排序保留规则。；利用LLM结合业务目标和领域知识解释保留规则，并将LLM解释与统计证据合并为最终报告。

Abstract: Recommender systems learn from observed user-item interactions, but explicit negative feedback is often unavailable. Since deep learning models require negative signals for training, negative sampling methods typically treat selected unobserved interactions as negatives. However, a missing interaction does not explain why a user is uninterested in an item or whether there is sufficient evidence to label it negative. This is especially important in business recommendation, where negative signals should be interpretable and aligned with business objectives. We formulate implicit negative candidate discovery to identify unobserved interactions supported by observed customer behavior. We encode these patterns as symbolic rules, score them based on support, informativeness, and product relevance, and rank the retained rules by evidence. An LLM then interprets the retained rules using business objectives and domain knowledge; the interpretations are combined with the statistical evidence in the final report. We evaluate our method in an industrial B2B setting and across five public recommendation datasets. Candidate-quality evaluations in the industrial setting and three public datasets show higher precision than the evaluated baselines, while symbolic selection improves downstream test PR-AUC by 12.5% over random selection with four negatives per positive example in the industrial task. Our results show that negative candidate validity can be evaluated separately from downstream recommendation performance. This distinction enables evidence-based, business-aligned, and explainable negative selection, improving both interpretability and model training in sparse, skewed, real-world recommendation settings.

</details>


### [81] [PERSIST: Who-What-When Memory Across Sessions for Full-Duplex Spoken Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07725)
*Achira Lin,Siyuan Hou,Wenyi Yu,Xinnian Zhao,Haoyu Niu,Wang Geng,Longshuai Xiao,Shihai Xiao,Mangsuo Zhao,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PERSIST 提出面向多用户、多会话全双工语音助手的长时记忆系统，用 Who-What-When 事件记录和 3W 联合打分完成跨会话记忆检索，并通过复用对话骨干中间表示把检索延迟从 578.42 ms 降至 7.03 ms；在 SpokenTrace 基准上达到 85.08% 端到端准确率，all-support EM@3 从 49.01% 提升至 82.10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索式记忆只关注话题相似度，无法在跨会话多方语音对话中识别当前说话人、恢复相关历史状态，并区分其与后续修正版本，同时全双工实时交互下检索延迟过高。

Method: PERSIST 面向多会话、多说话人语音对话，将跨会话历史结构化为可读事件记录，显式携带 Who（说话人身份）、What（对话内容/状态）和 When（时间与状态版本）信息。检索阶段使用 3W 联合打分机制，融合语义内容相似度、声学说话人身份匹配和时间状态匹配，从而在存在后续修正的情况下恢复正确的历史状态。为支持实时全双工交互，PERSIST 复用对话骨干网络的中间表示，避免对查询音频重新编码，显著降低检索延迟。论文还提供 SpokenTrace 诊断基准，按记忆任务和说话人-查询类型分解评估，覆盖召回、说话人归属和时间状态跟踪三类失败模式。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "end-to-end task accuracy", "value": "85.08%", "setting": "SpokenTrace 基准"}, {"metric": "all-support EM@3", "value": "82.10%", "setting": "SpokenTrace；对比 BGE-large 的 49.01%"}, {"metric": "retrieval latency", "value": "从 578.42 ms 降至 7.03 ms", "setting": "实时全双工交互，复用对话骨干中间表示，避免 query 音频重编码"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 显式建模 Who、What、When 的跨会话可读事件记录结构，将历史整理为事件级记忆。；提出 3W 联合打分机制，融合语义内容、声学说话人身份和时间状态进行记忆检索。；复用对话骨干中间表示，避免对 query 音频重新编码，将检索延迟从 578.42 ms 降至 7.03 ms。；提出 SpokenTrace 诊断基准，按记忆任务和说话人-查询类型分解评测，暴露召回、说话人归属和时间状态跟踪失败。

Abstract: Modern voice assistants may be shared by multiple users and should be able to answer questions about earlier conversations such as "When did I originally plan to leave?" or adapt their behavior to individual users based on past interactions. This requires more than retrieving a topically similar passage: the assistant must identify the current speaker, recover the relevant past state, and distinguish it from later revisions. We present PERSIST, a persistent memory system for multi-session, multi-speaker spoken dialogue that explicitly models Who, What, and When. PERSIST structures cross-session histories into readable event records and retrieves them with a 3W joint scoring mechanism that combines semantic content, acoustic speaker identity, and temporal state. For real-time full-duplex interaction, PERSIST further reuses intermediate representations from the dialogue backbone, avoiding query-audio re-encoding and reducing retrieval latency from 578.42 ms to 7.03 ms. We also introduce SpokenTrace, a diagnostic benchmark that factorizes evaluation along memory tasks and speaker-query types, exposing failures in recall, speaker attribution, and temporal-state tracking. On SpokenTrace, PERSIST achieves 85.08% end-to-end task accuracy and improves all-support EM@3 from 49.01% with BGE-large to 82.10%.

</details>


### [82] [AgentMemGate: Addressing Speculation Contamination in Conversational Assistant Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07707)
*Chirag Sharma,Benjamin Fowlersmith,Karime Maamari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对对话助手长期记忆中的推测污染问题，提出写入时门控AgentMemGate，将用户计划等推测性陈述排除在档案记忆外，并贡献包含计划确认/放弃/未解决的多会话数据集。在核心基准上消除观测到的污染并将任务准确率从65%提升到95%，在更难保留集上污染率降至3.4%-5.7%，任务准确率提升9-13个百分点，但字段匹配仍是剩余瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话AI长期记忆中的推测污染问题：用户可能或计划中的事情被抽取为已完成事实存入记忆，并在后续会话中作为当前状态使用。

Method: AgentMemGate是一个针对档案存储记忆的写入时门控。输入为用户消息及其抽取出的陈述。门控模块将抽取陈述分类为四类：推测、已完成事件、修正或其他。推测类陈述不进入长期记忆，而是暂存于记忆之外，并附带条件（如计划得到确认或放弃）用于后续提升为事实或删除。对于被判定为已完成事件或修正的陈述，正常写入档案存储。检索时仅使用已写入的存储，因此未解决的推测不会污染后续会话的状态。训练目标或优化方法在摘要中未具体说明。推理流程为：抽取陈述→门控分类→条件性写入/暂存/丢弃→后续会话检索。论文识别出字段匹配是剩余瓶颈：现实推测往往不匹配任何档案字段，导致无法到达门控。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "断言未解决计划为当前状态的比例", "value": "35.2%", "setting": "Mem0在147个对话的保留集上"}, {"metric": "断言未解决计划为当前状态的比例", "value": "27.3%", "setting": "Graphiti在147个对话的保留集上"}, {"metric": "污染率下降", "value": "从87.5%降至0%", "setting": "核心基准，最暴露的抽取风格，与未门控相同流水线对比"}, {"metric": "任务准确率提升", "value": "从65%升至95%", "setting": "核心基准，与未门控相同流水线对比"}, {"metric": "门控后污染率", "value": "3.4%-5.7%", "setting": "更难保留集"}, {"metric": "任务准确率提升", "value": "9-13个百分点", "setting": "更难保留集"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出推测污染（speculation contamination）概念，并定义其在中间状态上的错误表现。；设计写入时门控AgentMemGate，将抽取的陈述分类为推测、已完成事件、修正或其他，推测被排除在记忆外并附带条件控制后续提升或删除。；贡献多会话对话数据集，包含计划被确认、放弃或未解决的状态，用于评估中间状态推理。

Abstract: Conversational AI assistants with long-term memory extract facts from user messages into a store consulted in later conversations. A stated plan can enter that store as fact: a user who might move to Seattle may be recorded as already living there. We call this speculation contamination. Final-state memory benchmarks miss this error because they do not probe intermediate state and include few unresolved speculations. We present AgentMemGate, a write-time gate for profile-store memory that classifies extracted statements as speculation, completed event, correction, or other. Speculations remain outside memory, with conditions governing later promotion or deletion. We also contribute a dataset of multi-session conversations in which plans are confirmed, abandoned, or left unresolved. On our 147-conversation held-out set, Mem0 and Graphiti assert unresolved plans as current state for 35.2% and 27.3% of pending plans. On the core benchmark, AgentMemGate eliminates all observed contamination relative to the identical ungated pipeline (87.5% to zero for the most exposed extraction style) and raises task accuracy from 65% to 95%. On the harder held-out set, gated contamination is 3.4% to 5.7% and task accuracy rises by 9 to 13 percentage points. Our analysis identifies field matching as the main remaining bottleneck: realistic speculations often match no profile field and never reach the gate. We release our datasets, prompts, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [83] [Massive Activation Gating Channel in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07661)
*Minjia Mao,Shi Chen,Bowen Yin,Xiao Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文发现LLM中massive activations由FFN输入embedding中的单一通道（MAGC）控制，并给出理论解释。在六个LLM上验证了该机制，属于LLM内部机制理解类工作，对模型压缩、推理优化和可靠性有潜在启发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Massive activations是LLM中普遍存在的现象，但其在预训练LLM中随token传播时如何产生仍不清楚。本文旨在揭示massive activation的产生机制。

Method: 论文研究LLM中massive activation（少量隐藏通道幅度异常大）的产生机制。输入为token，经过embedding层后进入FFN。作者发现FFN输入embedding中存在一个单一通道（MAGC），其位置对特定LLM固定。当MAGC的值足够大（或小，取决于LLM）时，spike FFN的输出会呈现massive activations。理论上，当MAGC值足够大（或小）时，spike FFN的输出渐近退化为二次形式，混合FFN下投影矩阵的少数列，这些列的形状与massive activations一致，因此输出呈现massive activations。实验在六个LLM（四个模型家族、不同规模）上验证了MAGC的存在和效果。未披露训练目标、推理服务流程或部署细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现massive activation由FFN输入embedding中的单一通道（MAGC）控制，该通道位置对于给定LLM固定。；当MAGC值足够大（或小，取决于LLM）时，spike FFN的输出呈现massive activations。；在四个模型家族的六个LLM及不同规模上验证了MAGC的存在和效果。；从理论上解释MAGC诱导massive activation的机制：spike FFN输出渐近退化为二次形式，混合FFN下投影矩阵的少数列，这些列呈现massive activation形状。

Abstract: Massive activations, a phenomenon in which a small number of hidden channels exhibit exceptionally large magnitudes, are pervasive in large language models (LLMs). However, the mechanism by which a token develops massive activations as it propagates through a pretrained LLM remains poorly understood. In this paper, we find that the emergence of massive activations is controlled by a single channel in the input embedding to a spike feed-forward network (FFN). The position of this channel is fixed for a particular LLM. We name this channel the massive activation gating channel (MAGC). When the value of the MAGC is sufficiently large (or small, depending on the LLM), the output of the spike FFN exhibits massive activations. Examining six LLMs across four model families and different model sizes, we verify the existence and effect of MAGC. We further provide a theoretical explanation of the mechanism by which MAGC induces massive activations. When the value of MAGC is sufficiently large (or small), the output of a spike FFN asymptotically reduces to a quadratic form that mixes a few columns of the down-projection matrix of the FFN. Since these columns exhibit the shape of massive activations, the output therefore exhibits massive activations.

</details>


### [84] [Where Rules End and Judges Begin: Measuring the Judgment Boundary in Multi-Agent Systems Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07657)
*Shaswata Mitra,Raj Patel,Subash Neupane,Sudip Mittal,Md Rayhanur Rahman,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出DEFER1多智能体安全防御框架，通过28个确定性检查与四法官LLM面板的级联，将攻击成功率从约30%降至约3%，揭示了确定性规则与LLM判断的边界及风险分数门的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统面临对抗内容攻击，现有防御孤立评估且难以审计，需要一种统一的防御框架来平衡确定性规则与LLM判断的边界。

Method: DEFER1（DEterministic-First Enforcement with Residual judgment）采用级联防御架构：首先经过28个确定性检查，尽可能拦截违反明确策略的恶意内容；无法明确判定的剩余请求则提交给由四个LLM法官组成的面板进行判断。在独立测试中，78%的被拦截攻击由确定性检查处理，安全运营领域仅四分之一提案到达法官，表明规则处理明显违规，法官处理意图误报。系统还包含风险分数批准门，但该门不准确地批准了大多数攻击提案却只批准少数合法提案。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将防御组织为五个原则并实现为DEFER1框架，包含28个确定性检查的级联；残余判断委托给四个LLM法官组成的面板；提出风险分数批准门，但揭示其误判问题

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) engage tools, share memory, and delegate tasks, often encountering adversarial content. Current defenses for MAS are typically evaluated in isolation, focusing on one attack type at a time, which can lead to costly and hard-to-audit outcomes. This study organizes defenses into five principles, implementing them as DEFER1 (DEterministic-First Enforcement with Residual judgment), which includes a cascade of 28 checks that blocks what it can and refers the rest to a panel of four judges. In independent testing across four domains, attack success rates drop from about 30.0% to approximately 3.0%, with 78% of blocked attacks handled by deterministic checks. Only a quarter of proposals reach the judges in the security-operations domain, illustrating that the rules provide security for attacks violating clear policies, while judges manage those that only misrepresent intent. Both systems have weaknesses, such as a risk-score approval gate that inaccurately approves most attack proposals but few legitimate ones, highlighting the challenges in assessing threats accurately.

</details>


### [85] [VALSE: Vertical Adaptive Layer Skipping for Efficient Inference in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07606)
*Jia-Dong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VALSE方法，通过轻量级难度估计器和逐层门控实现逐样本非连续层跳过，并建立Expected FLOPs公式、函数空间包含关系及与MoE的对偶性理论，初步验证了原型可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM推理中，如何根据每个输入样本的难度自适应地跳过冗余层，以减少计算量（FLOPs），同时保持模型近似完整的表达能力。

Method: VALSE采用逐样本非连续跳层架构。输入为任意文本序列，首先通过一个轻量级难度估计器，该估计器仅利用模型前几层的输出对当前输入的难度进行评分。基于难度评分，每一层配备一个门控模块，决定是否跳过该层计算。门控允许跳过任意中间层，同时保留后续更深层，因此跳层是非连续且数据依赖的。被跳过的层不参与前向计算，从而减少FLOPs。推理时，输入经过难度估计后，动态生成逐层跳层调度，仅激活必要的深度完成前向传播。训练目标未在摘要中明确，但理论部分建立了Expected FLOPs与层跳概率的关系，并分析了跳层模型与全层模型函数空间的关系。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证明Expected FLOPs公式，给出任意跳层调度下计算代价的闭式表达式；证明跳层模型函数空间严格包含于全层模型函数空间，并提供显式分离示例；揭示VALSE与Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）之间的结构对偶性，将垂直深度稀疏与水平宽度稀疏统一；提出基于轻量级难度估计器和逐层门控的逐样本非连续跳层方法，可跳过任意中间层而保留更深层

Abstract: This paper establishes a theoretical framework for vertical adaptive layer skipping, proving three foundational results: (i) an Expected FLOPs formula (theorem 2) giving a closed-form expression for the computational cost of arbitrary per-sample skip schedules as a function of layer-wise skip probabilities; (ii) function-space superset (theorem 10) and strict inclusion (theorem 11) theorems showing that skip-layer models are strictly contained in---yet meaningfully approximate---the full-layer function space, with an explicit separating example; and (iii) a structural duality between VALSE and Mixture-of-Experts architectures (proposition 6), positioning vertical depth-wise sparsity as the orthogonal counterpart to horizontal width-wise sparsity. Building on this theory, we propose VALSE (Vertical Adaptive Layer Skipping for Efficiency), a per-sample, non-contiguous layer skipping method: a lightweight difficulty estimator scores each input from the first few layers, and per-layer gates selectively skip redundant layers---including arbitrary middle layers while retaining deeper ones---so that only the necessary depth is activated for each input, whose feasibility is preliminarily assessed at prototype scale.

</details>


### [86] [Personal-Agent Mediated Recommendation with Cross-Platform User History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07588)
*Yu Xia,Jiangfan Zhang,Jun Xiao,Julian McAuley,Xiangjun Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化个人智能体中介推荐范式：平台先基于本地信息排序，个人LLM智能体再利用跨平台用户历史对结果进行中介重排。提出MediateRec基准和PAMO训练方法，通过反事实掩蔽跨平台历史估计中介支持度并重分配排序感知优势，理论上保持截断优势质量且局部最优。实验显示中介能带来平台修正，但强专有LLM仍存在有害覆盖；PAMO在已见和未见目标平台及真实跨平台测试上均优于仅结果RL，并取得更好的rescue-harm平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在平台中心推荐向用户治理的个性化转变中，个人LLM智能体如何利用用户授权的跨平台历史，对平台本地排序结果进行有效中介，平衡有益修正（rescue）与有害覆盖（harmful override），从而产生最终top-K推荐列表。

Method: 论文提出Personal-Agent Mediated Recommendation架构：输入包括平台本地候选集及平台推荐器产生的初始排序，以及用户授权的跨平台历史。平台推荐器仅使用平台局部信息对候选集排序；个人智能体接收该排序及跨平台历史，通过中介机制修正排序并输出最终top-K列表。核心训练方法PAMO通过反事实掩蔽跨平台历史来估计个人中介支持度，并重分配排序感知优势质量，同时施加平台相对价值下限以避免过度降低平台排序的平均价值。理论分析表明PAMO保持截断层面的优势质量，并在保持平均平台相对价值不降低的一阶重分配中局部最优。推理时，智能体利用跨平台历史对平台排序进行中介，平衡rescue与harmful override，生成最终推荐列表。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 形式化Personal-Agent Mediated Recommendation范式：平台推荐器基于本地信息排序，个人智能体基于跨平台历史进行中介以生成最终top-K。；提出MediateRec benchmark，包含可扩展代理跨平台环境和受控平台-智能体信息边界的真实跨平台测试。；提出Personal Attribution Mediation Optimization (PAMO)，通过反事实掩蔽跨平台历史估计个人中介支持度，并在平台相对价值下限约束下重分配排序感知优势质量。；理论证明PAMO保持截断层面的优势质量，且在不降低平均平台相对价值的一阶重分配中局部最优。

Abstract: Modern recommendation is shifting from platform-centric personalization toward user-governed personalization, where a personal LLM agent can act on the user's behalf across services. We formalize this emerging paradigm as Personal-Agent Mediated Recommendation: a platform recommender ranks a candidate set using platform-local information, and a personal agent uses user-authorized cross-platform history to mediate the resulting ranking and produce the final top-K slate. Such mediation is nontrivial: the platform ranking can encode strong population evidence that the personal agent cannot observe, so effective mediation must therefore balance beneficial rescues against harmful overrides. To study this trade-off, we introduce MediateRec, a benchmark that includes scalable proxy cross-platform environments and a real cross-platform test under a controlled platform-agent information boundary. To train the agent to use cross-platform history effectively, we further propose Personal Attribution Mediation Optimization (PAMO), which counterfactually masks that history to estimate personal mediation support and reallocates rank-aware advantage mass under a platform-relative value floor. We theoretically prove that PAMO preserves cutoff-level advantage mass and is locally optimal among first-order reallocations that preserve this mass without lowering average platform-relative value. Experiments on MediateRec show that personal-agent mediation enables meaningful platform corrections, yet even strong proprietary LLMs introduce non-negligible harmful overrides. PAMO consistently improves over matched outcome-only RL across seen and unseen target platforms and on the real cross-platform test, while achieving a better rescue-harm balance.

</details>


### [87] [LSC-DPO: Learning-Signal-Controlled Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07592)
*Yang Qu,Yusheng Han,Chengjia Feng,Handan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文从损失几何角度分析DPO，将sigmoid因子视为学习信号，提出LSC-DPO动态控制学习信号至目标区间，并通过信号预算补偿减少初始化方差。在AlpacaEval 2等基准上超越DPO及强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO训练中，随着偏好边际（preference margin）增大，logistic损失对进一步变化的敏感度下降，导致学习信号逐渐减弱，如何控制学习信号以稳定并提升偏好优化效果。

Method: 论文未提供完整的端到端系统架构说明，属于算法/损失函数层面的研究。核心架构可概括为：输入为语言模型和偏好数据对，DPO损失基于策略模型与参考模型的对数概率差计算隐含奖励，其logistic sigmoid因子被识别为学习信号。LSC-DPO引入一个控制器，动态调整该学习信号使其接近目标区间，通过对数空间分析给出稳定跟踪条件。训练目标仍为偏好优化，但根据当前学习信号状态调节系数。此外，针对系数初始化引起的瞬态轨迹差异，提出信号预算补偿规则，调整目标学习信号以补偿瞬态差异，从而降低不同初始化带来的性能波动。推理路径与标准LLM一致。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能提升", "value": "优于DPO及强偏好优化基线", "setting": "AlpacaEval 2, MT-Bench, Anthropic-HH基准实验"}, {"metric": "跨系数初始化方差", "value": "显著降低", "setting": "应用信号预算补偿规则后"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从损失几何视角将sigmoid因子识别为学习信号，刻画目标的局部敏感度；提出LSC-DPO，将学习信号动态调节到目标区间；通过log空间分析建立稳定跟踪目标学习信号区间的条件；发现不同系数初始化导致不同的瞬态学习信号轨迹，并提出信号预算补偿规则以降低跨初始化性能方差

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has become a standard reward-model-free approach for aligning language models with preference data. However, as the scaled preference margin grows during training, the logistic DPO loss becomes progressively less sensitive to further changes. We study DPO from a loss-level geometric perspective and identify the sigmoid factor as a learning signal that characterizes the local sensitivity of the objective. Based on this view, we propose Learning-Signal-Controlled Direct Preference Optimization (LSC-DPO), which dynamically regulates the learning signal near a target regime. A log-space analysis establishes conditions for stable tracking of the target learning-signal regime. Experiments on AlpacaEval 2, MT-Bench, and Anthropic-HH show that LSC-DPO consistently improves over DPO and strong preference-optimization baselines. We further find that different coefficient initializations induce distinct transient learning-signal trajectories even when their later signal levels become similar. Based on this observation, we derive a signal-budget compensation rule that adjusts the target learning signal to compensate for these transient differences. The resulting compensation substantially reduces performance variation across coefficient initializations.

</details>


### [88] [Decoupled Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07556)
*Xinle Wu,Yao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeOrch 提出解耦式多智能体编排，将 worker 无关的任务分解与具体 worker 选择分离，用轻量匹配器和上下文赌博机在线适配新 worker，在多样化任务上优于已有自动 MAS 编排方法且调用次数更少，并能零重训练迁移到全新 worker 池。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动多智能体编排方法将规划耦合到固定的 worker 池，并从同一个终端结果训练分解与协作，导致迁移能力受限且难以进行信用分配。

Method: DeOrch 将多智能体编排解耦为两个阶段。第一阶段，规划器在不获取任何 worker 信息的情况下对任务进行分解，生成与 worker 无关的子任务或步骤表示。第二阶段，规划器基于来自 worker 池的紧凑、与 worker 身份无关的 matchability 反馈，选择具体的协作操作（如调用哪个 worker 或如何组织协作）。匹配器是一个轻量级模块，在固定探测集上评估每个 worker 的行为表现，从而估计其适用性，并使用上下文赌博机进行在线更新，使得新 worker 加入时无需重新训练规划器或匹配器。训练时对分解决策和协作决策进行条件信用分配，从而将终端结果归因到不同阶段。推理时，两阶段规划器先分解任务，再结合匹配器给出的适用性估计选择 worker/协作操作，执行后获得反馈用于在线更新匹配器。该设计解决了规划与固定 worker 池耦合、迁移受限以及信用分配不清的问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 解耦式两阶段规划器：先进行与 worker 无关的任务分解，再基于与 worker 身份无关的可匹配性反馈选择协作操作；条件信用分配：将终端结果分解到分解决策和协作决策，分别进行信用分配；轻量级匹配器：通过固定探测集上的行为估计 worker 适用性，并采用上下文赌博机在线适配，无需重训练即可纳入新 worker

Abstract: Learned orchestration can automatically construct effective language-model multi-agent systems, but existing approaches couple planning to fixed worker pools and train decomposition and collaboration from the same terminal outcome, limiting transfer and obscuring credit assignment. We introduce DeOrch, which separates worker-agnostic planning from concrete worker selection. Its two-stage planner first decomposes the task without worker information, then chooses collaboration operations using compact, worker-identity-free matchability feedback from the pool, enabling conditional credit assignment to decomposition and collaboration decisions. A lightweight matcher estimates worker suitability from behavior on a fixed probe set and adapts online with a contextual bandit, allowing new workers to be incorporated without retraining the planner or matcher. Across diverse in- and out-of-distribution tasks, DeOrch outperforms prior automatic MAS orchestration methods with fewer worker calls than competing learned orchestrators, remains effective when transferred to an entirely unseen worker pool without retraining, and shows consistent gains from both components.

</details>


### [89] [MARS: Multi-resolution Adaptive Routing for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07505)
*Ming Yin,Sixun Dong,Yudong Liu,Wen-Yun Yang,Yunjiang Jiang,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARS 提出多分辨率用户记忆，将长历史写入不同半衰期的递归状态轨道，并用稀疏路由读取器为每个 seed 选择相关时间分辨率，缓解单体缓存记忆的时间混叠问题；在三个公开数据集上优于强基线，且热缓存打分延迟约为基线的 1.02×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长历史推荐器中，单体缓存记忆对用户历史的多尺度语义结构（短期意图、中期兴趣、长期偏好）保留不均衡，导致近期与中期内容恢复效果远差于长期内容，作者称之为 temporal aliasing（时间混叠）。

Method: MARS 针对长历史序列推荐设计。输入为用户完整历史序列，并写入多条递归状态轨道，每条轨道锚定不同的半衰期，从而在同一序列中分别承载短期意图、中期兴趣与长期偏好。读取阶段采用稀疏路由读取器：针对每个 seed（候选相关的小样本集合），选择相关的时间分辨率并物化出紧凑的 seed 记忆，从而在固定大小的候选打分接口下复用候选无关的缓存记忆。训练目标与优化细节未在摘要中披露。该设计解决的核心问题是单体缓存记忆对多尺度语义保留不均（temporal aliasing），通过多分辨率写入与按需稀疏读取，在保持热缓存候选打分延迟近似不变（1.02× 基线）的同时提升推荐效果。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "整体性能", "value": "在三个公开数据集上优于强基线，且优势随历史长度增长而增大", "setting": "三个公开数据集，序列推荐任务，与强基线对比"}, {"metric": "消融贡献", "value": "时间多样性与选择性路由各自在组件匹配消融中贡献超过硬窗口记忆或增加容量", "setting": "组件匹配消融，配对检验"}, {"metric": "行为偏移后优势", "value": "MARS 相对接口匹配基线的优势在用户内行为偏移后进一步扩大", "setting": "within-user behavioral shifts 分析"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 MARS 多分辨率用户记忆，将完整历史写入具有不同半衰期锚定的递归状态轨道（recurrent state tracks），使不同时间尺度语义被分别保留。；提出稀疏路由读取器（sparse routing reader），为每个 seed 选择相关时间分辨率以物化紧凑 seed 记忆，同时保持固定大小的候选打分。；通过线性探针诊断 temporal aliasing 失败模式，并用组件匹配消融与配对检验证明时间多样性与选择性路由的独立贡献超过硬窗口记忆或单纯增加容量。

Abstract: Long-history recommenders often compress each user's history into a compact, candidate-independent memory that is cached and reused to score large candidate pools. We show that real user histories exhibit multi-scale semantic structure, with short-lived intent, medium-term interests, and long-term preferences coexisting in one sequence, and that monolithic cached memories preserve these scales unevenly: linear probes recover recent and mid-range content far worse than long-range content. We call this failure mode \textit{temporal aliasing}. We propose \textbf{MARS}, a multi-resolution user memory that writes the full history into recurrent state tracks anchored to different half-lives, and a sparse routing reader that materializes compact seed memories by selecting the relevant temporal resolutions for each seed, preserving fixed-size candidate scoring. MARS outperforms strong baselines on three public datasets, with gains that grow with history length. Component-matched ablations with paired tests show that temporal diversity and selective routing each contribute beyond what hard-window memories or added capacity provide. The advantage of MARS over its interface-matched baseline also widens after within-user behavioral shifts, at about $1.02\times$ that baseline's warm-cache serving latency for $1{,}000$ candidates per user.

</details>


### [90] [Does Muon Need Fine-Grained Spectral Shaping?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07497)
*Meher Chaitanya,Tianyi Zhou,Aristides Gionis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究 Muon 优化器是否需要细粒度谱修形，提出 BulkBoost 两带谱重加权框架，在 30 个 Pythia 继续预训练设置上表明单一谱主体增益可媲美 Freon 的细粒度谱 profile，并优于 Spectra，且优于 Muon 平坦谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 优化器的理想化极向更新对所有奇异方向赋予相同权重（平坦谱），而近期 Freon、Spectra 等优化器采用细粒度谱映射为每个方向分配增益。本文探究 Muon 更新是否需要如此细粒度的谱细节，以及能否用低维的谱重加权捕获同等收益。

Method: BulkBoost 在 Muon 优化器基础上进行谱重加权。Muon 将当前梯度和过去梯度结合成矩阵动量，对动量矩阵 M 进行奇异值分解 M=UΣV^⊤，理想化极向更新 Q=UV^⊤ 对所有奇异方向赋予相同权重（平坦谱）。BulkBoost 将奇异值分为主体和尖峰两组：固定秩变体按照预设秩阈值划分；噪声校准变体则通过分半小批量梯度差估计 Marchenko-Pastur 噪声边缘，将低于边缘的奇异值视为噪声主体，高于边缘的视为信号尖峰。随后对两组施加单一共享增益，增加谱主体相对权重，同时保持每个矩阵未重加权方向的 Frobenius 范数不变。理论部分给出固定划分下向谱主体移动权重降低损失的一阶条件，并量化两带划分捕获最大一阶改进率的能力。训练流程为：计算梯度、更新矩阵动量、执行 SVD、计算谱重加权增益、重构更新矩阵、应用 Nesterov 动量更新参数。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最终损失相对预适应损失的降低比例", "value": "Freon 平均降低0.022%，两带变体降低0.073%–0.147%", "setting": "30 个从 Pythia-14M 到 410M 的继续预训练设置，涵盖六个语料库，与 Muon 平坦谱对比"}, {"metric": "与细粒度谱优化器竞争力", "value": "两带重加权与 Freon 的幂律谱 profile 竞争，并优于 Spectra", "setting": "相同的30个继续预训练设置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 BulkBoost 两带谱重加权框架，包含固定秩和噪声校准两种变体。；噪声校准变体使用分半小批量梯度差校准 Marchenko-Pastur 参考边缘，将 Muon Nesterov 输入的奇异谱分为噪声主体和信号尖峰。；两种变体通过单一共享增益增加谱主体相对权重，同时保持每个矩阵未重加权方向的 Frobenius 范数。；给出固定划分下将权重移向谱主体降低损失的一阶条件，并量化两带划分能捕获所有逐模重分配中最大一阶改进率的比例。

Abstract: Muon combines current and past gradients into matrix momentum. For $M=U\Sigma V^\top$, the idealized polar update $Q=UV^\top$ gives every singular direction the same weight. We refer to this as the flat profile. Several recent optimizers replace this flat profile with fine-grained spectral maps that give each direction its own gain. We ask how much of this spectral detail a Muon update needs. Our spectral diagnostics show that approximately $94$--$97\%$ of measured singular modes lie below an estimated noise edge, yet collectively align positively with a reference gradient. We introduce BulkBoost, a two-band spectral reweighting framework with fixed-rank and noise-calibrated variants. The latter uses split-minibatch gradient differences to calibrate a Marchenko--Pastur reference edge for Muon's Nesterov input, separating the bulk below the edge from the spikes above it. Both variants increase the bulk's relative weight through one shared gain while preserving the Frobenius norm of each matrix's unreweighted direction. For a fixed partition, our theory gives the first-order condition under which moving weight toward the bulk lowers the loss. It also quantifies the fraction of the maximal first-order improvement rate, over all per-mode reallocations, that two bands can capture. Across 30 continued-pretraining settings spanning Pythia-14M to 410M and six corpora, two-band reweighting is competitive with the fine-grained power-law profile of Freon and outperforms Spectra. Measured against Muon's flat profile, Freon reduces final loss by $0.022\%$ of the pre-adaptation loss on average, whereas the two-band variants achieve reductions of $0.073$--$0.147\%$. These observations suggest that useful departures from the flat profile are surprisingly low-dimensional: a single bulk-to-spike gain captures at least as much benefit as the fine-grained spectral profiles.

</details>


### [91] [Evaluating Escalation Signals for LLM Routing: Targets, Controls, and Five Ways to Fool Yourself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07354)
*Ramin Pishehvar,Andrea Morandi,Mahesh Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估semantic entropy作为LLM升级信号，在GSM8K上AUROC达0.871并提升路由准确率9个百分点，但揭示合成基准上难度控制信号可匹配semantic entropy，提出五项检查避免评估自欺，并预测缓存复用信号在新数据集上崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何廉价且可靠地判断是否应将查询从小语言模型升级到大语言模型，并避免评估中的虚假信号。

Method: 论文提出一种评估框架，用于检验查询升级信号（即决定是否将查询从小模型路由到大模型）的有效性。核心流程：给定输入查询，小模型生成多个采样答案；semantic entropy计算这些答案在语义空间中的分歧程度，作为升级信号。同时构建一个仅基于问题难度的廉价估计（无模型参与）作为控制信号。评估时，在三个基准（含GSM8K）和两个模型族（小/大模型对规模约12倍差距）上进行。通过比较semantic entropy与难度控制信号的预测能力，判断信号是否真正提供信息。进一步定义两种“升级成功”标准，分析基准天花板效应，计算实时采样的真实成本，并测试缓存复用方案的可预测性。最终提出五项检查清单，用于避免评估中的自欺陷阱。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AUROC", "value": "0.871", "setting": "GSM8K，小/大模型对规模约12倍差距，semantic entropy区分小模型错误"}, {"metric": "路由准确率提升", "value": "高达9个百分点", "setting": "GSM8K，匹配成本下相对随机升级"}, {"metric": "AUROC", "value": "0.908", "setting": "缓存复用信号在原始数据集"}, {"metric": "AUROC", "value": "0.518", "setting": "缓存复用信号在新数据集，预测性能崩溃至随机水平"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统测试semantic entropy作为升级信号，跨三个基准和两个模型族；揭示合成基准上仅靠问题难度规则即可匹配semantic entropy的误导性结果；提出五项检查以避免自我欺骗：难度控制、升级成功定义、基准天花板、实时采样成本、缓存复用预测；预测缓存复用方案在新数据集上失效，AUROC从0.908降至0.518

Abstract: Deciding when to escalate a query from a small language model to a larger one requires a cheap signal that predicts, before the large model is called, whether escalating would help. Semantic entropy, originally developed to detect hallucinations, is a natural candidate: it measures how much a model's sampled answers disagree in meaning, and high disagreement often signals an unreliable answer. We test it across three benchmarks and two model families. On GSM8K, with a small/large pair about twelve times apart in size, semantic entropy reliably distinguishes the small model's mistakes (AUROC 0.871) and improves routed accuracy over random escalation by up to nine points at matched cost. An earlier strong-looking result on a synthetic benchmark proved misleading: a simple rule based only on question difficulty, with no model involved, matched semantic entropy almost exactly. This paper's main contribution is a set of checks that catch this before it is reported as real. We show that scoring a cheap, question-only difficulty estimate alongside any signal reveals whether the signal adds real information or just tracks how hard a question looks; that two reasonable definitions of "escalation worked" can produce very different results on the same data; that a benchmark can leave almost no room for any signal to beat simply always using the large model; and that the true cost of live sampling can make routing more expensive than calling the large model directly. For a cheaper alternative that reuses cached past outcomes, we show how to predict whether it will work on a new dataset -- confirmed by correctly forecasting a collapse from AUROC 0.908 to chance level (0.518) ahead of time. We offer these as a general checklist for evaluating escalation signals.

</details>


### [92] [Evaluate the Stack, Not the Layer: Do Deterministic and LLM Gates for Agent Actions Fail Independently?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07359)
*Chenglin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文实证检验了智能体工具调用运行时门控堆叠（一层确定性规则 + 四个 LLM judge）的错误独立相乘假设：两个 judge 组合仅等效约 1.2–1.4 个独立层，而规则层加一个 judge 可达约 1.86–2.09 层，说明错误耦合并非均匀；单独准确率不能预测联合覆盖增量，且 judge 耦合中难度占比不可识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时门控层被假设其错误独立相乘，但该假设在真实智能体动作数据上是否成立、规则层与多个 LLM 评判器堆叠时实际等效于多少独立层，尚缺乏实证检验。

Method: 论文研究的是一个智能体动作运行时门控堆叠：输入为带标签的智能体工具调用动作（共 1,119 条，来自三个语料库），输出为拦截/放行/升级人工的判定。堆叠由一层确定性规则层与四个 LLM 评判器（judges）组成；其中三个 judge 的响应被重新采集，并在每次调用时记录实际服务模型版本，用于检测版本漂移。评估方法将每一层视为一次独立错误乘法，通过统计层间错误耦合（φ 系数）把整个堆叠折算为“乘法等效层数”n_mult，并给出完全耦合下的 n_mult 下界。实验控制两种漏检定义：STRICT（升级人工计为未拦截）与 PRIMARY（升级人工计为已拦截），并在池化数据与各语料库上分别报告区间与点估计。此外，实验对比规则层单独漏检率与其对联合覆盖的增量，并分析 judge 耦合中难度占比的可识别性。未披露训练过程、模型规模、硬件与服务时延等部署细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "多次乘法等效层数 n_mult（两个 LLM judge 组合，STRICT 定义）", "value": "约 1.2–1.4 层（φ 中位数 +0.430，6/6 对显著，下界 1.02–1.17）", "setting": "1,119 条标注智能体动作，三个语料库，无自适应对抗者"}, {"metric": "多次乘法等效层数 n_mult（规则层 + 一个 judge，STRICT 定义）", "value": "约 1.86–2.09 层（φ 中位数 +0.014，0/4 显著，下界 1.01–1.09）", "setting": "同上，STRICT miss 定义（升级人工记为未拦截）"}, {"metric": "多次乘法等效层数 n_mult（PRIMARY 定义）", "value": "judge 对 1.21–1.57 层；规则+judge 1.80–2.13 层", "setting": "PRIMARY miss 定义（升级人工记为已拦截）"}, {"metric": "云规则包对规则层单独漏检率的影响", "value": "单独漏检率降低 20%，但联合覆盖无新增", "setting": "规则层单独评估与联合评估对比"}, {"metric": "judge 耦合中难度占比", "value": "31.8%–61.8%（不可识别）", "setting": "不同探针与 miss 定义下"}, {"metric": "未请求模型版本导致的批次占比", "value": "112 批次中 50 批次（集中于外部语料库）", "setting": "某 judge 层级服务端实际版本记录"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出以乘法等效层数 n_mult 及其完全耦合下界来度量堆叠门控的独立错误层数；在 1,119 条标注智能体动作、三个语料库上实证：两个 LLM judge 组合约 1.2–1.4 层且 6/6 对显著，规则层 + 一个 judge 约 1.86–2.09 层且 0/4 显著；发现单独准确率不能预测层对联合覆盖的增量（云规则包将规则层单独漏检率降低 20% 但不增加联合覆盖）；揭示 judge 耦合中难度占比不可识别（31.8%–61.8%）并报告服务模型版本漂移对结论的影响

Abstract: Runtime gates for agent tool calls are stacked on the assumption that their errors multiply. We test it on 1,119 labelled agent actions from three corpora, without an adaptive adversary. The stack has one deterministic rule layer and four LLM judges, three of them re-collected with the served model recorded on every call. We read each stack as a number of multiplication-equivalent layers, n_mult, with its floor under perfect coupling. Under the STRICT miss definition (escalation to a human scored as not stopped), any two judges compose to about 1.2 to 1.4 layers ({\phi} median +0.430, 6 of 6 pairs significant, floors 1.02 to 1.17). The rule layer plus one judge composes to 1.86 to 2.09 layers ({\phi} median +0.014, 0 of 4 significant, floors 1.01 to 1.09). Under PRIMARY (escalation scored as caught) the bands are 1.21 to 1.57 and 1.80 to 2.13. Intervals separate on the pooled data, point estimates split on each corpus, and a third-vendor judge lands in the judge band. Solo accuracy does not predict what a layer adds: a cloud rule pack lowers the rule layer's solo miss rate by 20% and adds no new joint coverage. The difficulty share of judge coupling is not identifiable: 31.8% to 61.8% depending on the probe and the miss definition. One judge tier was served by an unrequested model version in 50 of 112 batches, concentrated on the external corpus. That event overturned a pre-declared analysis rule, and the scoring of review verdicts reversed five conclusions. We report both.

</details>


### [93] [Trajectory-Retrieval Speculative Decoding: When Does a Model's Own History Help?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07350)
*Yuyang Dai,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Trajectory-Local Adaptive Retrieval (TLAR)，从模型自身长链推理轨迹中检索候选草稿，并与草稿模型结合到共享候选树中，通过精确验证保持输出分布。实验表明在匹配验证预算下提高token接受率，并提升端到端吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维推理中顺序解码成本高，但轨迹中积累的可重用续写未被利用；如何判断模型自身历史轨迹何时能提供有用的草稿，并补充现有草稿模型。

Method: TLAR基于投机解码框架。输入为当前长链思维轨迹（已生成的token序列），以及目标模型和可选草稿模型。系统维护一个动态可检索的记忆，存储当前轨迹中已验证的续写片段。在每一步解码前，TLAR根据近期验证结果（接受/拒绝统计）自适应决定是否激活检索以及候选宽度。若激活，则从轨迹记忆中检索近似匹配的续写作为候选草稿，并与草稿模型生成的草稿合并到共享候选树中。共享树中的候选由目标模型进行精确验证，只接受与目标模型输出分布一致的token。该过程在不改变最终输出分布的前提下，通过增加有效草稿长度提升接受率，从而减少目标模型的前向调用次数，提高端到端吞吐。论文在代码调试、数学和开放写作任务上评估，比较源重用、增量接受和成本，并与检索基线及草稿模型基线对比。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 轨迹局部自适应检索（TLAR）：从当前轨迹中检索近似匹配的续写作为草稿候选。；利用近期验证结果自适应调整检索激活与候选宽度。；将检索到的续写与模型生成的草稿统一到共享候选树中，通过精确验证保持目标模型输出分布。；系统评估源重用、增量接受率与执行成本之间的关系，支持将生成轨迹作为运行时记忆。

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought reasoning increases sequential decoding cost while creating a growing history of potentially reusable continuations. We investigate when this history supplies useful drafts and complements an existing drafter. Controlled source comparisons reveal trajectory-specific reuse, motivating our method Trajectory-Local Adaptive Retrieval (TLAR). TLAR retrieves approximately matched continuations from the current trajectory and uses recent verification outcomes to adapt retrieval activation and candidate width. TLAR combines retrieved continuations with model-generated drafts in a shared candidate tree, preserving the target model's output distribution through exact verification. Across code debugging, mathematics, and open-ended writing, our evaluation connects source reuse, incremental acceptance, and execution cost. Combining TLAR with strong retrieval baselines improves token acceptance under matched verification budgets and increases end-to-end throughput over the draft-model baseline. These findings support generated trajectories as runtime memory for adaptive inference.

</details>


### [94] [Rationale-Guided Policy Optimization: Learning to Reason with Adaptive Rationale Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07342)
*Hoang Phan,Minh Pham,Chau Pham,Chinmay Hegde,Trung Le,Qi Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RGPO将ground-truth rationale作为临时脚手架用于on-policy强化学习后训练，先引导模型生成更好回答，再将高奖励模型解迁回无引导设置，无需off-policy格式匹配或强模型拒绝采样。语言与多模态推理实验均优于RLVR基线，消融验证自适应引导是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy强化学习在困难问题上奖励稀疏导致优化信号不足、训练停滞，而现有借助off-policy演示、专家轨迹或模型生成解的方法通常要求辅助数据与RL任务格式一致，还需依赖强模型拒绝采样。

Method: RGPO是一个基于on-policy强化学习的后训练框架。输入为需要推理的问题以及可用的ground-truth rationale信息，输出为经过优化的大语言模型或多模态模型策略。其流程包含阶段式数据流：首先在原始无引导的RLVR设置下，模型对问题生成响应并接收奖励信号；同时框架根据模型当前能力自适应地引入ground-truth rationale作为临时脚手架，使模型在rationale辅助下生成改进的响应；随后仅将其中获得更高奖励的模型生成解从有引导生成阶段迁移回原始无引导设置进行策略优化，从而避免把参考解当作固定模仿目标，并保持模型在原始任务分布上的探索自由。训练目标仍然是on-policy强化学习目标，但通过自适应rationale脚手架缓解奖励稀疏，使优化在困难问题上获得更有效的信号。论文未披露具体模型规模、训练硬件、数据量、推理延迟或服务成本。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出RGPO框架，将ground-truth rationale作为临时脚手架而非固定模仿目标，根据模型当前能力自适应利用。；先用rationale帮助模型生成改进回答，再把高奖励的模型生成解迁移回原始无引导设置，从而保持探索自由。；无需off-policy数据与RL任务格式匹配，也不需要强模型拒绝采样来获取训练轨迹。；在语言和多模态推理设置中一致优于RLVR基线，消融显示自适应rationale引导是增益关键。

Abstract: On-policy reinforcement learning has become a central paradigm for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models. However, its effectiveness is often limited by reward sparsity: when a model fails to discover correct trajectories for difficult problems, the optimization process receives little useful signal and may stagnate. Existing approaches mitigate this issue by incorporating off-policy demonstrations, expert traces, or model-generated solutions, but they typically require the auxiliary data to match the format of the reinforcement-learning task, often relying on rejection sampling from stronger models to obtain suitable training trajectories. We introduce Rationale-Guided Policy Optimization (RGPO), a framework that adaptively leverages ground-truth rationale information according to the model's current capability while preserving its freedom to explore. Rather than treating reference solutions as fixed imitation targets, RGPO uses them as temporary scaffolds: rationales help the model generate improved responses, after which only higher-reward, model-generated solutions are transferred back to the original unguided setting. This design allows training to exploit available ground-truth information without requiring off-policy data to follow the same format as the RL task. Across both language-only and vision-language reasoning settings, RGPO consistently improves performance over RLVR baselines, and ablation studies show that adaptive rationale guidance is a key contributor to these gains. These results suggest that RGPO offers a practical and general approach for reducing reward sparsity, stabilizing reinforcement learning, and improving reasoning performance in both text-only and multimodal models.

</details>


### [95] [Verifying Coordination in Parallel Coding Agents: NP-Bench and a Scheduling Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07261)
*Sumanyu Muku*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将并行编码智能体的冲突协调建模为调度问题，提出基于作用域划分与生产者-消费者图合并排序的调度规划器并集成到Nerveplane，配合NP-Bench三臂基准在真实git merge上验证，干净集成场景从1/9提升至9/9、合并冲突从13降至0，真实契约变更场景集成率从0提升至1.0/0.6，跨会话记忆将重复错误率从1.00降至0.00，并报告了事实路由对长上下文无益的负结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行编码智能体各自通过自测但团队合并结果损坏，现有协调工具为反应式（先等待冲突再警告），在智能体速度下警告到达时编辑已完成，无法事前避免冲突

Method: 系统名为Nerveplane，核心是一个调度规划器（Scheduling Planner）。输入为各工作项（work item）声明的变更作用域（declared scope）以及工作项之间的生产者→消费者依赖关系。处理流程：第一步，将工作项按声明作用域划分（partition）为互不重叠（disjoint）的范围，保证并行智能体不会写同一函数或同一代码区域；第二步，沿生产者→消费者图对合并（merge）进行拓扑排序，使消费者智能体在生产者变更落地后再编码，从而避免依据刚被修改的契约编码。整个规划在上游一次性完成（up front），而非事后反应式冲突检测。系统还包含跨会话记忆（cross-session memory）模块，用于在多次会话间传递已犯错误的记录以抑制重复错误。评测环境NP-Bench为环境锚定的三臂基准：无协调、反应式检测、主动规划三种策略对比；集成验证通过真实git merge完成，既在确定性仿真中运行，也由真实在线智能体驱动。论文还包含一条负结果路径：将事实路由给智能体并不能在窗口适配规模下提升长上下文准确率，其价值体现在成本与容量维度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "clean-integration（无冲突干净集成场景数）", "value": "1/9 → 9/9", "setting": "NP-Bench确定性仿真，无协调基线对比主动规划"}, {"metric": "merge conflicts（合并冲突数）", "value": "13 → 0", "setting": "NP-Bench确定性仿真"}, {"metric": "clean-integration rate（真实破坏性契约变更场景）", "value": "0 → 1.0（前沿模型）/ 0 → 0.6（小模型）", "setting": "live breaking contract change，无协调与反应式检测基线均为0"}, {"metric": "scope leakage（作用域泄漏）", "value": "0/5", "setting": "真实智能体遵循分配的作用域"}, {"metric": "repeated-mistake rate（重复错误率，跨会话记忆）", "value": "1.00 → 0.00", "setting": "强模型与弱模型上均如此"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将并行智能体协调重构为调度问题：按工作项声明的作用域划分互斥范围，并沿生产者→消费者图事先排序合并；构建NP-Bench三臂基准（无协调/反应式检测/主动规划），基于真实git merge验证集成；将规划器集成到Nerveplane系统并在确定性仿真与真实智能体上评估；跨会话记忆机制降低重复错误率；报告负结果：向智能体路由事实并不能在窗口适配规模下挽救长上下文准确率，其价值在成本与容量而非注意力

Abstract: A team of coding agents can look fine agent by agent yet fail as a team: each passes its own tests while the merged result is broken, and single-agent evaluation never catches it. As teams run several LLM coding agents in parallel on one codebase, the agents collide: two rewrite the same function, one codes against a contract a teammate just changed, and integration fails after the work is done. Most coordination tools react (watch for a conflict, then warn), but at agent speed the warning arrives after the wasted edit. We recast the problem as scheduling: take each work item's declared scope, partition the work into disjoint scopes, and order merges along the producer->consumer graph, all up front. We build this planner into Nerveplane and evaluate it with NP-Bench, an environment-grounded three-arm benchmark (no coordination; reactive detection; proactive planning) that verifies integration off a real git merge, both in a deterministic simulation and with live agents. The planner lifts clean-integration from 1/9 to 9/9 scenarios and cuts merge conflicts from 13 to 0, with a gap that grows in the number of agents. On a live breaking contract change it rescues an outcome both baselines miss on every seed: the clean-integration rate rises from 0 (no coordination and reactive detection) to 1.0 on a frontier model and 0.6 on a small one, while agents respect assigned scopes (0/5 leakage). A cross-session memory drops the repeated-mistake rate from 1.00 to 0.00 on strong and weak models alike. We also report a negative result: routing facts to agents does not rescue long-context accuracy at window-fitting scales; its value is cost and capacity, not attention. Across two capability tiers and two vendors, the benefit did not shrink as models got stronger, because it comes from how work is allocated, not model reasoning.

</details>


### [96] [Understanding and Mitigating Inference-Time Overreliance Using Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07311)
*Luoxi Tang,Yuqiao Meng,Nilesh Auradkar,Muchao Ye,Dazheng Zhang,Zhaohan Xi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究 LLM 智能体记忆复用中的“记忆过度依赖”失败模式：检索到的良性记忆可能在查询与记忆部分重叠时误导推断。作者提出即插即用框架 MEMTRIM，在写入时索引记忆证据、在读取时控制复用，去除重复或冲突证据并保留有用信息，无需重训练，兼容嵌入与结构化记忆。实验显示其可减少过度依赖并保留有用记忆收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在推理时过度依赖检索到的记忆，即使记忆是良性、正确存储并被恰当检索，也会扭曲推断；尤其当查询与记忆仅部分证据重叠时，记忆会误导模型。

Method: MEMTRIM 是一个即插即用的框架，作用于 LLM 智能体记忆生命周期的两个阶段：写入时和读取时。写入阶段，系统对记忆证据进行索引，为后续的复用控制提供结构化信息。读取阶段，系统根据当前查询与记忆证据的重叠情况控制复用：去除重复或相互冲突的证据，同时保留对当前任务有用的记忆特异性信息。该设计旨在缓解“记忆过度依赖”现象，即当查询与记忆仅部分证据重叠时，检索到的记忆可能误导推断。MEMTRIM 无需重新训练模型，可应用于基于嵌入的记忆和结构化记忆。论文通过跨模型、跨记忆设置的实验表明，该方法在保留有用记忆收益的同时减少了记忆过度依赖。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统性地刻画并命名“记忆过度依赖”失败模式，通过多基准、多记忆架构实验确认其存在条件；提出 MEMTRIM，一种即插即用框架，在记忆写入时索引证据、在读取时控制复用；MEMTRIM 去除重复或冲突证据，保留有用的记忆特异性信息，无需重新训练，兼容基于嵌入和结构化记忆

Abstract: Agentic memory allows LLM agents to reuse past experience, yet retrieved memories can also distort inference even when they are benign, correctly stored, and appropriately retrieved. We study this failure mode, which we call memory over-reliance. Across benchmarks and memory architectures, we find that memory is useful when past experience transfers to the current task, but can become misleading when only part of the evidence transfers. Failures are strongest under partial query-memory overlap, a pattern further confirmed by controlled experiments thatvary the amount of overlapping evidence. Motivated by this finding, we propose MEMTRIM, a plug-and-play framework that indexes memory evidence at write time and controls its reuse at read time. MEMTRIM removes repeated or conflicting evidence while preserving useful memory-specific information, requires no retraining, and applies to both embedding-based and structured memory this http URL show that MEMTRIM reduces memory overreliance while preserving the benefits of useful memory across models and memory settings.

</details>


### [97] [SPECTRUM: Proximal Spectral Modulation for Looped Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07237)
*Yunbo Long,WenJie Chen,Jiaquan Zhang,Guangya Hao,Zihang Zeng,Pengze Li,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPECTRUM针对循环自蒸馏中正确解多样性收缩问题，提出从参考锚点估计损失敏感KV几何并转化为全秩近端谱调制，在MBPP上5轮后保留89.9%的初始正确AST丰富度，并迁移到HumanEval+和APPS Intro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定信息预算、无外部评估和基于测试样本选择的条件下，模型反复从自身原始输出学习时，正确性可能提升但正确实现解的多样性（广度）会收缩。

Method: SPECTRUM作为一个自蒸馏框架，在每轮迭代中，学生模型从固定参考锚点重新估计损失敏感的key/value几何，并将其转化为全秩近端谱调制。该调制应用于学生模型的生成过程，使得所有生成的completion用于训练单个学生模型。训练完成后，学生的推理过程不再需要任何干预。整个过程在固定信息预算下进行，不依赖持续的外部评估或基于测试的样本选择。在MBPP上经过五轮实验，SPECTRUM保留了初始模型64样本正确AST丰富度的89.9%，并在HumanEval+和APPS Intro上展示了多样性收益的迁移。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "64样本正确AST丰富度保留率", "value": "89.9%", "setting": "MBPP，五轮实验，与Vanilla自蒸馏和子空间投影控制对比"}, {"metric": "64样本正确AST丰富度保留率（Vanilla自蒸馏）", "value": "66.4%", "setting": "MBPP，五轮实验，基线对比"}, {"metric": "64样本正确AST丰富度保留率（子空间投影控制）", "value": "65.5%", "setting": "MBPP，五轮实验，控制对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 形式化Looped Self-Distillation自进化框架，强调生成样本的原始输出学习而不依赖持续外部评估或测试筛选；提出SPECTRUM方法，在每轮从固定参考锚点重新估计损失敏感的key/value几何，并转换为全秩近端谱调制；发现并提出正确解保留（correct-solution retention）作为递归自我改进（RSI）的补充目标，并验证了多样性收益在HumanEval+和APPS Intro上的迁移性

Abstract: A model that learns from its own outputs inherits more than their correctness: it inherits which solutions it produces. We formulate Looped Self-Distillation, a self-evolution framework for code generation in which a model repeatedly generates and learns from its own raw outputs, under a fixed information budget, without ongoing external assessment or test-based selection of the generated samples. We identify a consequential separation: correctness can improve while the breadth of correct implementations contracts. We introduce SPECTRUM, which re-estimates loss-sensitive key/value geometry from a fixed reference anchor at each round and converts it into full-rank proximal spectral modulation. All generated completions train a single student, whose subsequent inference requires no intervention. After five rounds of experiments on MBPP, SPECTRUM retains 89.9% of the initial model's 64-sample correct AST richness, compared with 66.4% for Vanilla self-distillation and 65.5% for a subspace-projection control. The advantage persists at matched correct-sample counts. Without further training or recalibration, the resulting student also achieves higher matched-correct richness than Vanilla SD on HumanEval+ and APPS Intro, demonstrating transfer of the diversity benefit. These findings establish correct-solution retention as a complementary objective of recursive self-improvement (RSI) and show that generation-time intervention can improve the solution repertoire retained by subsequent students.

</details>


### [98] [Cascadia: Resident 975B MoE Inference on Eleven AI PCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07219)
*Tate Berenbaum,Matias Parij,Muthaiah Venkatachalam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Cascadia 在十一台 Intel AI PC 上分布式驻留并服务 975B 总参数/41B 激活参数的 MoE 模型 Inkling，通过自定义 MoE 引擎、OpenVINO 融合 iGPU 计算图、FP16 专家计算与 FP32 恢复、流式流水线及草稿评估，实现 88 流时 60.29 聚合解码 token/s，并系统测量了并发、上下文长度与数值路径一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在多台内存有限、带集成显卡的 AI PC 上分布式驻留并高效服务一个总参数达 975B、激活参数 41B 的稀疏 MoE 模型，同时保持路由规则、数值精度和流式并发性能。

Method: 系统将 975B 总参数、41B 激活参数的稀疏 MoE 模型 Inkling 分布式驻留到十一台 AI PC 上，每台机器容纳六个连续解码层。每台机器具有 64 GB 共享内存、Arc B390 集成显卡和千兆以太网。推理引擎为自定义驻留式 MoE 引擎，保留模型原始路由规则；将稠密前馈块表示为全激活专家切片以复用专家计算路径，并用 OpenVINO 的融合 iGPU 原语构建压缩计算图。专家计算采用 FP16，随后用 FP32 恢复输出数值。跨机器通过流式流水线协调并发生成，千兆以太网承担层间激活与协调流量。解码使用投机式草稿评估，草稿状态从部署的数值路径中捕获，从而测量草稿与目标模型的一致性。推理过程中，注意力循环由单线程 CPU 实现，成为上下文长度增长时首 token 延迟与解码延迟的主要瓶颈；内存可支持每流 512k 位置。默认上下文预算为 1,024 位置，提升后 1k 到 64k token 的真实提示能恢复嵌入代码，首 token 时间随上下文长度呈 aN+bN^2 增长，解码延迟近似线性增长。系统在十五个并发级别（1 至 176 流）下进行配对测量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "稠密层调用时间", "value": "从约 8.1 ms 降至 4.5 ms", "setting": "将稠密前馈块表示为全激活专家切片后的测量值"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 自定义驻留式 MoE 引擎，在十一台机器上保持 Inkling 的路由规则并分布六层连续解码层。；面向 OpenVINO 融合 iGPU 原语的压缩计算图构建，并将 FP16 专家计算与 FP32 输出恢复协调。；将稠密前馈块表示为全激活专家切片，使稠密层调用时间从约 8.1 ms 降至 4.5 ms。；支持并发生成的流式流水线，并采用捕获状态下的草稿评估以测量与部署数值路径的一致性。；在十五个并发级别（1 到 176 流）上进行配对测量，揭示分布式客户端服务的吞吐与延迟边界。；通过捕获的集群状态分离词表选择与权重量化对草稿一致性的影响。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts models make nearly trillion-parameter capacity accessible with sparse per-token computation, provided that the serving system can distribute the weights and coordinate their execution. We present Cascadia's resident execution of Inkling, a 975B-total/41B-active-parameter model, on eleven Intel Core Ultra X7 358H AI PCs, each with 64 GB of memory, Arc B390 integrated graphics and gigabit Ethernet. We contribute a custom resident MoE engine that preserves Inkling's routing rules, constructs compressed graphs for OpenVINO's fused iGPU primitives, and coordinates FP16 expert computation with FP32 output restoration. The engine fits six consecutive decoder layers per machine and represents dense feed-forward blocks as all-active expert slices, reducing measured dense-layer call time from approximately 8.1 to 4.5 ms. A streaming pipeline coordinates concurrent generation, while captured-state draft evaluation measures agreement with the deployed numerical path. Paired measurements at fifteen concurrency levels from 1 to 176 streams reach 60.29 aggregate decode tokens/s at 88 streams, with 46.87 tokens/s over the complete serving phases. At fifteen streams, median first-token latency is 6.05 s. Raising the context budget from the 1,024-position default, real prompts of 1k to 64k tokens recover the embedded code in all 19 measured answers, with first-token time growing as $aN+bN^2$ and decode latency growing approximately linearly, both bounded by a single-threaded CPU attention loop rather than by memory, which holds 512k positions per stream. Evaluation on captured fleet states separates the effects of vocabulary selection and weight quantization on draft agreement. Together, these contributions establish an execution and evaluation approach for large sparse models on distributed client systems with shared CPU-GPU memory.

</details>


### [99] [Internalizing Agent Experience into Diffusion Model Weights via On-Policy Context Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07250)
*Wenxuan Wang,Zekai Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yu Cheng,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出D-OPCD与ASE，将文生图agent harness的知识通过on-policy context distillation内化进扩散模型权重，使模型在仅用原始query时平均分从60.52升至65.09，并支持harness与模型的持续协同演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: agentic harness（记忆、技能、工作流编排、结果验证、迭代精炼）对文生图带来的收益是外部的，仅在整个harness运行时才存在；如何在仅给定原始query的条件下，将harness能力部分内化到扩散模型权重中，使模型本身获得提升。

Method: 系统由两部分组成：一个文生图agent harness与一个扩散生成模型。harness集成记忆、技能、工作流编排、结果验证与迭代精炼，并配备作者提出的Auto Skill Evolver (ASE)来自动演化技能；在推理时，harness针对用户的原始query反复构造和修改prompt，从而引出更好的图像。D-OPCD将harness产生的“agent改进后的prompt”视为privileged context（特权上下文），在on-policy方式下让扩散模型以该特权上下文为条件进行训练，把harness中编码的知识蒸馏到模型权重里。训练目标使模型在仅以原始query为条件时也能部分复现使用harness时的生成质量。蒸馏完成后，模型权重吸收了harness的一部分能力，原harness中相应技能趋于饱和，可被裁剪，从而释放容量让ASE开展新一轮技能演化；第二轮演化的收益又可再次蒸馏回模型，形成harness与模型持续共同演化的闭环。推理路径为：原始query→（可选）harness生成改进prompt→扩散模型生成图像；蒸馏后的模型可直接由原始query生成更优图像。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "average direct-generation score", "value": "60.52 → 65.09", "setting": "四个基准上的平均直接生成得分（无harness，仅原始query条件）"}, {"metric": "second ASE round improvement over skill-free harness", "value": "+1.83 points", "setting": "在更新后的生成器上进行第二轮ASE，相对无技能harness"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Diffusion On-Policy Context Distillation (D-OPCD)，将agent改进后的prompt视为privileged context，把harness知识蒸馏进扩散模型权重；提出Auto Skill Evolver (ASE)，为文生图agent自动演化技能；实现harness与模型的持续协同演化：权重吸收饱和技能后harness可丢弃这些技能并继续演化，第二轮ASE在更新后的生成器上继续带来增益

Abstract: Wrapping an image generation model in an agentic harness can effectively boost Text-to-Image task performance: the harness can leverage memory, skills, workflow orchestration, result verification, and iterative refinement to continually construct and revise prompts, thereby eliciting better images. These gains, however, remain external to the diffusion model and are realized only while the full harness runs. We propose Diffusion On-Policy Context Distillation (D-OPCD), which treats the agent-improved prompt as privileged context and distills the knowledge encoded in the agent harness into the weights of the diffusion model, so that the model retains part of the harness's benefit when conditioned on the original query alone. Using a Text-to-Image agent equipped with our proposed Auto Skill Evolver (ASE), we show that D-OPCD can internalize harness capabilities into the generator's weights, raising the average direct-generation score from 60.52 to 65.09 across four benchmarks. With this knowledge absorbed into the weights, the harness can shed its saturated skills and resume evolving: a second ASE round on the updated generator improves on a skill-free harness by additional 1.83 points, pointing toward text-to-image systems in which harness and model keep improving each other through continual co-evolution.

</details>


### [100] [AMBER: Training Long-Horizon Web Agents through Append-Only Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07118)
*Chinmay Savadikar,Zhaoyu Zhang,Mingyu Zhao,Shuang Xie,Han Li,Tianfu Wu,Lingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AMBER 提出 append-only 记忆库，让 LLM web agent 在长时程任务中用结果奖励端到端 RL 联合学习推理、行动与记忆写入，从结构上保证关键信息与纠正反馈不被覆盖删除。在 WebArena Lite 上平均成功率比 overwrite 记忆基线高 4.09 个百分点，五次重复任务解决比例高 4.8 个百分点，并在实用 token 预算下与更昂贵监督数据训练的基线持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长时程多步交互中，交互历史超出上下文预算，而基于 overwrite 的固定大小记忆在稀疏结果奖励下难以学习，会删除轨迹所需的关键信息以及环境给出的纠正反馈。

Method: AMBER 是一个让语言模型 Agent 在长时程交互中联合学习推理、行动与记忆写入的框架。输入为当前任务与交互历史，Agent 产生推理/行动，同时将自由形式的记忆条目追加到只增不删的记忆库中；append-only 规则保证任何已写入的证据（事实、执行错误、环境纠正反馈、进度）都不会被后续重写删除。训练上，Agent 端到端地用结果奖励进行强化学习，策略需要学会在合适的时刻写入何种记忆，以在有限上下文预算内支持后续决策，而不需要大量人工策划的 SFT 数据。推理/服务时，Agent 从 append-only 记忆库中读取相关条目而非完整历史，从而将上下文控制在实用 token 预算内并保持长时程可靠性。论文以 WebArena Lite 为评测环境，与基于 overwrite 的固定大小记忆基线对比。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均成功率", "value": "相对 overwrite 记忆基线提升 4.09 个百分点", "setting": "WebArena Lite"}, {"metric": "五次重复运行中任务解决比例", "value": "提升 4.8 个百分点", "setting": "WebArena Lite, 5 repeated runs"}, {"metric": "与使用更昂贵 curated supervision 训练的 overwrite 基线相比", "value": "持平", "setting": "WebArena Lite"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Append-only Memory Bank for Evidence Retention（AMBER），通过 append-only 规则从结构上保证信息保留，无需学习逐次改写来传递事实；Agent 联合学习推理、行动与自由形式记忆写入，端到端用结果奖励进行强化学习训练；无需大量精心策划的 SFT 数据进行训练；在实用 token 预算下平衡上下文效率、任务表现与长时程执行可靠性

Abstract: Modern language-model agents increasingly interact with external environments over long-horizon, multi-step trajectories, where the accumulated interaction history can quickly exceed practical context budgets. To ensure reliability, agents must maintain factual information over long horizons, remember execution errors and corrective feedback, and track progress across actions. Several approaches have been proposed to achieve this without the need for maintaining the entire execution history in context, such as using the reasoning and action history, learning to maintain a fixed-size memory through an overwrite mechanism, and periodic summarization. Although overwrite memory can in principle retain anything an append-only memory can, it must learn to carry each fact through every subsequent rewrite, which is difficult to learn from sparse outcome rewards; for interactive applications like web agents, we find that trained overwrite memories delete key information required by the trajectory, as well as corrective feedback received from the environment. We introduce AMBER (Append-only Memory Bank for Evidence Retention) - a simple and scalable framework where an agent jointly learns to reason, act, and write free-form memory, while an append-only rule guarantees retention by construction. This allows AMBER to be trained end-to-end with reinforcement learning from outcome rewards without the need for extensive curated SFT data. On WebArena Lite, AMBER improves average success over overwrite-based memory by 4.09 percentage points, increases the fraction of tasks solved in five repeated runs by 4.8 percentage points, and matches an overwrite baseline trained on substantially more expensive curated supervision. AMBER achieves these improvements while maintaining a practical token budget, providing a strong balance between context efficiency, task performance, and reliable long-horizon execution.

</details>


### [101] [JIVEAdapter: A Multi-Task Additive Low-Rank Adapter via Joint and Individual Variation Explained (JIVE)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07036)
*Sara Abdali,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JIVEAdapter提出一种受JIVE启发的多任务加性低秩适配器，将权重更新分解为共享Joint结构与任务特有Individual结构，通过近正交惩罚分离共享与特有信号，并支持冻结Joint复用。在GLUE/SuperGLUE上以DeBERTaV3-base达到与强基线相当的性能，且无需MoE等额外模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩适配器大多是单任务且以乘性方式表示权重更新，缺乏对跨任务共享信息与任务特有信息的显式分离，导致多任务微调成本高、可解释性差且难以复用。

Method: JIVEAdapter以预训练模型（DeBERTaV3-base）为基座，对每个权重更新矩阵进行加性低秩分解：每个任务组的更新 = 所有任务共享的Joint结构 + 每个任务独有的Individual结构。Joint结构在任务组内联合学习，或增量地逐任务学习，然后冻结；Individual结构按任务独立学习。训练目标包含任务损失与促使Individual结构近正交于Joint的惩罚项，从而分离共享信号与任务特有信号。秩分配在共享Joint池与逐任务Individual池之间自适应进行。推理时，若新任务与已有held-in任务相关，则复用冻结的Joint并结合该任务的Individual，仅需一个便宜的逐方向缩放即可适配；否则训练一个较小的新Individual。整个流程无需MoE等额外模块，保持加性低秩结构，实现参数高效的多任务适配与共享先验复用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能", "value": "与强单任务和多任务低秩基线在匹配每任务有效秩下相当（competitive）", "setting": "GLUE和SuperGLUE，DeBERTaV3-base，匹配每任务有效秩"}, {"metric": "复用效果", "value": "当存在相关held-in任务时，冻结的Joint可通过复用该任务的Individual仅用便宜的逐方向缩放服务于held-out任务，否则训练一个小新Individual", "setting": "GLUE/SuperGLUE held-out任务迁移"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 受统计JIVE启发的加性低秩适配器，将每个权重更新分解为所有任务共享的Joint结构加每个任务独有的Individual结构；对Individual结构施加近正交惩罚，使共享与任务特有信号保持可解释的分离；在共享Joint池与逐任务Individual池之间自适应分配秩；Joint可一次性联合学习或增量逐任务学习，然后冻结并作为新任务的先验复用，无需重训共享部分；无MoE等额外模块，在匹配每任务有效秩下性能与强单任务/多任务低秩基线相当

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts pretrained models at a fraction of the cost of full fine-tuning, yet most low-rank adapters are single-task and represent each weight update multiplicatively, leaving no explicit account of what is shared across tasks and what is task-specific. We introduce JIVEAdapter, a multi-task "additive" low-rank adapter inspired by statistical Joint and Individual Variation Explained (JIVE). JIVEAdapter decomposes every weight update into a Joint structure shared across all tasks plus a per-task Individual structure, penalizes the Individual structures to be near-orthogonal to the Joint so shared and task-specific signal stay "interpretable" and separated, and allocates rank adaptively across a shared Joint pool and a per-task Individual pool. The Joint is learned once, jointly over a task group or incrementally, one task at a time, then frozen and reused as a prior for new tasks without retraining the shared part. On GLUE and SuperGLUE with DeBERTaV3-base, JIVEAdapter is competitive with strong single-task and multi-task low-rank baselines at a matched per-task effective rank, without extra modules such as MoE, and when a related held-in task exists its frozen Joint serves a held-out task by reusing that task's Individual with only a cheap per-direction scale, otherwise training a small new one.

</details>


### [102] [When to Rethink: Learning Multi-Perspective Self-Verification for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07018)
*Ziquan Zhu,Hanruo Zhu,Si-Yuan Lu,Morris Yu-Chao Huang,Yicheng Lin,Wei Han,Tianlong Chen,Mingyuan Wu,Hanchao Yu,Gaojie Jin,Lu Liu,Bo Sun,Tianjin Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOTIVE提出多视角自验证与可靠性引导的接受或重思考框架，用于提升VLM多模态推理的答案可靠性，并在无需外部评判器的情况下减少不必要的推理回合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM自验证方法依赖单一验证标准或固定提示词，导致可靠性估计不完整且不稳定；如何利用多视角验证学习与可靠性引导的接受/重思考决策，在不依赖外部评判器的情况下提升多模态推理的可靠性与效率。

Method: MOTIVE以VLM生成多个候选答案作为输入，对每个候选答案从互补的验证视角进行评估。框架首先使用多视角提示对候选答案获取多维度验证信号。随后通过correctness-grounded multi-view verification learning学习一个与答案正确性对齐的可靠性分数，该分数融合多视角验证结果。推理阶段，可靠性分数用于接受或重思考决策：分数足够可靠时直接返回答案；不确定时触发history-guided rethinking，结合历史信息进行新一轮推理。该设计解决了单一验证标准或固定提示词导致的验证不完整与不稳定问题，并通过可靠性引导决策减少不必要的推理回合。论文未披露具体网络结构、参数规模、训练目标细节或推理硬件。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统分析验证器能力与提示词设计对验证性能的影响，发现更强验证器判断更可靠，且验证性能对提示词高度敏感、无单一提示词在所有任务上占优；提出MOTIVE多视角自验证框架，从互补验证视角评估候选答案；通过correctness-grounded多视角验证学习，训练与正确性对齐的可靠性分数；推理时用可靠性分数驱动accept-or-rethink决策：可靠答案直接返回，不确定答案触发history-guided rethinking；在不使用外部评判器的情况下减少不必要的推理回合，提高可靠性与效率

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance in multimodal reasoning, yet they remain prone to generating plausible but incorrect answers. Self-verification offers a practical way to improve answer reliability without relying on external judges, but existing methods typically depend on a single verification criterion or fixed prompt, resulting in incomplete and unstable reliability estimates. We first systematically analyze how verifier capability and prompt design affect verification performance.
