Our findings show that stronger verifiers provide more reliable judgments, while verification performance is highly sensitive to prompt choice, with no single prompt consistently dominating across tasks. Guided by these findings, we propose \texttt{MOTIVE}, a \textbf{M}ulti-View Self-Verificati\textbf{O}n wi\textbf{T}h Rel\textbf{I}ability-Guided Selecti\textbf{VE} Rethinking framework for reliable multimodal reasoning. \texttt{MOTIVE} evaluates each candidate answer from complementary verification perspectives and learns a correctness-aligned reliability score through correctness-grounded multi-view verification learning. During inference, this score governs an accept-or-rethink decision, allowing reliable answers to be returned directly while uncertain ones trigger history-guided rethinking. Extensive experiments across diverse multimodal benchmarks and VLM backbones demonstrate that \texttt{MOTIVE} consistently outperforms strong self-verification and self-correction baselines. Further results show that reliable verification improves accept-or-rethink decisions and reduces unnecessary reasoning turns, enabling more reliable and efficient self-verification without an external judge.

</details>


### [103] [Offline AI Modules: Voice-First Offline Architecture, Hardware Reference Stack, Quantization and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07026)
*Sunday Afariogun,Odunolaoluwa Jenrola,Zeinab Nezami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对非洲语言社区的离线语音优先AI场景，构建了包含模块化架构、低成本硬件参考栈及量化基准测试流水线的完整技术栈，并在Jetson Orin NX和Raspberry Pi5上评估了2-5B指令微调模型的四种量化格式，发现Q4_K_M兼顾大小与质量，gemma-4-E2B-it达到28.8 tokens/s和89.2%主题分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低功耗边缘硬件（NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX 与 Raspberry Pi5）上，为2-5B参数级指令微调语言模型找到兼顾质量与部署效率的量化方案，并评估其在非洲多语言语音任务上的表现。

Method: 论文提出了一个模块化的离线语音优先架构，包含三个强化组件：（1）模块化语音优先离线架构：处理语音输入到文本，再经指令微调语言模型生成响应，全程无需互联网连接，具体模块间数据流未详细披露；（2）低成本硬件参考物料清单：指定了NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX (TierB) 和 Raspberry Pi5 (TierA) 两类硬件平台；（3）可复现的量化与基准测试流水线：对2-5B参数级指令微调语言模型应用四种量化格式，评估部署指标（解码吞吐、聊天延迟、内存、功耗）和多语言质量（MasakhaNEWS主题分类准确率、每语言困惑度漂移）。语音识别采用Ethio-ASR评估阿姆哈拉语和奥罗莫语。主要发现Q4_K_M在两类硬件上提供最佳大小-质量权衡。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "解码吞吐", "value": "28.8 tokens/s", "setting": "gemma-4-E2B-it 模型，Q4_K_M量化，NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX (TierB)"}, {"metric": "主题分类准确率", "value": "89.2%", "setting": "gemma-4-E2B-it 模型，Q4_K_M量化，MasakhaNEWS多语言主题分类，NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX (TierB)"}, {"metric": "内存占用", "value": "所有三个模型均运行在16GB内存预算内", "setting": "Raspberry Pi5 (TierA)"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首个针对该离线语音技术栈的端到端基准评估，横跨两个硬件层级。；系统性比较四种量化格式对2-5B指令微调模型的部署指标（解码吞吐、聊天延迟、内存、功耗）与多语言质量（MasakhaNEWS主题分类准确率、每语言困惑度漂移）的影响。；将语音识别（Ethio-ASR 阿姆哈拉语、奥罗莫语）纳入同一离线栈进行跨层级评估。；得出Q4_K_M在两类硬件上均为最佳大小-质量权衡的结论，并提供可复现的量化与基准测试流水线。

Abstract: The Offline AI Modules workstream enables practical, low-power, and community-accessible deployment of voice-first AI systems that operate fully offline. Designed for African language communities where speech is the dominant mode of interaction and internet connectivity is unreliable or absent, the workstream delivers three reinforcing components: a modular voice-first offline architecture, a low-cost hardware reference bill of materials, and a reproducible quantization and a reproducible quantization and benchmarking pipeline for instruction-tuned language models in the 2-5B parameter class. This paper presents the first end-to-end benchmark evaluation of the stack across two hardware tiers: an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX (TierB) and a Raspberry Pi5 (TierA). Three instruction-tuned models are evaluated across four quantization formats, assessed for deployment metrics (decode throughput, chat latency, memory, power) and multilingual quality (topic classification accuracy on MasakhaNEWS across English, Hausa, Igbo, Nigerian Pidgin, and Yoruba; per-language perplexity drift). Speech recognition is evaluated using Ethio-ASR on Amharic and Oromo across both tiers. The principal finding is that Q4_K_M quantization represents the best size-to-quality trade-off for deployment on both tiers: gemma-4-E2B-it achieves 28.8t/s decode throughput and 89.2% topic classification accuracy at Q4_K_M on TierB, while all three models run within the 16GB memory budget on TierA.

</details>


### [104] [Principles that Guide, Actions that Inform: Agent Evolution via Knowledge Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06964)
*Bowen Ye,Yongchao Xu,Junkai Ma,Xiang Yin,Wenzhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGA提出经验驱动的知识抽象框架，把Agent交互轨迹逐级提炼为情景、流程与带适用条件的原理，并在推理时检索实例化以修正候选动作，形成执行-抽象反馈闭环，在ScienceWorld与ALFWorld上取得任务表现提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在无法访问大模型参数（尤其是大规模闭源模型）的前提下，把具体交互经验转化为可跨任务复用的抽象知识，从而让LLM Agent持续自我演化并泛化到新情境。

Method: SAGA以LLM Agent的交互轨迹为输入。运行时，Agent在环境中执行动作并记录完整执行证据（轨迹）。记忆层分为三级：底层为与具体情境绑定的情景描述（episodic descriptions），中层为从多个情景中提炼的可复用流程（procedures），顶层为带显式适用条件（applicability conditions）的原理（principles）；三者在分层记忆中保持指向底层执行证据的链接，以支持溯源与更新。推理时，Agent依据当前任务检索相关原理，检索结果被实例化为任务特定指导，并用于对候选动作施加纠正性反馈与重采样，从而调节最终动作选择。执行产生的新轨迹再次进入抽象流程，更新分层记忆，形成执行—抽象闭环。该设计在无需访问模型参数、不做微调的前提下实现经验积累，解决了外部记忆知识过度耦合特定任务与上下文、泛化能力不足的问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务成功率", "value": "在ScienceWorld与ALFWorld上任务表现提升（论文未在摘要中给出具体数值）", "setting": "ScienceWorld、ALFWorld交互式文本环境基准实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出SAGA框架，将交互轨迹逐级抽象为情景描述、可复用流程和带显式适用条件的原理，并保留与执行证据的链接；检索到的原理被实例化为任务特定指导，通过纠正性反馈与候选动作重采样来调节动作；构建执行-抽象反馈回路，使积累知识指导后续交互、新经验持续更新分层记忆；消融验证上下文实例化与动作调节对利用原理级知识的关键作用

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in interactive environments, yet their ability to continually evolve from experience remains limited. Although fine-tuning enables adaptation, its dependence on parameter access and high computational costs restrict its flexibility, especially for large-scale and closed-source LLMs. External memory offers an alternative by allowing agents to accumulate experience without modifying model parameters. However, existing methods mainly focus on experience representation and organization, while the acquired knowledge remains tightly coupled with specific tasks and contexts, limiting generalization. A key challenge is how to transform concrete interactions into abstract and reusable knowledge that guides future decisions beyond individual experiences. To address this challenge, we propose SAGA (\underline{\textbf{S}}elf-evolving \underline{\textbf{A}}gents through Experience-\underline{\textbf{G}}rounded \underline{\textbf{A}}bstraction), a framework for experience-grounded knowledge abstraction and utilization in LLM agents. SAGA progressively transforms interaction trajectories into episodic descriptions, reusable procedures, and principles with explicit applicability conditions, while maintaining links to execution evidence. Retrieved principles are instantiated into task-specific guidance and used to refine candidate actions through corrective feedback and resampling. This creates an execution--abstraction feedback loop, where accumulated knowledge guides future interactions and new experiences continuously update hierarchical memory. Experiments on ScienceWorld and ALFWorld demonstrate improved task performance, with ablation studies highlighting the importance of contextual instantiation and action regulation for leveraging principle-level knowledge.

</details>


### [105] [Anchor Divergence for Semantic Geometry in Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06919)
*Akash Kannan,Kiho Park,Victor Veitch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文从信息几何角度出发，揭示对比学习表征天然蕴含一族几何结构，提出通过锚点分布建模来指定上下文相关语义相似度的Anchor Divergences方法，并在检索任务上验证了其有效性。方法理论新颖，对向量检索和推荐系统的相似度度量有潜在启发，但缺少工业部署和大规模实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 余弦相似度提供固定的几何结构，但语义相似度依赖于上下文；如何为固定的对比学习表征指定上下文相关的语义几何，从而提升检索等任务的相似度匹配效果。

Method: 论文提出的Anchor Divergences框架理论上建立在对比学习、指数族与信息几何的融合之上。输入为已经通过对比学习训练好的固定图像表征。核心模块对应关系是：定义在表征空间上的锚点概率分布，与由该分布诱导的Bregman几何一一对应。具体来说，利用指数族与信息几何的性质，将锚点分布映射为一种Bregman散度，该散度刻画了在特定语义上下文下的距离几何。训练或推理时，给定一组锚点（例如代表不同语义结构的样本），通过拟合锚点分布来建模几何本身。推理阶段，在固定表征上使用选定的Anchor Divergence计算相似度，可灵活适配同物体、同风格、同临床发现等不同语义相似度定义。论文未披露具体网络架构、训练目标细节、数据规模、模型参数或部署资源，因此无法提供更细粒度的端到端描述。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 建立对比学习表征中锚点概率分布与Bregman几何之间的对应关系，将距离度量学习转化为锚点分布建模。；提出Anchor Divergences方法，可在固定表征上定义上下文特定的语义相似度度量。；在检索任务上验证了该度量方法高效且有效，能够灵活指定不同语义结构下的相似性。

Abstract: This paper concerns how semantic context determines geometry in learned vector representations. Similarity is typically measured using cosine similarity, which provides a single fixed geometry. Semantic similarity, however, is inherently context dependent: two images may be similar because they depict the same object, share a visual style, or are relevant to the same clinical finding. We show that contrastive representations naturally encompass a family of geometries that can be specialized to particular semantic structure. The key idea is to use an interplay between contrastive learning, exponential families, and information geometry to establish a correspondence between probability distributions over "anchors" and Bregman geometries on the representation space. We use this correspondence to define "Anchor Divergences", a method for specifying context-specific semantic geometries on fixed representations. Under this correspondence, modeling the anchor distribution models the geometry itself. Experiments on retrieval show that anchor divergences provide an effective and efficient way to specify context-specific semantic similarity.

</details>


### [106] [Text2Dashboard: A Governed Agent Architecture for Natural-Language Dashboard Generation over Enterprise DataBrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06914)
*Yiou Wu,Zezhi Tang,Ningwei Bai,Liuhaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Text2Dashboard 是以 DataBrain 企业数据为背景的受治理 Agent 原型，用类型化工具、持久化状态和确定性 Hooks（审批、审计、检查点、恢复）约束 LLM 提议，在冻结任务与故障注入上取得初步结果，但规模小、未证明生产可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在企业数据环境中，让 LLM 提议动作的同时由确定性软件控制执行，保证 SQL 只读、可审计、可恢复、抗故障，并生成可检查的仪表盘。

Method: 系统由可安装的 Codex 插件与独立 Agent Runtime 组成。输入为自然语言分析请求；Agent Runtime 结合 schema 约束的模型决策与类型化工具，并维护持久化状态。处理流程为：先解析实体与发现元数据（元数据选择任务），再在只读 SQL 约束下生成查询，随后组合仪表盘，并经过静态检查、动态预检与浏览器内审查。模型仅负责提议动作，确定性 Hooks 控制执行、记录状态转换并处理审批、审计、检查点、恢复与失败处理。训练目标与优化细节未披露；推理时，所有动作经 Hooks 治理流水线执行并记录。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "strict success", "value": "6/8", "setting": "冻结的 DataBrain 元数据与 SQL 任务"}, {"metric": "metadata selection pass rate", "value": "4/4", "setting": "冻结的 DataBrain 元数据任务"}, {"metric": "semantic criteria pass rate", "value": "4/4", "setting": "四个 SQL 任务"}, {"metric": "exact output-column contract pass rate", "value": "2/4", "setting": "四个 SQL 任务"}, {"metric": "final release single-panel dashboard tasks", "value": "4/4", "setting": "最终版本单面板仪表盘任务"}, {"metric": "final release two-panel dashboard tasks", "value": "1/1", "setting": "最终版本双面板仪表盘任务"}, {"metric": "final release existing-dashboard refinement", "value": "1/1", "setting": "最终版本已有仪表盘改进任务"}, {"metric": "fault scenarios meeting specified outcomes", "value": "10/10", "setting": "受控 Hook 故障注入场景，无不批准的外部副作用"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 模型提议 + 确定性 Hooks 治理架构：审批、审计、检查点、恢复与失败处理由确定性软件控制，模型不直接执行。；类型化工具与持久化状态结合，支持只读 SQL 强制、元数据发现、实体解析、仪表盘组合与静态检查。；静态检查、动态预检与浏览器内审查三层验证流程，以及针对 Hook 故障的受控故障注入实验。

Abstract: Text2Dashboard is a DataBrain-specific prototype that turns natural-language analytic requests into inspectable dashboards. An installable Codex plugin and standalone Agent Runtime combine schema-constrained model decisions with typed tools, persistent state, and deterministic Hooks for approval, audit, checkpointing, recovery, and failure handling. The pipeline resolves entities, discovers metadata, enforces read-only SQL, composes dashboards, and applies static checks, dynamic preflight, and browser inspection. The model proposes actions while deterministic software controls execution and records state transitions. We evaluate the workflow on frozen real-DataBrain tasks and controlled Hook faults. Strict success was 6/8 on metadata and SQL tasks: metadata selection passed 4/4, all four SQL tasks met semantic criteria, and 2/4 met the exact output-column contract. The final release passed 4/4 single-panel dashboard tasks, one two-panel task, and one existing-dashboard refinement; a parameterised task exceeded its step limit. All ten fault scenarios met their specified outcomes without unapproved external side effects. Model inference accounted for over 97\% of observed runtime in every reported group. These small, DataBrain-specific results do not establish production readiness, general text-to-SQL accuracy, or an efficiency advantage over manual dashboard construction.

</details>


### [107] [FluidPD: In-Place Elasticity for SLO-Aware Prefill-Decode Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06917)
*Kartik Ramesh,Kaidi Fu,Zihan Zheng,Jiahuan Yu,Fabio Oliveira,Carlos Costa,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FluidPD 提出 SLO 感知的原地弹性机制，通过 FluidToken 和 FluidRole 动态调整 prefill/decode 工作负载，在生产 Azure trace 上将 SLO 达成率最高提升 94.6 个百分点，无需额外 GPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 prefill-decode 分离的 LLM 服务中，固定 prefill/decode 工作节点比例难以适应工作负载的突发和持续变化，导致 SLO 违反，而现有自动扩缩容反应慢且需要额外 GPU。

Method: FluidPD 是面向 P/D 分离 LLM 服务系统的 SLO 感知原地弹性框架。系统输入为生产 Azure 请求 trace，包含 prefill 和 decode 阶段的需求波动。核心模块包括轻量级压力指数计算、FluidToken 和 FluidRole。压力指数实时监测 prefill 和 decode 侧的资源压力，在出现 SLO 违反前发出信号。FluidToken 在检测到 decode 侧有闲置资源且 prefill 侧压力高时，将一部分 prefill 计算卸载到 decode worker 上执行，以平滑瞬时负载波动。FluidRole 在检测到持续性的 prefill/decode 需求比例偏移时，将正在运行的 worker 原地重新分配角色（prefill 与 decode 之间切换），避免模型重新加载和引擎重启带来的开销和延迟。系统通过压力指数驱动两种机制协同工作，实现不增加额外 worker 的情况下提升 SLO 达成率。推理流程中，请求经过路由后由当前角色 worker 处理，并在运行中动态调整资源分配。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "SLO 达成率", "value": "提升最高 94.6 个百分点", "setting": "生产 Azure trace 工作负载，与静态 SGLang 配置对比"}]}

Conclusion: FluidToken：利用 decode 侧空闲资源，将部分 prefill 计算卸载到 decode worker，动态处理瞬时相位不平衡。；FluidRole：在不重启引擎、不重新加载模型的情况下，原地切换运行中 worker 的角色，以应对持续的相位不平衡。；轻量级压力指数：提前暴露 prefill 和 decode 侧资源压力，指导弹性决策，避免 SLO 违反。

Abstract: Prefill-decode disaggregation is becoming a common architecture for LLM serving because it separates two phases with distinct execution patterns and SLO objectives. Existing systems typically combine a fixed prefill/decode worker ratio with request routing across workers. However, real-world workloads exhibit both short bursts and sustained shifts in the prefill-to-decode demand ratio. As a result, a configuration that is well provisioned at one time may quickly become mismatched, causing latency SLO violations even when idle capacity exists elsewhere. Existing autoscaling mechanisms can add capacity, but they react slowly, require spare GPUs, and do not directly address short-timescale phase imbalance. We present FluidPD, a P/D-disaggregated serving system that provides SLO-aware in-place elasticity. FluidPD introduces two complementary mechanisms. FluidToken handles transient imbalance by offloading a bounded portion of prefill computation to decode workers when decode-side slack is available. FluidRole handles sustained imbalance by reassigning running workers between prefill and decode roles in place, avoiding model reload and engine restart. Both mechanisms are guided by lightweight pressure indices that expose prefill and decode-side resource pressure before they appear as SLO violations. Across production Azure trace workloads, FluidPD improves overall SLO attainment over static SGLang by up to 94.6 percentage points, demonstrating that SLO-aware in-place P/D elasticity improves service quality without provisioning additional workers.

</details>


### [108] [Smart Content Ingestion for Generative AI Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07091)
*Abbas Raza Ali,Muhammad Ajmal Siddiqui,Moona Zahid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将企业异构文档的内容提取定位为生成式AI系统的独立生命周期阶段，提出包含选择性OCR路由、参考式提取评分、结构感知父子分块和只读检索评估的生产就绪摄入系统，并在180篇文档、25,050个生成问题上报告了提取与检索的量化结果，对企业RAG数据侧工程有直接参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI中模型与任务解耦后，企业数据的异构性与低质量表示破坏了模型推理，内容提取阶段的错误无法被下游检索器或重排器修复；需要将内容提取变为显式、可配置、可度量的生命周期阶段。

Method: 系统面向生成式AI工作负载的内容摄入流水线，输入为企业异构文档（PDF、演示文稿、电子表格、扫描件、表单、表格、图表及混合版式文件）。处理流程按顺序为：首先通过选择性OCR路由判断文档中哪些页面或区域需要OCR，仅对必要部分启用识别，以减少噪声与计算；随后进入稀缺优先的策展引擎，并使用参考式提取评分器，将提取结果与参考文本对齐，分别计算字符级、词语级以及表格结构准确率，形成可比较的提取质量分数；然后由确定性结构感知父子分块器对已提取内容进行切分，保持文档结构与层级关系，生成父块与子块以支持下游检索；最后使用只读检索评估器，从每个页面生成有据可依（grounded）的问题，据此计算Hit@k、平均倒数排名（MRR）和延迟，且评估过程不修改被测对象。整个系统是配置化、可度量的，核心目标是在数据摄入界面保证表示质量，使下游检索器与重排器能够在正确表示上工作。文中未披露具体的模型架构、训练目标与优化方法。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "提取综合得分", "value": "97.4/100", "setting": "180篇文档语料，最佳提取器"}, {"metric": "字符错误率（CER）", "value": "0.13%", "setting": "180篇文档语料，最佳提取器"}, {"metric": "表格相似度", "value": "0.995", "setting": "180篇文档语料，最佳提取器"}, {"metric": "hit@1", "value": "68.6%", "setting": "结构感知父子分块器，25,050个自动生成问题"}, {"metric": "hit@10", "value": "92.8%", "setting": "结构感知父子分块器，25,050个自动生成问题"}, {"metric": "MRR", "value": "0.77", "setting": "结构感知父子分块器，25,050个自动生成问题"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 选择性OCR路由：按需对文档页面/区域启用OCR，降低不必要的计算与噪声；稀缺优先的策展引擎（scarcity-first curation engine）；参考式提取评分器：同时度量字符、词语与表格结构准确率；确定性结构感知的父子分块器（structure-aware parent-child chunker）；只读检索评估器：从每页生成有据可依的问题，报告Hit@k、MRR与延迟；提出三条设计原则：结构先于语义、绝不篡改被度量对象、预算化标注

Abstract: The evolution of machine learning has progressively changed where intelligence resides in an AI system. In conventional machine learning the task, data representation, labels and model architecture were tightly coupled, so data preparation was narrow, schema-bound and visible. Generative AI decouples the model from any single task: one foundation model serves open-ended downstream tasks, and the generality gained on the model side is matched by heterogeneity on the data side, because enterprise knowledge is authored in the formats people use (PDF, presentations, spreadsheets, scanned documents, forms, tables, diagrams and mixed-layout files) that carry textual, visual, geometric and structural information at once. A language model or retriever cannot reason reliably over information misrepresented at this interface, so content extraction becomes a lifecycle stage in its own right whose errors no downstream retriever or re-ranker can repair. This paper presents a production-ready content-extraction system that makes this stage explicit, configurable, and measurable. The system incorporates selective OCR routing, a scarcity-first curation engine with a reference-based extraction scorer that measures character, word, and table-structure accuracy, a deterministic structure-aware parent-child chunker, and a read-only retrieval evaluator that generates grounded questions from every page and reports Hit@k, mean reciprocal rank, and latency. On a 180-document corpus the best extractor scores 97.4 of 100 (character error rate 0.13%, table similarity 0.995) and the chunker reaches hit@1 of 68.6%, hit@10 of 92.8% and MRR 0.77 over 25,050 generated questions. We distil three design principles (structure before semantics, never mutate what you measure, budget your labels) and position measured content extraction as the perception layer of enterprise agentic systems.

</details>

### [109] [Aligning Performance with Contribution: Towards Contribution-Aware Fair Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08245)
*Shuai Zhang,Hui Fang,Zun Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文提出贡献-性能公平性视角，并设计可插拔的 CPFR 框架，通过构建贡献有序用户组并联合优化准确率与公平性，在三个数据集和三个骨干模型上取得较好的准确率-公平性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在推荐系统中将用户对模型学习的贡献与其获得的推荐收益对齐，以激励用户持续提供有效反馈并维持推荐生态的可持续性。

Method: CPFR 框架适用于不同骨干推荐器。输入为用户-物品交互数据。首先，基于训练过程中产生的贡献信号构建有序用户组，贡献由交互量、损失对齐和优化强度三部分决定。然后，在推荐模型训练中联合优化推荐准确率与两项公平性约束：跨用户组的性能-贡献对齐和同一贡献组内用户间的公平性。最终输出推荐结果，同时满足性能与公平性要求。论文未披露具体的推理或服务流程细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率-公平性权衡", "value": "在三个数据集和三个骨干模型上取得较强的准确率-公平性权衡", "setting": "离线实验，使用所提出的操作化公平性指标"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出贡献-性能公平性（Contribution-Performance Fairness）这一新的公平性视角，要求推荐性能跨用户组与估计贡献对齐，且同贡献组内用户间公平。；提出贡献-性能公平推荐器（CPFR）框架，可适配不同骨干推荐模型；通过交互量、损失对齐和优化强度构建有序用户组，并联合优化推荐准确率与两项公平性要求。；通过博弈论分析表明，这种对齐可以增强贡献激励并在自愿贡献下提升系统级推荐准确率。

Abstract: Existing research on user fairness in recommender systems has developed diverse objectives. However, it has paid limited attention to a distinct distributive perspective: whether users' contributions to model learning should be reflected in the recommendation benefits they receive. We argue that, in addition to existing fairness protections, a fair system may account for the alignment between users' estimated contributions and the recommendation performance they receive. Such alignment can incentivize sustained and informative engagement, thereby supporting a sustainable recommendation ecosystem. To this end, we propose Contribution-Performance Fairness, a novel fairness perspective which requires recommendation performance to be aligned with estimated contribution across user groups and to remain equitable among users with comparable contributions within a same group. To instantiate this perspective, we introduce the Contribution-Performance Fair Recommender (CPFR), a framework applicable to different backbone recommenders. CPFR constructs ordered user groups from a training-dependent contribution considering interaction volume, loss alignment, and optimization intensity, and jointly optimizes recommendation accuracy with the two fairness requirements. A game-theoretic analysis shows that such alignment can strengthen contribution incentives and improve system-level recommendation accuracy under voluntary contribution. Experiments on three datasets and three backbone models demonstrate that CPFR achieves a strong accuracy--fairness trade-off under the proposed operational metric.

</details>


### [110] [Seeing the Context: Enhancing Recommender Systems with Image-Derived Contextual Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08407)
*Tal Cordova,Tomer Geva,Moshe Unger*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出从图像中提取物理、社交和模态情境上下文的新表示，并通过 ICE-Fuse 流程将其融合到上下文感知推荐系统中。在 TripAdvisor 数据上与 Review-aware Graph Contrastive Learning 结合，发现图像上下文虽单独效果不佳，但能补充已有信号，提升推荐性能。该工作为图像在推荐系统上下文建模中的应用提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统主要从位置、时间或评论中提取上下文，而图像仅用于丰富物品或用户表示，很少被用于挖掘用户-物品交互的情境上下文。论文探索如何从图像中提取物理、社交和模态情境表示，并将其融入上下文感知推荐系统以提升性能。

Method: ICE-Fuse 是一个评估图像派生上下文表示的流程。首先使用视觉语言模型从图像中提取三类上下文信号：物理上下文（如环境场景）、社交上下文（如人群互动）和模态上下文（如图像风格/模态特性）。然后将这三类表示进行融合，形成图像上下文表示。该表示被集成到基于 Review-aware Graph Contrastive Learning 的上下文感知推荐系统中，与评论等已有上下文信号结合，共同用于用户-物品交互建模。推荐算法基于图对比学习框架，利用评论感知的图结构进行表示学习。论文未详细披露训练目标、优化细节及推理流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推荐性能（未指明具体指标）", "value": "图像上下文单独使用时不如已有信号，但融合后能提升已有信号的表现", "setting": "TripAdvisor 数据集，Review-aware Graph Contrastive Learning 推荐算法"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出一种新的图像派生上下文表示，涵盖物理、社交和模态三种类别，通过视觉语言模型学习。；提出 ICE-Fuse 流程，将图像上下文类别融合并集成到基于 Review-aware Graph Contrastive Learning 的上下文感知推荐系统中。；通过语义分析表明图像和评论派生的上下文捕捉交互的不同方面，图像上下文作为互补信号提升已有信号的效果。

Abstract: Contextual information, capturing the circumstances of a user-item interaction, is central to recommender systems. Prior work draws context from location, time, or reviews, but not images; multimodal recommender systems mainly use images to enrich item or user representations, not identify situational context. We propose a new representation of context derived from images, spanning physical, social, and modal categories learned via a vision-language model. We introduce ICE-Fuse, a pipeline for evaluating this representation that fuses these categories and integrates them into a context-aware recommender system, using TripAdvisor data and Review-aware Graph Contrastive Learning as the recommendation algorithm. Image context does not outperform established signals standalone, but improves them combined, indicating complementary information. Semantic analysis shows image- and review-derived context capture distinct aspects of the interaction, positioning images as complementary context.

</details>


### [111] [Disentangling Paradigm, Identifier, and Decoding in Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08716)
*Hicham Randrianarivo,Logan Renaud,Alexia Allal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该工作系统解耦生成式检索中的范式、标识符与解码三个变量，在 NQ320K 与 MS300K 上受控对比自回归与扩散检索器，发现解码策略单独可带来 6.6-13.7 点 Hit@1 变化，并揭示随机标识符仍保留大部分性能，提示范式比较必须在各自最优配方与解码下进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生成式检索中，先前工作同时改变解码范式、文档标识符和训练配方，导致无法归因性能差异；论文旨在解耦范式（自回归 vs 扩散）、标识符类型（残差量化、乘积量化、随机）与解码策略对检索效果的影响。

Method: 论文将生成式检索建模为：给定查询，语言模型生成相关文档的标识符（identifier），再通过标识符映射回文档。实验在 NQ320K 与 MS300K 上进行，固定标识符长度与训练预算，以隔离变量。文档标识符有三种构造：残差量化（residual-quantised）、乘积量化（product-quantised）与随机标识符。模型范式包括自回归解码器、掩码扩散与块扩散。解码阶段对比多种策略：generate-and-match 先生成标识符，再检索语料中最接近的标识符；one-pass scoring 让模型一次性读取完全掩码的标识符，并用各文档编码的概率为其打分，从而在一次前向中完成对候选文档的评分。训练目标与优化细节未在摘要中披露。推理路径分为生成式解码与打分式解码两类，实验表明解码选择对扩散检索器影响巨大，且 one-pass scoring 在多设置下可匹配或超过 generate-and-match，但仍落后于自回归加 beam search。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Hit@1", "value": "解码方式单独可使扩散模型 Hit@1 变化 6.6 至 13.7 个点", "setting": "NQ320K 与 MS300K，固定标识符长度与训练预算"}, {"metric": "生成标识符正确率", "value": "14-21%", "setting": "NQ320K 查询，generate-and-match 扩散解码"}, {"metric": "one-pass scoring 相对 generate-and-match", "value": "在 12 个设置中的 11 个匹配或超过", "setting": "扩散检索器解码对比"}, {"metric": "从单个采样标识符出发，one-pass scoring 消除掩码扩散相对 beam search 的差距", "value": "46-83%", "setting": "NQ320K"}, {"metric": "从 generate-and-match 出发，one-pass scoring 消除差距", "value": "最多四分之一", "setting": "NQ320K"}, {"metric": "随机标识符相对残差量化标识符的 Hit@1 保留比例", "value": "83-90%", "setting": "NQ320K"}, {"metric": "乘积量化相对残差量化标识符的 Hit@1 领先", "value": "自回归模型 +3.4 点；扩散模型 -0.7 至 +3.6 点", "setting": "NQ320K"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在固定标识符长度与训练预算下，对自回归、掩码扩散、块扩散三种范式配以残差量化、乘积量化、随机标识符进行受控对比；提出并评估多种扩散解码方式，包括 generate-and-match 与 one-pass scoring；发现解码方式单独可使扩散模型 Hit@1 变化 6.6 至 13.7 个点；one-pass scoring 在 12 个设置中的 11 个达到或超过 generate-and-match；揭示随机标识符仍保留残差量化标识符 83-90% 的 Hit@1，说明范式在 NQ320K 上主要是在记忆查询到标识符的映射

Abstract: Generative retrieval trains a language model to generate the identifier of a relevant document. Recent work replaces the autoregressive decoder with diffusion, but changes identifiers, training recipe and decoding at once, so differences cannot be credited to the paradigm. On NQ320K and MS300K, we train autoregressive, masked-diffusion and block-diffusion models with residual-quantised, product-quantised and random identifiers. With identifier length and training budget fixed, we decode each model in several ways. Decoding alone moves a diffusion model's Hit@1 by 6.6 to 13.7 points. Our reference diffusion decoding, generate-and-match, generates an identifier, then retrieves the closest corpus identifiers. The generated identifier is right for 14-21% of NQ320K queries. We test one-pass scoring to decode diffusion retrievers: the model reads a fully masked identifier once, and each document is scored by its codes' probabilities. It matches or beats generate-and-match in 11 of 12 settings. Autoregressive models still lead in Hit@1; on NQ320K, the lead comes from the model, not beam search. Starting from one sampled identifier, one-pass scoring removes 46-83% of masked diffusion's deficit to beam search; from generate-and-match, at most a quarter. On NQ320K, every paradigm largely memorises which identifier answers which query: random identifiers keep 83-90% of the Hit@1 of residual-quantised ones. There, product-quantised identifiers lead residual-quantised ones by 3.4 points in the autoregressive model and by -0.7 to +3.6 in diffusion models; across decodings, AR's gap exceeds diffusion's by 1.5-2.3 points, around our 2-point threshold. Paradigm comparisons must report each paradigm at its own recipe and best decoding.

</details>


### [112] [Behavior-Mining, Generative Conversations, and Collaborative Advisory: the Future of Travel and Tourism Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08232)
*Alejandro Bellogín,Linus W. Dietz,Francesco Ricci,Pablo Sánchez*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 一篇探讨旅游推荐系统未来方向的立场论文：指出TTRS落地受阻于数据、算法目标和旅行者需求三方面限制，并结合生成式AI趋势，提出构建融合多数据与AI技术的顾问式推荐系统，透明平衡多方利益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旅游与旅行推荐系统（TTRS）未能广泛落地，原因包括训练与验证数据陈旧稀疏、算法过度追求预测准确率而忽视新颖性与上下文相关性等领域维度、以及未能满足旅行者的具体需求；同时生成式AI正在成为旅行规划的主要入口，迫使TTRS重新设计。

Method: 论文为立场/综述论文，未提出具体系统架构。其核心论述为：现有TTRS依赖从案例推理到强化学习等多种数据驱动技术，可处理上下文相关、多利益相关方及可持续性（如过度旅游）等问题；但受限于数据陈旧稀疏、算法偏重准确率、未满足旅行者需求。未来方向是构建融合多类型数据与AI技术（数据挖掘、NLP等）的灵活顾问系统，在个性化信息过滤之外支持决策，并透明地平衡多方目标。论文未披露具体的模块、数据流、训练目标或推理路径。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出未来TTRS应从个性化信息过滤演变为更灵活的顾问式系统，支持决策过程并整合多种数据类型与AI技术（从数据挖掘到NLP）。；强调TTRS需透明地平衡旅行者、服务供应商、平台方与本地社区的多方冲突目标。；提出结合经典推荐技术与生成式AI的TTRS研究路线图。

Abstract: Since the early adoption of e-commerce, travel and tourism has been a lab for the design of recommender systems: tools that help travelers choose destinations, flights, accommodations, and combine them into itineraries. Data-driven recommendation techniques, ranging from case-based reasoning to reinforcement learning, have been adapted to travelers' needs. The research community has produced multifaceted prototypes of travel and tourism recommender systems (TTRSs), which are context-dependent, multistakeholder-oriented, and more recently, addressing sustainability issues, such as overtourism. Despite this enduring work, TTRSs are not widespread yet. We argue that three limitations can explain this: outdated and sparse data sets used to train and validate TTRSs, algorithms that prioritize prediction accuracy over domain-specific dimensions such as novelty and contextual relevance, and a failure to address the specific needs of travelers. Targeted incremental research could address these limitations, but a disruptive factor has meanwhile entered the ecosystem of tourism information and commercialization platforms: generative artificial intelligence. According to market research, GenAI applications are becoming the primary entry point for travelers planning their trips. This forces research to rethink how TTRSs should be designed and which core techniques should be integrated. We claim that future TTRSs, in addition to offering personalized information filtering, should become more flexible advisors that support decision making, integrating multiple data types and AI techniques, from data mining to natural language processing. Moreover, they must transparently balance the conflicting goals of travelers, service suppliers, platform owners, and local communities. We then outline research targets for building more effective TTRSs, fruitfully combining old and new recommendation techniques.

</details>


### [113] [Learning to Retrieve via Reinforcement Learning in Embedding Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07731)
*Qi Liu,Fengming Liang,Yiqun Chen,Erhan Zhang,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RELER，一种在嵌入空间中进行强化学习的稠密检索训练框架，通过vMF采样和条件均值投影直接优化检索指标与下游RAG奖励，在BRIGHT基准上优于InfoNCE和LambdaLoss。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对比学习（InfoNCE/LambdaLoss）只优化表征，不直接优化检索指标或下游任务表现，难以对齐任务特定奖励。

Method: RELER将稠密检索训练建模为强化学习问题。输入为查询和文档，编码器输出归一化嵌入。策略在单位球面上定义：以编码器输出为中心、使用von Mises-Fisher（vMF）分布采样单位长度的查询/文档嵌入动作。采样得到的嵌入用于计算检索分数或下游RAG结果，并得到任务奖励（如检索指标或答案质量）。使用REINFORCE算法，并以RLOO（leave-one-out）作为基线降低方差来更新编码器。为缓解高维嵌入空间采样噪声，提出条件均值投影（CMP）：将每个采样嵌入投影到由其编码器输出与候选嵌入张成的低维子空间中，在保持期望的同时减小策略梯度噪声。在RAG场景中，仅对查询编码器进行适配，文档索引和生成器保持固定，奖励由检索和答案质量联合构成，从而端到端对齐检索与生成目标。训练目标为最大化期望奖励；推理时使用更新后的编码器直接生成嵌入进行检索。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均nDCG@10", "value": "RELER consistently outperforms InfoNCE and LambdaLoss（未给出具体数值）", "setting": "BRIGHT基准，后训练BGE-M3和Qwen3-Embedding骨干模型"}, {"metric": "平均检索性能与答案质量", "value": "联合优化检索与答案奖励提升了平均检索性能和答案质量（未给出具体数值）", "setting": "7个QA数据集，仅调整查询编码器，固定文档索引与生成器"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: RELER：在嵌入空间中使用强化学习直接优化检索/下游奖励，采样单位长度查询与文档嵌入动作。；使用von Mises-Fisher（vMF）分布对归一化编码器输出进行动作采样，并以REINFORCE+RLOO基线更新编码器。；条件均值投影（CMP）：将采样嵌入投影到由编码器输出与候选嵌入张成的低维子空间，降低策略梯度噪声并保持期望。；在RAG中仅调整查询编码器，固定文档索引与生成器，联合优化检索与答案奖励。

Abstract: Dense retrieval models are typically trained with contrastive objectives that learn effective representations but do not directly optimize retrieval metrics or downstream task performance. To address this problem, we introduce RELER (REinforcement LEarning for Retrieval), a reinforcement learning framework that enables existing embedding models to learn to retrieve directly in embedding space and align to task-specific rewards. We train RELER by sampling unit-length query and document embedding actions from von Mises-Fisher (vMF) distributions centered on normalized encoder outputs, scoring the resulting retrieval or downstream outcomes as rewards, and updating the encoder with REINFORCE using a leave-one-out baseline (RLOO). As exploration in the high-dimensional embedding space is prone to sampling noise, we further propose conditional-mean projection (CMP), which projects each sampled embedding onto the low-dimensional subspace spanned by its encoder output and the candidate embeddings it is compared against, reducing noise in the policy gradient while preserving its expectation. We evaluate RELER on BRIGHT, a benchmark with reasoning-intensive queries that remain challenging for existing embedding models. RELER consistently outperforms InfoNCE and LambdaLoss in average nDCG@10 when post-training BGE-M3 and Qwen3-Embedding backbones. We further evaluate downstream utility through retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), where we adapt only the query encoder while keeping the document index and generator fixed. Across seven QA datasets, jointly optimizing retrieval and answer rewards improves both average retrieval performance and answer quality in RAG.

</details>


### [114] [DBRAG: Multi-Table Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Complex Database Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07622)
*Prince Larbi Ampofo,Ryoji Kubo,Djellel Difallah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DBRAG提出了一种多表检索增强生成框架，通过离线索引检索候选表、LLM重排序和程序辅助推理，提升复杂数据库查询的准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对整个数据库的多表复杂查询，现有方法多局限于单表或假设相关表已知，难以自动检索分散在多个关系中的相关信息。

Method: DBRAG是一个面向多表问答的检索增强生成框架。首先，通过离线表索引检索候选表；然后，利用查询相关行丰富候选表的摘要信息；接着，使用LLM对候选表进行重排序；最后，程序辅助推理器选择所需表，并对其完整内容执行操作，同时保持初始提示上下文紧凑。训练目标和推理流程未在摘要中详细说明。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "表检索性能", "value": "提升", "setting": "Spider, GeoQuery, ATIS 数据集"}, {"metric": "多表问答性能", "value": "提升", "setting": "Spider, GeoQuery, ATIS 数据集"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 离线表索引用于候选表检索；利用查询相关行丰富表摘要；基于LLM的候选表重排序；程序辅助推理器选择所需表并对其完整内容执行操作，保持初始提示上下文紧凑

Abstract: Recent advancements in large language models have introduced new capabilities for reasoning over structured data, particularly through program-aided tools that can analyze tables. However, many existing methods address single-table scenarios or assume that the relevant tables are already provided. In practice, users often issue complex data exploration queries over entire databases, where relevant information may be distributed across multiple relations. In this work, we introduce DBRAG, a retrieval-augmented generation framework tailored for multi-table question answering. DBRAG first retrieves candidate tables using an offline table index, enriches their summaries with query-relevant rows, and uses an LLM to rerank the candidates. A program-aided reasoner then selects the required tables and executes operations over their full contents, keeping the initial prompt context compact. Experiments on the Spider, GeoQuery, and ATIS datasets used in this study demonstrate improvements in table retrieval and multi-table question answering.

</details>


### [115] [Rethinking Semantic ID Construction for Generative Recommendation: SimHash with Parallel Decoding and Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07402)
*Yuqing Liu,Huiyuan Chen,Yibo Wang,Wooseong Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文挑战了SimHash在生成式推荐中劣于学习式量化的共识，提出FLASH框架，通过并行解码和显式语义对齐，使无需训练的SimHash tokenization达到SOTA性能，并在冷启动场景表现更强泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点认为基于哈希的语义ID（如SimHash）性能不如复杂学习量化方法，论文指出性能差距源于哈希tokenization与自回归解码的结构不匹配以及刚性离散化导致的信息损失，而非哈希本身。

Method: FLASH是一个两阶段框架。第一阶段采用无需训练的SimHash对物品进行tokenization，生成离散语义ID序列，替代复杂的学习式量化。第二阶段针对自回归解码的结构不匹配问题，引入并行解码机制，使解码过程与哈希tokenization的特性兼容；同时加入显式语义对齐，以补偿刚性离散化造成的信息损失。整个模型无需tokenizer训练，直接在生成式推荐框架下进行训练和推理。语义对齐机制被验证可跨多种范式通用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推荐性能", "value": "达到state-of-the-art", "setting": "多个数据集，无tokenizer训练"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 揭示SimHash语义ID性能差距的结构性原因：与自回归解码不匹配及刚性离散化信息损失；提出FLASH两阶段框架：无需训练的SimHash tokenization + 并行解码 + 显式语义对齐；证明语义对齐机制可跨不同范式通用；在冷启动场景下展现更强泛化能力

Abstract: Semantic ID-based generative recommendation represents each item as a sequence of discrete tokens, enabling structured modeling of item semantics. A critical challenge is constructing semantic IDs that are both semantically expressive and computationally efficient. While recent approaches favor complex learned quantization, simple hashing-based methods such as SimHash are widely regarded as fundamentally inferior. In this work, we challenge this consensus by showing that the apparent performance gap does not stem from inherent limitations of hashing, but rather from a structural mismatch with autoregressive decoding, coupled with the inevitable information loss during rigid discretization. Based on this insight, we propose FLASH, a two-stage framework that revitalizes training-free SimHash tokenization through parallel decoding and explicit semantic alignment. Despite its simplicity, FLASH achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple datasets without requiring any tokenizer training, while exhibiting stronger generalization in cold-start scenarios. Notably, we demonstrate that semantic alignment acts as a universally effective mechanism across diverse paradigms. Our findings suggest that, with compatible decoding and semantic grounding, simple and efficient tokenizers can achieve performance comparable to complex learned counterparts in generative recommendation. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [116] [Contrastive Learning for Aspect Representation towards Explainable Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07761)
*Emrul Hasan,Chen Ding*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CLARER将评分MLP特征与aspect级评论Transformer表示通过对比学习融合，并用Transformer解码器生成推荐解释，在三个基准数据集上报告优于基线，但未披露具体指标与生产验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何将用户评分信息与评论中的aspect级文本特征有效融合，以同时提升推荐准确率与解释质量。

Method: 模型输入包括用户-物品评分以及对应评论文本。评分路径通过多层感知机（MLP）学习基于评分的用户和物品特征表示。文本路径使用Transformer编码器从评论中学习aspect级别的表示，并引入对比学习目标，使不同用户偏好下的aspect表示更具区分度。最终将评分特征与aspect特征组合为用户和物品的联合表示，用于评分预测任务（推荐准确率）。在解释生成阶段，以用户和物品的联合表示作为上下文，训练Transformer解码器生成自然语言解释。论文在三个基准数据集上评估，报告在推荐准确率和解释生成上优于基线方法，但未披露具体数值、模型参数量、训练资源或在线部署信息。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 融合评分MLP特征与基于Transformer编码器的aspect级评论表示；使用对比学习增强aspect表示以区分用户偏好；使用Transformer解码器以用户和物品的评分与aspect联合表示为上下文生成解释

Abstract: In this work, we propose a novel recommendation model, CLARER (Contrastive Learning for Aspect Representation towards Explainable Recommendation) that integrates aspect features learned from textual reviews with rating information to improve the accuracy and explainability of recommendations. Our proposed framework learns user and item representations by combining rating-based features and aspect-based features from reviews. Specifically, rating-based features are learned through a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) model, while aspect-specific review representations are learned using a transformer encoder to capture the semantic information and contrastive learning to better distinguish user preferences. To provide explanations, we train a transformer decoder, using the final representations of users and items from both rating and aspect-based features as context. Experimental results in three benchmark data sets demonstrate that our model achieves superior performance compared to baseline methods in both recommendation (accuracy) and explanation generation.

</details>


### [117] [RAGFlip: Measuring Query-Level Negative Flips in Retriever Upgrades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07266)
*Elyas Irankhah,Muhammad Arif*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RAGFlip研究检索器升级中的查询级负翻转：BM25能命中而替换检索器（BGE-large、E5-large-v2、SPLADE）不能命中的查询。在三个BEIR数据集、五个深度上，k=1时8.6%-37.5%的BM25成功被丢失，且随语料、检索器与深度显著变化；与BM25的固定预算组合可减少回归，HotpotQA上部分检索翻转伴随答案回归，说明升级评测需同时看聚合质量与查询级兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索器升级通常只用聚合指标评估，会掩盖旧检索器已正确服务的查询上的回归；论文将这类回归定义为查询级负翻转，并系统测量其规模与影响因素。

Method: 论文不提出新模型架构，而是构建评测流程：以BM25作为被替换的基线检索器，在Natural Questions、HotpotQA、FiQA三个BEIR集合上，分别用BGE-large、E5-large-v2、SPLADE作为替换检索器；在每个检索深度k（共五个深度）上，先按BM25在该深度是否检索到已判定相关段落定义支持查询集合，再检查替换检索器在同一深度是否仍然检索到相关段落，从而计算负翻转率；支持标签采用any-relevant，另在HotpotQA k=10上采用every-positive qrel变体；随后测试将替换检索器与BM25做固定预算融合以降低回归；最后在HotpotQA上用一个reader模型检查检索翻转是否伴随答案回归。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出查询级负翻转（negative flips）作为检索器升级的兼容性评测指标；在BM25与BGE-large、E5-large-v2、SPLADE之间跨三个BEIR数据集、五个检索深度的系统测量；区分any-relevant与every-positive qrel两种支持标签对负翻转率的影响；验证与BM25的固定预算组合可降低负翻转，并用HotpotQA reader做下游答案回归的有限检查

Abstract: Retriever upgrades are typically evaluated using aggregate metrics, which can hide regressions on queries the previous retriever already served correctly. We study these regressions as negative flips: queries for which BM25 retrieves a judged relevant passage and the replacement does not. We evaluate BGE-large, E5-large-v2, and SPLADE on three BEIR collections: Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and FiQA, across five retrieval depths. All replacements improve overall retrieval coverage. Negative flips occur in every setting and vary substantially by corpus, retriever, and depth. At k=1, 8.6-37.5% of BM25 successes are lost across the evaluated settings. Negative-flip rates are lower in the larger-depth settings, where the BM25-supported cohort is defined separately at each depth. These rates use the any-relevant support label. On HotpotQA at k=10, requiring every positive qrel passage raises the negative-flip rate to 12.3-17.3%. Simple fixed-budget combinations with BM25 reduce these regressions, and a HotpotQA reader experiment provides a limited downstream check in which some retrieval flips are accompanied by answer regressions. These results motivate evaluating retriever updates using query-level compatibility alongside aggregate retrieval quality.

</details>


### [118] [From Delivery to Stateful Exploration: Rethinking the Index for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07960)
*Deogyong Kim,Sunghwan Kim,Sangam Lee,Wonjae Lee,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: IndexAct提出一种索引原生的语料交互接口，将候选集精炼与文本检查解耦。智能体通过倒排索引上的词法条件和集合操作维护持久候选集，接收状态引用和候选统计反馈，按需请求段落。在五个智能体搜索与多跳问答基准上优于基线，BrowseComp-Plus上以更小上下文实现更高证据覆盖，且答案准确率随语料扩展保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体搜索接口在返回匹配段落时，将候选集精炼与源文本暴露耦合，导致智能体需要处理大量文本而非仅获得候选集反馈，限制了探索效率与上下文管理。论文旨在解耦候选集精炼与文本检查，让智能体仅通过索引操作和统计反馈来操纵候选集。

Method: IndexAct构建于倒排索引之上，智能体通过词法条件（如关键词、布尔表达式）和集合操作（交、并、差等）来构造与维护持久候选集。系统不直接返回匹配段落，而是返回可重用的状态引用以及候选集统计信息（如候选数量），智能体根据这些反馈进行下一轮精炼操作。当需要证据或线索时，智能体可显式请求特定段落，这些段落可帮助形成后续操作，而保留的候选集状态仍可继续使用。整个流程形成候选集持续精炼与按需文本检查的分离循环，从而减少实时上下文占用并支持持续的探索。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能（各基准综合）", "value": "在五个基准上均优于评估基线", "setting": "五个基准涵盖智能体搜索与多跳问答"}, {"metric": "证据覆盖率", "value": "高于基于终端的语料接口，且平均实时上下文更小", "setting": "BrowseComp-Plus基准"}, {"metric": "答案准确率", "value": "随语料扩展保持稳定", "setting": "语料规模扩展实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出IndexAct接口，实现索引原生语料交互，将候选集精炼与文本检查分离；智能体通过词法条件与集合操作在倒排索引上构造并维护持久候选集；返回可重用状态引用与候选集统计（如候选数量）而非匹配段落；支持按需请求段落作为证据，并可在保留的候选集上继续操作；实验证明在多个智能体搜索与多跳问答基准上优于基线，并在BrowseComp-Plus上以更小实时上下文实现更高证据覆盖

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic search have given large language model (LLM) agents finer control over corpus exploration. However, search interfaces often return matching passages even when feedback about the candidate set would suffice for the next decision, coupling candidate refinement with source-text exposure. We propose IndexAct, an interface for Index-Native Corpus Interaction that separates candidate-set refinement from text inspection. Agents construct and manipulate persistent candidate sets through lexical conditions and set operations over an inverted index, receiving reusable state references and statistics such as candidate counts rather than matching passages. This feedback guides further refinement, while separately requested passages provide new clues or evidence that can inform subsequent operations on retained candidate sets. Experiments on five benchmarks spanning agentic search and multi-hop question answering show that IndexAct outperforms the evaluated baselines on each benchmark. On BrowseComp-Plus, it also achieves higher evidence coverage with a smaller average live context than terminal-based corpus interfaces, and maintains answer accuracy as the corpus expands. Further analyses suggest that informative refinement feedback and state reuse support continued evidence discovery, while shorter contexts or fewer search steps alone do not ensure better performance.

</details>


### [119] [Adapting Generative Recommenders for Multi-Turn Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08136)
*Yu-Chen Den,Zhi Rui Tam,Yung-Yu Shih,Shih-Hsin Wang,Yun-Nung Chen,Pu-Jen Cheng,Eugene Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: INTEGER将生成式推荐与多轮对话结合，通过路由token、历史重锚定和行为回放解决对话适配中的遗忘问题，在Amazon Beauty/Toys上Hit@10提升13.3%并保持对话质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐器无法让用户通过对话修正推荐意图；直接引入对话训练会导致历史到物品的映射被覆盖（灾难性遗忘），从而损失推荐准确率。

Method: INTEGER扩展生成式推荐模型以支持多轮交互。模型输入包括用户历史交互序列和对话上下文。核心模块包括：1）一个可学习的路由token，嵌入在输出空间，用于让模型决定当前步骤是生成推荐物品还是生成对话文本；2）历史重锚定机制，在生成物品时不仅条件于对话上下文，还重新注入用户行为历史作为条件，确保推荐结果与历史偏好一致；3）训练时采用行为回放（behavior replay）和指令数据排练（instruction-data rehearsal），在微调对话能力的同时保留原始推荐映射。推理时，模型根据路由token的决策交替进行对话回复或推荐物品，整个流程端到端可微。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Hit@10", "value": "提升13.3%", "setting": "Amazon Beauty数据集，与最强基线比较"}, {"metric": "准确率与对话质量", "value": "匹配或超越最强基线", "setting": "Amazon Beauty和Toys数据集"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 可学习路由token：让模型自主决定何时进行推荐还是继续对话；历史重锚定（history re-anchoring）：在生成物品时同时条件于历史行为和当前对话；行为回放与指令数据排练：在适配对话能力时防止遗忘历史到物品的映射

Abstract: Generative recommenders decode items from a user's interaction history, but offer no way for users to correct a recommendation that misses their current intent. Adding conversation is natural since items and words share same output space, yet training the model to converse may overwrite the history-to-item mapping it relies on. We introduce INTEGER (**INTE**ractive **GE**nerative **R**ecommendation), which extends generative recommendation to multi-turn interaction with a learned routing token that lets the model decide when to recommend, history re-anchoring that conditions each item on both past behavior and the dialogue, and behavioral replay with instruction-data rehearsal that prevents forgetting during adaptation. Users can thus give feedback on recommendations within the dialogue, while recommendations stay grounded in behavioral history and accuracy is not traded for fluency. On Amazon Beauty and Toys, INTEGER matches or exceeds the strongest baselines in accuracy with competitive conversation quality, improving Hit@10 by 13.3% on Amazon Beauty, and significantly outperforms the generative recommender it starts from. Our analyses show that INTEGER learns behaviors that naive adaptation fails to acquire, recommending once the user's intent is clear and staying attentive to behavioral history at the moment of recommendation. INTEGER also learns an intent-agnostic replacement over the item space, which suppresses rejected items but points to attribute-aware feedback as the next step.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond Successor Accuracy: State Retention for Recursive Self-Improvement in Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07105)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Ziyue Peng,Zheng Lin,Wenhao Yuan,Jian Chen,Shujie Li,Edith Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出推荐系统递归自我改进中的分布式进展概念，用跨代优势（CGA）量化，并提出秩分离统计量作为无标签选择指标，实验表明不同编码器偏好不同的保留机制，在多个数据集上选择效果优于直接后继。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在推荐系统递归自我改进中，仅用最新模型评估每一轮更新会忽略前后模型互补的排序决策（分布式进展），需要判断保留哪一代模型状态。

Method: 论文研究递归自我改进（Rec-RSI）中的状态保留问题。输入为推荐模型在迭代训练中的前代与后代模型，以及对应的排序决策。核心模块包括：1）跨代优势（CGA）计算模块：对跨代模型对和同代模型对进行边际匹配对比，量化分布式进展；2）秩分离统计量模块：在无标签条件下计算一个秩分离统计量，用于预测应保留哪一代模型；3）保留机制选择：根据秩分离值选择跨代配对或同代配对作为保留策略。训练目标为通过递归自我改进持续更新推荐模型。推理或选择流程：在每次更新后，计算秩分离统计量，决定保留前代模型、后代模型或两者的组合，以用于后续训练或最终预测。该架构旨在解决仅依赖最新模型可能丢失互补排序信息的问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "选择更强家族的比例", "value": "12/12 第一次更新数据集-编码器设置", "setting": "秩分离统计量预测应保留家族，基于四个数据集和三种编码器"}, {"metric": "选择更强家族的比例", "value": "5/6 第二次更新数据集-编码器设置", "setting": "秩分离统计量预测应保留家族，基于四个数据集和三种编码器"}, {"metric": "保留家族优于直接后继的轨迹比例", "value": "34/36", "setting": "留出测试集，基于四个数据集和三种编码器的轨迹"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出跨代优势（CGA）指标，通过边际匹配对比跨代与同代模型对来量化分布式进展；提出秩分离统计量作为无标签的选择时指标，用于预测应保留哪一代模型；在四个数据集和三种序列推荐编码器上验证了不同架构偏好不同的保留机制

Abstract: Recommendation recursive self-improvement (Rec-RSI) feeds recommender outputs into subsequent training. Evaluating each round solely through its latest model assumes that the successor consolidates the update, although pre- and post-update models may retain complementary ranking decisions. We term this \emph{distributed progress} and quantify it using cross-generation advantage (CGA), a marginally matched contrast between cross- and within-generation model pairs. A rank-separation statistic, label-free at selection time, predicts which family to retain. Across four datasets and three sequential recommendation encoders, the preferred retention regime varies by architecture: cross-generation pairing benefits GRU4Rec and SASRec, whereas FMLP initially favors within-generation pairing and shifts toward cross-generation pairing after a second update. Rank separation selects the stronger family in 12/12 first-update and 5/6 second-update dataset-encoder settings; on held-out tests, the selected family outperforms the direct successor in 34/36 trajectories. Five transfer mechanisms do not consistently reproduce these gains in one model. These findings establish state retention as a distinct Rec-RSI problem: progress may reside in relations between generations as well as in the latest model.
